# @referencedByName 
  - Do not rename this script! It is called externally by name via fmp url
  - Recommended: If you always call this script by name you can replace the entire script without breaking any calls to it!

# # fmIDE
# A FileMaker Integrated Development Environment

# - fmIDE extends your FileMaker development environment and integrates it in your documentation and workflows
# - fmIDE goes straight to the thing you name in the FileMaker IDE
# - fmIDE runs fmIDE actions or entire action scripts (fmIDEAS) you pass it
# - fmIDE uses fmJAML to make writing fmIDEAS in text simply a dream

# - fmIDE comprises, at its heart, a single "fmIDE" script (to make using it and updating it simple)
# - fmIDE uses lower_snake_case for (nearly) all parameter and variable names for simplicity
# - fmIDE is developed, tested and proven on Mac
#   - and maybe works (or can be made to work - at least partially) on windows
# - fmIDE builds on the MBS plugin for many of its best features

# ## About
# @author @mrwatson-de
# @license MIT
# @version - see the fmIDE_version variable at the top of this script - fmp://$/fmIDE?script=fmIDE&$script_name=fmIDE&$script_step_number=2
# @changed - see @script "###…" - fmp://$/fmIDE?script=fmIDE&$script_name_stub=%23%23%23&$script_step_number=13
# @created 2022-06-23

# ### Source
# @see for fmIDE Repo https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE
# @see for fmJAML Repo https://github.com/fmIDE/fmJAML

# ##- Read more in the fmIDE Wiki
# @see Wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki

# ## Requirements
# @needs FileMaker 19.5.1 (absolute minimum version) to function since JSONGetElementType is used
# @needs FileMaker 26 if FM-26 steps are required - the most recent FileMaker is recommended
# @needs Mac for some functions
# @needs MBS Plugin for some functions (actually, for most of the deeper-GUI functionality) - the most recent MBS plugin is recommended

# ## Installing fmIDE
# @see Installing fmIDE https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki#installing-fmide

# ## Setting up fmIDE
# You may have to customise the Scripts in the `fmIDE Callback Interface` folder

# ### Customise these scripts to be able to jump to the right Basetable:
# See @script "fmIDE.GetBaseLayoutName" - fmp://$/fmIDE?script=fmIDE&$script_name=fmIDE.GetBaseLayoutName
# See @script "fmIDE.GetBaseTableName" - fmp://$/fmIDE?script=fmIDE&$script_name=fmIDE.GetBaseTableName

# ### Customise this script if you wish to be able to run the `Open File` action
# See @script "fmIDE.OpenFile" - fmp://$/fmIDE?script=fmIDE&$script_name=fmIDE.OpenFile

# ## Download the latest fmIDE here:
# @see Latest Release https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/releases/latest

# ## Update this script
# To update the contents of this script
  
  1. First copy the steps of the new version onto the clipboard
  2. Make sure all scripts are saved (to avoid dialogs)
  3. Then click the MBS URL link in the following step
     - If anything goes wrong use undo or revert script to return to the previous state
# @action paste-update fmIDE
  - fmp://$/fmIDE?script=fmIDE&$fmide_actions=%5B%7B%22Go%20to%20Script%22%3A%7B%22script_name%22%3A%22fmIDE%22%2C%22script_step_range%22%3A%221%E2%80%A6-1%22%7D%7D%2C%7B%22Press%22%3A%7B%22key%22%3A%22%5Cb%22%7D%7D%2C%7B%22Press%22%3A%7B%22key%22%3A%22%5Cb%22%7D%7D%2C%7B%22Paste%22%3Anull%7D%5D


# # Using fmIDE

# ## Parameters

# Parameters can be passed…

# - as an fmJAML fmIDE action script - this is the de facto best method of passing an action script 
# - as a JSON object with an actions JSON array
# - as a JSON array of actions
# - as a JSON object with a single action JSON object
# - as a JSON object with parameter names
# - as a list of key=value pairs
# - as direct variables when called directly via fmpurl

# @example of a `Go to Layout` action using fmJAML

# ` [+].Go to Layout = "fmIDE Actions"

# Note: Action names in fmIDE can be specified in any way you want - independently of how the action is passed:
  
  - `Go to Layout`	(Original/natural case - recommended)
  - `go_to_layout`	(lower_snake_case)
  - `GoToLayout`	(UpperCamelCase)
  - `Go To Layout`	(Title Case)
  - `GO TO LAYOUT`	(UPPERCASE)
  
  The first style, Original case, is the recommended format, being identical to FileMaker step names
  The second style, lower_snake_case, is like all other fmIDE parameter names, so is also good
  The third style, UpperCamelCase, is how the action is identified in the script
  The final two are just capitalisation variants of the original case
  
  …but you can choose whatever suits you!

# @param {object} $fmide_action - A single JSON action object
# @param {string} $fmide__action_name - The name of a simple action

# An action can be structured as an object having the name of the action and containing (multiple) action_parameters 
# @param {…} action.«action_name».«action_parameter»

# @example of a `Go to Layout` action using the *action object* syntax:
# ` JSONSetElement( "{}" ; "action.Go to Layout.layout_name" ; "fmIDE Actions" ; JSONString )

# or
# An action can be passed as a simple list of action-parameters,
  in which the parameter `action_name` specifies the name of the action
# @param {string} action.action_name - the name of the action
# @param {…} action.«action_parameter» - an action parameter

# @example of the same `Go to Layout` action using the *action_name* syntax:
# ` JSONSetElement( "{}"
    ;[ "action.action_name" ; "Go to Layout"; JSONString]
    ;[ "action.layout_name" ; "fmIDE Actions" ; JSONString]
    )


# @example of a `Go to Layout` action using the *action object* syntax and lower_snake_case:
# ` JSONSetElement( "{}" ; "action.go_to_layout.layout_name" ; "Start Layout" ; JSONString )

# @param {array} $fmide_actions - A JSON array of action objects
# ` JSONSetElement( "[]"
    ;[ "[0].Go to Layout.layout_name" ; "fmIDE Actions" ; JSONString]
    ;[ "[1].Enter Find Mode" ; "" ; JSONNull]
    ;[ "[2].Set Field.fmIDE_Action::ActionName.text" ; "Set" ; JSONString]
    ;[ "[3].Perform Find" ; "" ; JSONNull]
    )
# Here is that action script as an fmpurl targeted at this file:
# @action Search for set actions in the fmIDE demo file
  - fmp://$/fmIDE?script=fmIDE&$fmide_actions=%5B%7B%22Go%20to%20Layout%22%3A%7B%22layout_name%22%3A%22fmIDE%20Actions%22%7D%7D%2C%7B%22Enter%20Find%20Mode%22%3Anull%7D%2C%7B%22Set%20Field%22%3A%7B%22fmIDE_Action%3A%3AActionName%22%3A%7B%22text%22%3A%22Set%22%7D%7D%7D%2C%7B%22Perform%20Find%22%3Anull%7D%5D

# actions array structure = [index].action.param:value

# Alternartively in an fmpurl call parameters can be passed directly as variables

# The following list documents all parameters that we support or // plan to support (commented out)
# Supported parameters can also be passed as variables in an fmp/claris url
# Note that this list is an active part of the script variable-checking semantics:
  - Since the MBS-Plugin recognises these as real variable definitions it does not flag up
  references to these variables later, even though the variables have not actually been previously
  defined in this script
  - This is, of course, what we want, because we know the variables may have been created by the url

# @var $fmide_actions - An array of actions
# @var $fmide_action - A single action object
# @var $fmide__action_name - The name of a simple action

# @param $account_name (string) - Name of the account
# @param $account_number (number) - Number of the account
# @param $account_id (number) - Internal ID of the account

# @param $base_table_name (string) - Name of the base_table
# @param $base_table_number (number) - Number of the base_table
# @param $base_table_id (number) - Internal ID of the base_table

# @param $custom_function_name (string) - Name of the custom_function
# @param $custom_function_number (number) - Number of the custom_function
# @param $custom_function_id (number) - Internal ID of the custom_function

# @param $custom_menu_name (string) - Name of the custom_menu
# @param $custom_menu_number (number) - Number of the custom_menu
# @param $custom_menu_id (number) - Internal ID of the custom_menu

# @param $custom_menu_item_name (string) - Name of the custom_menu_item
# @param $custom_menu_item_number (number) - Number of the custom_menu_item
# @param $custom_menu_item_id (number) - Internal ID of the custom_menu_item

# @param $custom_menu_set_name (string) - Name of the custom_menu_set
# @param $custom_menu_set_number (number) - Number of the custom_menu_set
# @param $custom_menu_set_id (number) - Internal ID of the custom_menu_set

# @param $external_data_source_name (string) - Name of the external_data_source
# @param $external_data_source_number (number) - Number of the external_data_source
# @param $external_data_source_id (number) - Internal ID of the external_data_source

# @param $extended_privilege_name (string) - Name of the extended_privilege
# @param $extended_privilege_number (number) - Number of the extended_privilege
# @param $extended_privilege_id (number) - Internal ID of the extended_privilege

# @param $field_name (string) - Name of the field
# @param $field_number (number) - Number of the field
# @param $field_id (number) - Internal ID of the field

# @param $layout_name (string) - Name of the layout
# @param $layout_number (number) - Number of the layout
# @param $layout_id (number) - Internal ID of the layout

# @param $layout_field_name (string) - Name of the layout field
# @param $layout_field_number (number) - Number of the layout field
# @param $layout_field_id (number) - Internal ID of the layout field

# @param $layout_part_name (string) - Name of the layout part
# @param $layout_part_number (number) - Number of the layout part
# @param $layout_part_id (number) - Internal ID of the layout part

# @param $object_name (string) - Name of the object
# @param $object_number (number) - Number of the object
# @param $object_id (number) - Internal ID of the object

# @param $privilege_set_name (string) - Name of the privilege_set
# @param $privilege_set_number (number) - Number of the privilege_set
# @param $privilege_set_id (number) - Internal ID of the privilege_set

# @param $script_name (string) - Name of the script
# @param $script_name_stub (string) - Stub-name of the script
# @param $script_number (number) - Number of the script
# @param $script_id (number) - Internal ID of the script

# @param $script_step_name (string) - Name of the script_step
# @param $script_step_number (number) - Number of the script_step
# @param $script_step_number_to (number) - Number of the script_step to select up to
# @param $script_step_id (number) - Internal ID of the script_step

# @param $table_name (string) - Name of the table
# @param $table_number (number) - Number of the table
# @param $table_id (number) - Internal ID of the table

# @param $t_o_name (string) - Name of the TO (table occurence)
# @param $t_o_number (number) - Number of the TO (table occurence)
# @param $t_o_id (number) - Internal ID of the TO (table occurence)

# @param $theme_name (string) - Name of the theme
# @param $theme_number (number) - Number of the theme
# @param $theme_id (number) - Internal ID of the theme

# @param $value_list_name (string) - Name of the value_list
# @param $value_list_number (number) - Number of the value_list
# @param $value_list_id (number) - Internal ID of the value_list

# @param $window_name (string) - Name of the window

# @param $fmide_debug (number) - true = pause / show debugging info

# @param $*_uuid // uuid-based targets are not yet supported internally


# Private parameter
# @param $fmide__continuation{} - A continuation object, to continue the action script, where fmIDE left off before HALTing to do some GUI-actions
# When fmIDE must halt to undertake some GUI-actions, but wishes to continue with the script thereafter it uses a 'continuation'
# fmIDE re-schedules itself to restart after a moment and passes the continuation object back to itself to reestablish execution exactly where it left off
# Neat!

If [ Get ( AccountPrivilegeSetName ) ≠ "[Full Access]" ]
	Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 9 ]
	Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_detail[1] ; "The current account does not have sufficient privileges to use fmIDE." ]
	Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_location[1] ; 0 ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__error_thrown[1] ; 1 ]
	Exit Script []
	# FIXME …or…
	Set Variable [ $fmide__script_result[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
					;["fmide_get_last_error" ; $$fmide_get_last_error ; JSONNumber]
					;["fmide_get_last_error_detail" ; $$fmide_get_last_error_detail ; JSONString]
					) ]
	Exit Script [ $fmide__script_result ]
End If

# # Initialise error + abort handling

# - errors in this script are caught - and handled explicitly by fmIDE FIXME - is that OK?
# - users are allowed to abort
Set Error Capture [ ON ]

# (?)
Set Variable [ $fmide_dialog_title[1] ; Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide_dialog_title ) ; $fmide_dialog_title ; Get ( FileName ) & "::fmIDE" ) ]

# # Private Vars and Script settings

Set Variable [ $fmide__is_win[1] ; Get ( Device ) = 2 ]

				"RemoteControl.PressCommandKey";
				"RemoteControl.PressControlKey" 
				) ]
Set Variable [ $fmide__fm_version_major[1] ; +GetValue ( Substitute ( Get ( ApplicationVersion ) ; "." ; ¶ ) ; 1 ) ]
Set Variable [ $fmide__current_window_uuid[1] ; Let(
				[
				¢window_id = Evaluate ( "Get ( WindowUUID )" ) ;
				¢window_id = Case( ¢window_id ≠ "?" ; ¢window_id ; MBS( "Window.Current") )
				];
				¢window_id
				) ]

# Script Parameter    | Input                          | _JSP_Type   | How is this processed
  --------------------|--------------------------------|-------------|----------------------------------------------
  ""                  | None                           | JSONNull    | No parameter, mostly with call by fmpurl. (Repacked as empty object {})
  "" (+magic $vars)   | Name-that-Thing fmp-url-vars   | JSONNull    | Vars created by fmp url are later processed into an actions array
  "fmIDESS-*¶«param»" | fmIDE-Subscript call           | fmIDESS     | The named fmIDE-Subscript (*) is run with the remaining «param» 
  "---¶…"             | fmJAML with frontmatter        | -           | The fronmatter is extracted and the remaining text is processed below…
  "$var=…"            | Name-that-Thing variable-defs  | "Let"       | Vars are created by evaluation and later processed into an actions array
  "{…}"               | Object of actions/one action   | JSONObject  | Used as is or, if invalid JSON, repacked as {"malformed_parameter": …}
  "[…]"               | Action array                   | JSONArray   | Action array is wrapped in an {"actions": … } object
  "[0]…"              | Action array definition        | "fmJAML"    | fmJAML: Action array is created and wrapped in an {"actions": … } object
  "[+]…"              | Action array def. (only fm21+) | "fmJAML"    | fmJAML: Action array is created and wrapped in an {"actions": … } object
  otherwise           | Other text                     | "Text"      | Processed into an actions array [+]=Line1…

# ############
# # Parameters
# ############
Set Variable [ $fmide__sp[1] ; Get ( ScriptParameter ) ]


# =====================
# ## Handle Frontmatter
# =====================

Set Variable [ $fmide__pos_frontmatter_end[1] ; Case( Left ( $fmide__sp ; 4 ) = "---¶" ; Position ( $fmide__sp ; "¶---" ; 1 ; 1 ) ) ]
Set Variable [ $fmide__pos_frontmatter_end[1] ; Case( Left ( $fmide__sp ; 4 ) = "---¶" ; Position ( $fmide__sp ; "¶---" ; 1 ; 1 ) ) ]
If [ $fmide__pos_frontmatter_end ]
	# front-matter / $vars
	# Extract front matter and define vars
	Set Variable [ $fmide__frontmatter[1] ; Middle( $fmide__sp ; 5 ; $fmide__pos_frontmatter_end - 5 ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__r[1] ; Let(
					[
					
					¢frontMatter           = $fmide__frontmatter ;
					¢frontMatterNoWs       = Substitute ( ¢frontMatter ; [" ";""] ; [Char(9);""] ; [Char(10);""] ; [¶;""] ) ;
					¢defineFrontMatterVars = "Let([" & ¢frontMatter & "¶" & Case ( Right ( ¢frontMatterNoWs ; 1 ) ≠ ";" ; ";" ) & "r=0];0)"
					
					];
					
					Case( not IsEmpty ( ¢frontMatter ) ; Evaluate ( ¢defineFrontMatterVars ) )
					
					) ]
	
	# Continue without frontmatter
	Set Variable [ $fmide__sp[1] ; Right ( $fmide__sp ; Length ( $fmide__sp )+1 - $fmide__pos_frontmatter_end - 5 ) ]
End If

# For easier control make debugging options visible (& thus editable) in the data viewer
Set Variable [ $fmide_debugger[1] ; not not $fmide_debugger ]
Set Variable [ $fmide_debug[1] ; 0+$fmide_debug ]
Set Variable [ $fmide_pause_on_error[1] ; GetAsBoolean ( $fmide_pause_on_error ) ]
Set Variable [ $fmide_pause_on_condition[1] ; GetAsBoolean ( $fmide_pause_on_condition ) ]
Set Variable [ $fmide_pause_on_condition_calculation[1] ; Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide_pause_on_condition_calculation ) ; $fmide_pause_on_condition_calculation ; 0 ) ]


# ## Construct a JSON Script Parameter (_JSP) from the actual script parameter

# What kind of parameter?
Set Variable [ $fmide__jsp_type[1] ; Let([
				¢SP = $fmide__sp ;
				¢Char1 = Left ( ¢SP ; 1 ) ;
				¢Chars3 = Left ( ¢SP ; 3 ) ;
				¢Chars8 = Left ( ¢SP ; 8 ) ;
				¢CharN = Right ( ¢SP ; 1 ) ;
				¢Char1N = ¢Char1 & ¢CharN
				
				];
				
				Case (
				
				¢Chars8 = "fmIDESS-" ; "fmIDESS" ; /* fmIDE-Subscript call */
				¢Char1  = "#"   ; "fmJAML" ;
				//¢Chars8 = "#!fmjaml" ; "fmJAML" ; /* Shebang for fmJAML */
				¢Chars3 = "[+]" ; "fmJAML" ; /* [+] native in FM21, interpreted in earlier */
				¢Chars3 = "[0]" ; "fmJAML" ;
				¢Chars3 = "---" ; "YAML" ;
				¢Char1N = "{}" ; JSONObject ;
				¢Char1N = "[]" ; JSONArray ;
				¢Char1N = ""   ; JSONNull ;
				¢Char1  = "$"   ; "Let" ;
				"Text"
				)
				
				) ]


# ======================================
# ## 1. fmIDESS = fmIDE Subscript System
# ======================================


If [ $fmide__jsp_type = "fmIDESS" ]
	# The fmIDE Subscript System allows fmIDE to call subscripts within itself
	# Allowing fmIDE to basically comprise one script, making deployment a piece of cake 🍰
	
	# Parameter format is
	  
	  ---¶
	  «optional_frontmatter»¶
	  ---¶
	  fmIDESS-«script_name»¶
	  «script_parameter»
	
	# -------------------------------
	# ### Read the fmIDESS-parameters
	# -------------------------------
	Set Variable [ $fmidess__line1[1] ; GetValue ( $fmide__sp ; 1 ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmidess__script_name[1] ; Right ( $fmidess__line1 ; Length ( $fmidess__line1 ) - 8 ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmidess__script_parameter[1] ; Right ( $fmide__sp ; Length ( $fmide__sp ) - Length ( $fmidess__line1 ) - 1 ) ]
	
	# SWITCH fmIDE Subscript
	Set Variable [ $fmidess__script_name[1] ; Trim ( $fmidess__script_name ) ]
	If [ $fmidess__script_name = "" ]
		# none
		
	Else If [ $fmidess__script_name = "ExitScript" ]
		# ################################
		# ### fmIDE-Subscript "ExitScript"
		# ################################
		
		# Sets the script result to the given parameter
		
		Exit Script [ $fmidess__script_parameter ]
		
	Else If [ $fmidess__script_name = "OnTimerScriptProxy" ]
		# ########################################
		# ### fmIDE-Subscript "OnTimerScriptProxy"
		# ########################################
		
		# Calls the target OnTimerScript and passes the script parametereter
		
		# The target script_name is stored in a 'window variable', which is just a repeating global variable indexed using the current window's uuid.
		# There is a native function for Window UUIDs in fm26, before that the MBS plugin is used.
		
		# @parameter $fmidess__script_parameter = Parameter for the OnTimer Script
		
		# @return *none*
		
		# - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
		# #### Read the window variable
		Set Variable [ $fmide___target_script_name[1] ; $$fmide__ontimer_script_name[$fmide__current_window_uuid] ]
		
		# - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
		# #### Call the target OnTimer Script
		Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; $fmide___target_script_name ; Parameter: $fmidess__script_parameter ]
		
		# Work of the proxy is finished
		Exit Script []
		
	Else
		# Error - unknown fmIDE Subscript
		Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 101 ]
		Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_detail[1] ; "fmIDE: unknown subscript " & $fmidess__script_name ]
		Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_location[1] ; "" ]
	End If
	
	# All fmIDE-Subscripts MUST EXIT HERE
	Exit Script []
	
	# ========
	# /fmIDESS
	# ========
End If

If [ $fmide__jsp_type = "fmJAML" ]
	# ==================================
	# ## 2. Handle fmJAML parameter type
	# ==================================
	#    __                _         __  __ _
	#   / _|              | |  /\   |  \/  | |
	#  | |_ _ __ ___      | | /  \  | \  / | |
	#  |  _| '_ ` _ \ _   | |/ /\ \ | |\/| | |
	#  | | | | | | | | |__| / ____ \| |  | | |____
	#  |_| |_| |_| |_|\____/_/    \_\_|  |_|______|
	
	# fmIDE ships as a one script process without custom function dependencies.
	  
	  If you want to use the fmJAML custom function for quick testing:
	  -> set $fmide_fmjaml_per_cf to true.
	If [ not $fmide_fmjaml_per_cf ]
		# ------------------------------
		# ### fmJAML - EMBEDDED function
		# ------------------------------
		# Please keep the embedded fmJAML function consistent with the CF!
		  -> copy & paste the CF code between the cut marks!
		# ` vvvvvvvvvvv
		Set Variable [ $fmide__fmjaml[1] ; Let([ pOptionalInitialJSON = "" ; pFmJAML = $fmide__sp ];
						/*---------------- ✂︎ ----------------*/
						/* fmJAML ( pOptionalInitialJSON ; pFmJAML )
						   __                _         __  __ _      
						  / _|              | |  /\   |  \/  | |     
						 | |_ _ __ ___      | | /  \  | \  / | |     
						 |  _| '_ ` _ \ _   | |/ /\ \ | |\/| | |     
						 | | | | | | | | |__| / ____ \| |  | | |____ 
						 |_| |_| |_| |_|\____/_/    \_\_|  |_|______|
						
						
						`code from your keyboard`
						
						**fmJAML** = **F**ile**M**aker **J**SON **A**s a **M**arkdown **L**angauge
						
						…a Domain Specific Langauage (DSL) for encoding JSON as a simple, concise, readable string in FileMaker.
						
						The fmJAML function takes an optional initial JSON and an fmJAML text as input 
						and constructs the equivalent JSON, applying relevant processing logic to data type, value and path.
						
						fmJAML, originally created to write fmIDE Action Scripts,
						aims to be a generalised templating system for creating most any JSON.
						
						@author: @mrwatson-de
						@created: 2026-03-10
						@version: See ¢release
						@history: See fmJAML_changes
						
						@param pOptionalInitialJSON (string) - a JSON that the fmJAML code builds upon or an empty string
						@param pFmJAML (string) - the fmJAML code to express your JSON in FileMaker
						@returns json (string) - the JSON constructed from the fmJAML string
						@input $fmjaml_debug - fmLog357 log level to use: 0/""=none, 3=errors, 5=info, 7=debug (See fmLog357 for more).
						@output $$fmjaml_log (string) - debugging log
						@output $$fmjaml_debug_dtype (string) - just the data types
						
						Note: Please do not use the reserved prefix `($)$fmjaml__` in your variables.
						*/
						
						/*******************
						Process fmJAML lines
						*******************/
						
						While (
						[
						¢release = "2026-09-29 @mrwatson-de v0.36";
						
						/* input param */
						$fmjaml__json = pOptionalInitialJSON ;
						¢lines = pFmJAML ;
						
						/* input vars */
						¢debug = Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmjaml_debug ) ; $fmjaml_debug ; Get ( ActiveModifierKeys ) ) ;
						/* FIXME - Using the modifier keys for log level is a neat trick for quick debugging but a bit of a fudge. */
						
						/* internal vars */
						$fmjaml__path_prev = "" ;
						¢mbs = Evaluate ( "MBS(\"Version\")" ) ≠ "?" ;
						¢fmp_v_maj = 0 + GetValue ( Substitute ( Get ( ApplicationVersion ) ; "." ; ¶ ) ; 1 ) ;
						
						/* output vars */
						$$fmjaml_debug_dtype = "" ;
						$$fmjaml_log = Case( ¢debug ≥ 6 ; "¢debug: " & ¢debug & ¶ & "¢mbs: " & ¢mbs & ¶ ) ;
						
						/* Constants */
						¢TAB = Char(9) ;
						¢WS  = " " & ¢TAB ;
						¢JSONDate      = 20 ;
						¢JSONTime      = 21 ;
						¢JSONTimestamp = 22 ;
						¢JSONContainer = 23 ;
						
						/* FOR EACH ¢line */
						
						¢line_i = 1;
						¢line_n = ValueCount ( ¢lines )
						
						]; ¢line_i <= ¢line_n and Left( $fmjaml__json ; 1 ) ≠ "?" ;[
						
						¢line  = GetValue ( ¢lines ; ¢line_i );
						/* trim horizontally */
						¢line  = Case( not Position( ¢line ; ¢TAB ; 1 ; 1 ) ; Trim( ¢line ) ; Let ([
						  ¢tmp = Substitute( ¢line ; [" " ; ""] ; [¢TAB ; ""] ) ; 
						  ¢ch1 = Left( ¢tmp ; 1 ) ; ¢chN = Right( ¢tmp ; 1 ) ; ¢pos = Position( ¢line ; ¢ch1 ; 1 ; 1 )
						  ];
						  Case( ¢pos ; Middle ( ¢line ; ¢pos ; Position( ¢line ; ¢chN ; Length ( ¢line ) ; -1 ) - ¢pos + 1 ) )
						  ));
						
						/* Split line into «path»[=[;«DPS»;][«expr»]]] */
						
						/* ignore comment lines */
						¢line  = Case( Left ( ¢line ; 1 ) ≠ "#" ; ¢line ) ;
						¢isLineEmpty = IsEmpty( ¢line ) ;
						
						/* split line at `=` and trim path horizontally right*/
						¢posEquals = Position( ¢line ; "=" ; 1 ; 1 ) ;
						$fmjaml__path = Case( not ¢posEquals ; ¢line ; While(
						  [
						
						  ¢len = ¢posEquals-1
						
						  ]; Position ( ¢WS ; Middle( ¢line ; ¢len ; 1 ) ; 1 ; 1 ) ;[
						
						  ¢len = ¢len - 1
						
						  ];
						
						  Left ( ¢line ; ¢len )
						
						  ));
						
						¢lineRest = Case( ¢posEquals ; Trim( Right( ¢line ; Length( ¢line ) - ¢posEquals ) ) ) ;
						
						/* DPS */
						¢posDps = Position ( ¢lineRest ; ";" ; 1 ; 1 ) ;
						¢posDps = ¢posDps * IsEmpty( Substitute ( Left( ¢lineRest ; ¢posDps-1 ) ; [" " ; ""];[Char(9) ; ""] ) ) ;
						¢posDpsEnd = Case( ¢posDps ; Position ( ¢lineRest ; ";" ; ¢posDps+1 ; 1 ) ) ;
						¢dps = Case ( ¢posDpsEnd  ; Trim( Middle ( ¢lineRest ; 2 ; ¢posDpsEnd - 2 ) ) ) ;
						
						/* expr inc. value_op */
						¢expr  = Trim ( Right ( ¢lineRest ; Length ( ¢lineRest ) - ¢posDpsEnd ) ) ;
						¢value_op = Case( 
						Left ( ¢expr ; 1 ) ≠ "=" ; "" ; 
						Left ( ¢expr ; 5 ) = "=====" ;  2 ; /* pos -> read block, 2*Quote() */
						Left ( ¢expr ; 4 ) = "====" ; -2 ; /* neg -> read line,  2*Quote() */
						Left ( ¢expr ; 3 ) = "===" ;  1 ; /* pos -> read block, 1*Quote() */
						Left ( ¢expr ; 2 ) = "==" ; -1 ; /* neg -> read line,  1*Quote() */
						0 /* pos -> read block, 0*Quote() */
						);
						$fmjaml__inc = 0 ;
						
						/* DEBUG */
						If=Case ( ¢debug ≥ 5 ; Let([
						  $$fmjaml_log = List( $$fmjaml_log ;
						  "line["&¢line_i&"] <- " & ¢line ;
						  "- lineRest " & ¢lineRest ;
						  Case( ¢debug ≥ 6 ; List ( 
						    "- path:  " & Quote( $fmjaml__path ) ;
						    Case ( not IsEmpty( ¢dps ) ; "- dps:  " & Quote( ¢dps ) );
						    Case ( ¢posEquals ; "- value:  " & ¢expr ) ;
						    ))
						  ) ;
						EndIf=0];0));
						
						/* Process path array constructor */
						$fmjaml__dtype = Case( Length( $fmjaml__path ) > 2 and Right( $fmjaml__path ; 2 ) = "[]" and
						     not PatternCount( ¢TAB & " .[]" ; Middle ( $fmjaml__path ; Length( $fmjaml__path ) - 2 ; 1 ) ) ;
						  Let( $fmjaml__path = Left ( $fmjaml__path ; Length( $fmjaml__path ) - 2 ) ;
						  JSONArray )) ;
						
						/**********
						EXPAND PATH
						***********
						
						Support for avoiding key-repetition by inheriting empty keys from the previous line (ignoring white space):
						
						For example:
						
						Execute FileMaker Data API.target = "$result"
						.request.layouts = "fmIDE Tests Light"
						.       .limit = 1
						
						becomes
						
						Execute FileMaker Data API.target = "$result"
						Execute FileMaker Data API.request.layouts = "fmIDE Tests Light"
						Execute FileMaker Data API.request.limit = 1
						
						Method:
						
						1. ¢path_nows removes the white space
						2. ¢path_dots also converts all delimiters to dots to simplify adjacent dot recognition.
						3. Missing keys are inherited directly into $fmjaml_path (requiring ¢pos_path to be adjusted)
						4. [+]/[:] is resolved in the resulting path before being used for the next line
						
						Example @ line 3 above:
						
						prev = `Execute FileMaker Data API.request.layouts`
						path = `.       .limit`
						nows = `..limit`
						dots = `..limit`
						*/
						
						If = Case( not ¢isLineEmpty ; While ([
						
						  ¢path_nows = Substitute ( $fmjaml__path ; [" " ; ""] ; [¢TAB ; ""] ) ;
						  ¢path_dots = Substitute ( ¢path_nows ; ["[" ; "."] ; ["]" ; "."] ) ;
						
						  ¢pos_path = 0 ;
						  ¢pos_prev = 0 ;
						  ¢pos_dot = 0 ;
						  
						  ¢pos_dot_next  = Position ( ¢path_dots ; "." ; ¢pos_dot + 1 ; 1 )
						  
						  ]; ¢pos_dot_next ; [
						  
						  /*
						
						  1. Locate the next delimiter:
						         v-¢pos_dot
						  dots = "..limit"
						          ^-¢pos_dot_next
						
						  2. Identify delimiter:
						          v-¢delim -> '.'
						  nows = "..limit"
						
						  3. Locate delimiter in path:
						         v-¢pos_path
						  path = ".       .limit"
						          ^-¢pos_path_next
						
						  4. Locate delimiter in prev-path:
						         v-¢pos_prev
						  prev = "Execute FileMaker Data API.request.layouts"
						                                    ^-¢pos_prev_next
						  key  = "**************************"
						
						5. Inherit key from previous path only if dots adjacent
						
						  */
						  ¢delim         = Middle ( ¢path_nows ; ¢pos_dot_next ; 1 ) ;
						
						  ¢pos_path_next = Position ( $fmjaml__path      ; ¢delim ; ¢pos_path + 1 ; 1 ) ;
						  ¢pos_prev_next = Position ( $fmjaml__path_prev ; ¢delim ; ¢pos_prev + 1 ; 1 ) ;
						  ¢pos_prev_next = Case ( ¢pos_prev_next ; ¢pos_prev_next ; Length ( $fmjaml__path_prev ) + 1 ) ;
						  
						  ¢inherit = ¢pos_dot_next = ¢pos_dot + 1 ;
						  ¢key_prev = Case ( ¢inherit ; /* Get previous key */
						    Middle ( $fmjaml__path_prev ; ¢pos_prev + 1 ; ¢pos_prev_next - ¢pos_prev - 1 )
						  ) ;
						  $fmjaml__path = Case ( not ¢inherit ; $fmjaml__path ; /* Replace empty key */
						    Replace( $fmjaml__path      ; ¢pos_path + 1 ; ¢pos_path_next - ¢pos_path - 1 ; ¢key_prev )
						  ) ;
						  debug=Case ( ¢debug ≥ 7 and ¢inherit ; Let( $$fmjaml_log = List( $$fmjaml_log ;
						  "¶Path Inheritance: " ;
						  ¢line_i-1 & ":	" & Replace( Replace( $fmjaml__path_prev ; ¢pos_prev_next ; 1 ; "}" ) ; ¢pos_prev ; 1 ; "{" ) ;
						  ¢line_i   & ":	" & Replace( Replace( $fmjaml__path      ; ¢pos_path_next ; 1 ; "}" ) ; ¢pos_path ; 1 ; "{" ) ;
						  "key:	" & ¢key_prev ;
						  "delim:	" & ¢delim ;
						  "delims:	" & Replace( ¢path_nows ; ¢pos_dot_next ; 1 ; "•" )
						  ) ; "")) ;
						
						  /* 6. Make the next dot the current dot in dots + prev + path */
						  ¢pos_dot  = ¢pos_dot_next ;
						  ¢pos_prev = ¢pos_prev_next ;
						  ¢pos_path = Case( ¢inherit ; ¢pos_path + Length ( ¢key_prev ) + 1 ;  ¢pos_path_next ) ;
						
						  /* 7. Continue with the next dot */
						  ¢pos_dot_next  = Position ( ¢path_dots ; "." ; ¢pos_dot + 1 ; 1 ) ;
						
						EndIf=0];0)) ;
						
						
						/**************
						Support for [+] and [:] in FileMaker versions < 21
						***************
						*/
						
						$fmjaml__path = Case( ¢fmp_v_maj ≥ 21 ; $fmjaml__path ; While ([
						
						  ¢resolved_path = $fmjaml__path ;
						  ¢pos_plus  = Position ( ¢resolved_path ; "[+]" ; 1 ; 1 ) ;
						  ¢pos_colon = Position ( ¢resolved_path ; "[:]" ; 1 ; 1 ) 
						
						  ] ; ¢pos_plus or ¢pos_colon ; [
						
						  ¢pos = Case ( ¢pos_plus and ¢pos_colon ; Min ( ¢pos_plus ; ¢pos_colon ) ; ¢pos_plus + ¢pos_colon ) ;
						  ¢array_length  = ValueCount ( JSONListKeys ( $fmjaml__json ; Left ( ¢resolved_path ; ¢pos - 1 ) ) ) ;
						  ¢index = ¢array_length - ( ¢pos = ¢pos_colon ) ;
						  ¢resolved_path = Replace ( ¢resolved_path ; ¢pos + 1 ; 1 ; Max( 0 ; ¢index ) ) ;
						  ¢pos_plus  = Position ( ¢resolved_path ; "[+]" ; ¢pos+3 ; 1 ) ;
						  ¢pos_colon = Position ( ¢resolved_path ; "[:]" ; ¢pos+3 ; 1 )
						
						  ];
						
						  ¢resolved_path
						
						  )) ;
						
						$fmjaml__path_prev = Case( ¢isLineEmpty ; $fmjaml__path_prev ; $fmjaml__path ) ;  
						
						/******
						heredoc
						*******
						
						Process value or heredoc
						*/
						
						$fmjaml__val = Case (
						    ¢isLineEmpty ; "" ;
						
						    /* no expression -> empty object */
						    IsEmpty ( ¢expr ) ; "{}" ; Let(
						      [
						      /* Read value */
						      ¢value = Case (
						        IsEmpty( ¢value_op ) ; ¢expr ; 
						        ¢value_op < 0 ; /* hereline */ Trim ( Replace( ¢expr ; 1 ; -¢value_op*2 ; "" ) ) ;
						        /* 0 -> herecalc, >0 -> heredoc */ Let(
						          [ 
						          ¢marker    = Replace ( "==" & ¢expr ; 1 ; ¢value_op*2 ; "" ) ;/*
						"=…" → 0 → +== → "===…" 
						"===…" → 1 → +==-== → "===…" 
						"=====…" → 2 → +==-==== → "===…" 
						*/
						          ¢pos_start = Length( LeftValues ( ¢lines ; ¢line_i ) )+1 ;
						          ¢pos_end   = Position ( ¢lines ; ¢marker ; ¢pos_start ; 1 ) ;
						          ¢heredoc   = Middle( ¢lines ; ¢pos_start ; ¢pos_end - ¢pos_start /* and remove last EOL: */ -1 ) ;
						          $fmjaml__inc = ValueCount( ¢heredoc )+1
						          ];
						          ¢heredoc
						          )
						        )
						
						      ];
						
						      /* Return value, quoting depending on the operator */
						      Choose( Abs ( ¢value_op ) ;
						        Evaluate ( ¢value ) ;
						        ¢value ;
						        Quote( ¢value )
						      )
						      
						      )    
						    );
						
						
						/*
						Calculate the line-increment value based on the number of lines consumed by the heredoc.
						This is required for the next section where the DPS needs to drop the key if an empty condition (=EOF) is reached
						*/
						¢line_inc     = 1 + $fmjaml__inc ;
						
						
						/******
						;«DPS»;
						*******
						
						Process ;«DPS»;
						*/
						
						$fmjaml__sticky_op = "" ;
						$fmjaml__context = "" ;
						$fmjaml__clause_empty = "" ;
						$fmjaml__present = "" ;
						$fmjaml__test = "" ;
						$fmjaml__skip = "" ;
						¢param = FilterValues( Trim ( Substitute ( ¢dps ; "==" ; "") ) ; "release¶release_tag¶version" ) ;
						¢r = Case( not IsEmpty ( ¢param ) ; Let([ 
						  ¢r = Substitute( RightWords ( ¢release ; Case( ¢param = "release¶" ; 99 ; 1 ) ) ; "v" ; Case( ¢param = "release_tag¶" ; "v" ) ) ;
						  $fmjaml__val = Case ( Position ( ¢dps ; "==" ; 1 ; 1 ) ; Quote ( ¢r )  ; ¢r ) ;
						  $fmjaml__dtype = JSONString
						  ];0) ;
						  not IsEmpty ( ¢dps ) ; While (
						  [
						
						  /*************
						  FOR EACH ¢char
						  **************
						
						  in the ¢dps, at least until dropped…
						  */
						
						  ¢dpsPos = 1 ;
						  ¢dpsLength = Length( ¢dps )
						
						  ]; ¢dpsPos <= ¢dpsLength+1 and not IsEmpty ( $fmjaml__path );[
						
						  ¢char = Middle ( ¢dps ; ¢dpsPos ; 1 ) ;
						  $fmjaml__chars_read = 1 ;
						
						  ¢eof = ¢dpsPos > ¢dpsLength ;
						  ¢eoClause = ¢eof or Position ( "?-:," ; ¢char ; 1 ; 1 ) ;
						
						  /* DEBUG fmJAML */
						  If=Case ( ¢debug ≥ 7 ; Let([
						      $$fmjaml_log = List( $$fmjaml_log ;
						      "DPS[" & ¢dpsPos & "]: "  & Quote ( ¢char ) & Case( ¢eof ; " («EOF»)" );
						      "- $fmjaml__dtype: "        & $fmjaml__dtype ;
						      "- $fmjaml__present: "      & Quote ( $fmjaml__present ) ;
						      "- $fmjaml__test: "         & Quote ( $fmjaml__test ) ;
						      "- $fmjaml__skip: "         & Quote ( $fmjaml__skip ) ;
						      "- $fmjaml__clause_empty: " & Quote ( $fmjaml__clause_empty ) ;
						      "- $fmjaml__sticky_op: "    & Quote ( $fmjaml__sticky_op ) ;
						      "- $fmjaml__context: "      & Quote ( $fmjaml__context ) ;
						      "- $fmjaml__path: "         & Quote ( $fmjaml__path ) ;
						      "- $fmjaml__val: "          & Quote ( $fmjaml__val ) ;
						      ) ;
						  EndIf=0];0)) ;
						
						  /*****************
						  Lazy data coercion
						  ******************
						
						  Convert the value to the target data-type, but first when a processing operator (`!`/`?`/`-`) is encountered
						TNBAOUJDIMR
						  */
						  ¢notIE = not IsEmpty ( $fmjaml__val ) ;
						  ¢r = Case(
						    ¢eof or IsEmpty ( $fmjaml__dtype ) ; "" ;
						    Position ( "!?-" ; ¢char ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; "" ;
						    Let( $fmjaml__val = Case( 
						    $fmjaml__dtype = JSONString     ; GetAsText      ( $fmjaml__val ) ;
						    $fmjaml__dtype = JSONNumber     ; GetAsNumber    ( $fmjaml__val ) ;
						    $fmjaml__dtype = JSONObject     ; Case( ¢notIE ; JSONGetElement ( $fmjaml__val ; "" ) ) ;
						    $fmjaml__dtype = JSONArray      ; Case( ¢notIE ; JSONGetElement ( $fmjaml__val ; "" ) ) ;
						    $fmjaml__dtype = JSONBoolean    ; GetAsBoolean   ( $fmjaml__val ) ;
						    $fmjaml__dtype = JSONNull       ; ""                              ;
						    $fmjaml__dtype = JSONRaw        ; Case( ¢notIE ; JSONGetElement ( $fmjaml__val ; "" ) ) ;
						    $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONDate      ; GetAsDate      ( $fmjaml__val ) ;
						    $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONTime      ; GetAsTime      ( $fmjaml__val ) ;
						    $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONTimestamp ; GetAsTimeStamp ( $fmjaml__val ) ;
						    /* no coercion to container */
						    $fmjaml__val
						    ) ; "" )
						  );
						
						  /*************
						  Parse operator
						  **************
						
						  Switch statement to process the encountered operator
						  */
						  ¢parseNextOp = Case(
						
						    /* SWITCH ( ¢char ) */
						
						
						    /* Lazily drop path, if necessary, which is when… */
						    ¢eoClause and $fmjaml__clause_empty and /* …completed, empty (or missing) clause and… */ 
						    Case( $fmjaml__present ;
						      /* …value present and … explicitly empty presence clause: */ 
						      $fmjaml__context = "?" and ¢char = ":"    /* value + "?💢:" -> */
						      or
						      $fmjaml__context = "-:" ;                 /* value + "-:💢" -> */
						
						      /* …value empty and… */
						      $fmjaml__context = "?" and ¢char ≠ ":"    /* empty + "?💢" (implicit) -> */
						      or
						      $fmjaml__context = "?:"                   /* empty + "?:💢" (explicit) -> */
						    )
						    ; Let( $fmjaml__path = "" ; "" ) ;          /* -> drop value + path! */ 
						
						    /* Ignore EOF or white space */
						
						    ¢eof or
						    ¢char = " " or
						    ¢char = ¢TAB ; "" ;
						
						    /* Process end-skip chars (`:` / `,`) *before* processing any skipping */
						
						    ¢char = "," ; Let ([/* End conditional scope */
						                        $fmjaml__present = "" ;
						                        $fmjaml__test = "" ;
						                        $fmjaml__skip = "" ;
						                        $fmjaml__clause_empty = "" ;
						                        $fmjaml__context = "" ;
						                        $fmjaml__sticky_op = ""
						                       ]; "" ) ;
						    ¢char = ":" ; Let ([/* otherwise clause */
						                        $fmjaml__context = Case(
						                          $fmjaml__context = "?" ; "?:" ;
						                          $fmjaml__context = "-" ; "-:" ;
						                          $fmjaml__context
						                          ) ;
						                        $fmjaml__skip = $fmjaml__test ;
						                        $fmjaml__clause_empty  = 1 ;
						                        $fmjaml__sticky_op = ""
						                       ]  ; "" ) ;
						
						    /*****************
						    'value' or ~regex~
						    ******************
						
						    Read the entire apostrophe-string, then either 
						    - if skipping             -> ignore it, otherwise (mark the current clause as not empty and…)
						    - if not-value (`!'…'`)   -> remove the value if present or
						    - if EOL op (`¶'…'`)      -> replace EOLs with value
						    - if divide op (`/`)      -> divide value
						    - if replace op (`>`)     -> (regex-)replace value
						    - if normal value (`'…'`) -> set the value to the string.
						    */
						    ¢char = "`" or
						    ¢char = "'" or
						    ¢mbs and /* if mbs is not present just ignore the regex op -> unknown char error below */
						    ¢char = "~" ; Let([¢isRegex = ¢char = "~" ;
						                       $fmjaml__string_char = ¢char;
						                       ¢dpsValueEnd = Position ( ¢dps & ¢char ; ¢char ; ¢dpsPos+1 ; 1 ) ;
						                       $fmjaml__chars_read = ¢dpsValueEnd+1 - ¢dpsPos ;
						
						                       /* Interpret a pilcrow `\¶` as an EOL `¶`  */
						                       ¢dpsValue = Substitute( Middle( ¢dps ; ¢dpsPos+1 ; $fmjaml__chars_read - 2 ) ; "\¶" ; ¶ ) ;
						                       ¢dpsValue = Case( ¢char = "`" ; Evaluate ( ¢dpsValue ) ; ¢dpsValue ) ;
						
						                       ¢dpsGTPos = Position ( ¢dps ; ">" ; ¢dpsValueEnd+1 ; 1 ) ;
						                       ¢dpsReplaceOp = ¢dpsGTPos and (¢dpsGTPos = ¢dpsValueEnd+1 or  IsEmpty ( Substitute ( Middle ( ¢dps ; ¢dpsValueEnd+1 ; ¢dpsGTPos - ¢dpsValueEnd - 1 ) ; [" ";""] ; [Char(9);""] ) ) ) ;
						
						                       If=Case ( ¢debug ≥ 8 ; Let([
						                           $$fmjaml_log = List ( $$fmjaml_log ;
						                           "¢char=" & ¢char ;
						                           "$fmjaml__sticky_op: " & $fmjaml__sticky_op ;
						                           "$fmjaml__skip: " & $fmjaml__skip ;
						                           "¢dpsGTPos: " & ¢dpsGTPos ;
						                           "¢dpsReplaceOp: " & ¢dpsReplaceOp ;
						                           ) ;
						                       EndIf=0];"")) ;
						
						                       /* Ignore the value if skipping…  */
						                        If=Case ( not $fmjaml__skip ; Let([
						
						                            /* …else mark clause as not empty and process the value… */
						                            $fmjaml__clause_empty = "" ;
						                            $fmjaml__val = Case(
						
						                              /* Not op `!…`*/
						                              $fmjaml__sticky_op = "!" ; Case ( Case ( ¢isRegex ;
						                                  /* If regex match… */
						                                  Let([$fmjaml__eval = "MBS(\"RegEx.Match\";" & Quote ( ¢dpsValue ) & ";" & Quote ( $fmjaml__val ) & ")"
						                                      ]; Exact( Evaluate ( $fmjaml__eval ) ; 1 )
						                                      ) ;
						                                  /* If value is equal… (FIXME exact?) */
						                                  $fmjaml__val = ¢dpsValue
						                                ) /* …REMOVE, otherwise keep */
						                                  ; "" ; $fmjaml__val ) ;
						
						                              /* EOL op `¶'…'` -> Replace EOLs */
						                              $fmjaml__sticky_op = "¶" ; Let([
						                                $fmjaml__sticky_op = "" ;
						                                vResult = Substitute ( $fmjaml__val
						                                                     ; [Char(13) & Char(10);¶]       /* CRLF -> CR */
						                                                     ; [Char(10);¶]                  /* LF -> CR */
						                                                     ; [¶ ; ¢dpsValue] /* CR -> replacement */
						                                                     )
						                                ];
						                                vResult
						                                ) ;
						
						                              /* Divide op  (/'…') */
						                              $fmjaml__sticky_op = "/" ; Let( $fmjaml__sticky_op = "" ; $fmjaml__val / ¢dpsValue ) ;
						
						
						
						                              /* replace operation  ('…'>'…') -> Replace the value by the text */
						                              $fmjaml__sticky_op = ">" ; Let([
						                                $fmjaml__sticky_op = "" ;
						                                vResult = Case( ¢isRegex ;
						                                    /* Regex replace */
						                                  Let([$fmjaml__eval = "MBS(\"RegEx.ReplaceAll\";" & Quote ( $fmjaml__val ) & ";" & Quote ( $fmjaml__replace ) & ";" & Quote ( ¢dpsValue ) & ";" & Quote ( $fmjaml__attributes ) & ")"
						                                      ]; Evaluate ( $fmjaml__eval )
						                                      ) ;
						                                    /* Substitute replace */
						                                    Substitute ( $fmjaml__val ; $fmjaml__replace ; ¢dpsValue )
						                                  );
						                                $fmjaml__replace = ""
						                                ];
						                                vResult
						                                ) ;
						
						
						                              /* replace operation  ('…'>'…') -> Remember attributes */
						                              $fmjaml__sticky_op = "@" ; Let([
						                                $fmjaml__sticky_op = "" ;
						                                $fmjaml__attributes = ¢dpsValue
						                                ];
						                                $fmjaml__val /* don't change */
						                                ) ;
						
						                              /* else if followed by a `>` -> just remember value to replace ('…'>) and replaceOp */
						                              ¢dpsReplaceOp ; Let([
						                                $fmjaml__replace = ¢dpsValue ;
						                                $fmjaml__replaceOp = ¢char
						                                ];
						                                $fmjaml__val /* don't change */
						                                ) ;
						
						                              /* else set-text operation ('…') -> Set value */
						                                ¢dpsValue
						                              );
						
						                        EndIf=0];0))
						                      ]; "" ) ;
						
						    /* Process skip: skip any other simple operators (and continue skipping up to any of the above operators or <EOF>) */
						
						    $fmjaml__skip ; Let([ /* DEBUG fmJAML */
						                        If=Case ( ¢debug ≥ 8 ; Let([
						                          $$fmjaml_log = List( $$fmjaml_log ;
						                                         "SKIPPED: '" & ¢char & "'" ;
						                                         ) ;
						                        EndIf=0];""))
						                      ];"") ;
						
						    /* ANY other active character indicates the clause is not empty */
						    $fmjaml__clause_empty and Let( $fmjaml__clause_empty = "" ; False ) ; "" ;
						
						
						    /* Perform the next operator (using Choose for speed)… */
						    Choose ( Position( "TNOABUJDIMR*!\¶?/" ; Substitute( ¢char ; ["-" ; "?"]; [">" ; "/"]; ["@" ; "/"]; ["#" ; "/"] ) ; 1 ; 1 ) ;
						
						    /* ignore unknown char */ "" ; 
						
						    /* Process data type */
						
						    /* "T" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = JSONString     ; "" ) ;
						    /* "N" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = JSONNumber     ; "" ) ;
						    /* "O" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = JSONObject     ; "" ) ;
						    /* "A" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = JSONArray      ; "" ) ;
						    /* "B" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = JSONBoolean    ; "" ) ;
						    /* "U" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = JSONNull       ; "" ) ;
						    /* "J" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = JSONRaw        ; "" ) ;
						    /* "D" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONDate      ; "" ) ;
						    /* "I" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONTime      ; "" ) ;
						    /* "M" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONTimestamp ; "" ) ;
						    /* "R" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONContainer ; "" ) ;
						
						    /* Process white space operators */
						
						    /* "*" ;*/ Let([$fmjaml__chars_read = While( ¢counter = 1 ; Middle ( ¢dps ; ¢dpsPos + ¢counter ; 1 )="*" ; ¢counter = ¢counter + 1 ; ¢counter ) ;
						                    $fmjaml__val = Case(
						
						                      /* 5*s = Format JSON */
						                      $fmjaml__chars_read ≥ 5 ;
						                        JSONFormatElements ( $fmjaml__val ) ;
						
						                      /* 4*s = Compact JSON */
						                      $fmjaml__chars_read = 4 ;
						                        JSONGetElement ( $fmjaml__val ; "" );
						
						                      /* "*" = simple trim */
						                      $fmjaml__chars_read = 1 ;
						                        Trim( $fmjaml__val ) ;
						
						                      /* "*…" complex trim */
						                        Let(
						                        [
						
						                        /* "**" = horizontal trim (space and tab) */
						                        ¢tmp  = Substitute( $fmjaml__val ; [" ";""] ; [¢TAB; ""] ) ;
						
						                        /* "***" = vertical trim (and EOL) */
						                        ¢tmp  = Case( $fmjaml__chars_read < 3 ; ¢tmp ; Substitute( ¢tmp ; [¶;""] ; [Char(10);""]) ) ;
						
						                        /* trim everything outside the first and last characters */
						                        ¢posChar1 = Position ( $fmjaml__val ; Left( ¢tmp ; 1 ) ; 1 ; 1 ) ;
						                        ¢posCharN = Position ( $fmjaml__val ; Right( ¢tmp ; 1 ) ; Length ( $fmjaml__val ) ; -1 )
						
						                        ];
						
						                        Case( not IsEmpty ( ¢tmp ) ; Middle ( $fmjaml__val ; ¢posChar1 ; ¢posCharN+1 - ¢posChar1 ) )
						                        
						                        )
						                      )
						                   ]; "" ) ;
						    /* "!" ;*/ Let( $fmjaml__sticky_op = "!" ; "" );
						
						    /* Process EOL operator */
						
						    /* "\¶" ;*/ Let([  If=Case ( Middle ( ¢dps ; ¢dpsPos + 1 ; 1 )="1" ; Let([
						                          $fmjaml__chars_read = $fmjaml__chars_read + 1 ;
						                          $fmjaml__val = GetValue( $fmjaml__val ; 1 ) ;
						                        Else=0];"") ; Let([
						                          $fmjaml__sticky_op = "¶"  ;
						                        EndIf=0];"")) ;
						
						                          $fmjaml__sticky_op = Case( Middle ( ¢dps ; ¢dpsPos + 1 ; 1 )="1" ; "" ; $fmjaml__sticky_op )
						                      ]; "" ) ;
						
						    /* 
						     * Process conditional operators:
						     *
						     * - `?` optional operator // tests for presense // not IsEmpty ( «value» )
						     * - `-` mapping operator  // tests for absense  // IsEmpty ( «value» )
						     * 
						     */
						    /* "?" or 
						       "-" */
						    Let ([$fmjaml__context       = ¢char ;
						          $fmjaml__present       = not IsEmpty ( $fmjaml__val );  
						          $fmjaml__test          = $fmjaml__present xor (¢char = "-");  
						          $fmjaml__skip          = not $fmjaml__test ;
						          $fmjaml__clause_empty  = 1 ;
						          $fmjaml__sticky_op     = ""
						         ]; "" ) ;
						
						    /*
						     * Process sticky operators
						     *  `/` Divide
						     *  `>` Replace
						     *  `@` Attributes
						     *  `#` Comment
						     */
						     Let( $fmjaml__sticky_op = ¢char ; "" )
						
						    ) /* End Choose ( parseNextOp ) */
						
						  ); /* End SWITCH ( parseNextOp ) */
						
						  /*********
						  NEXT ¢char
						  **********
						
						  …skipping multiple characters read, if necessary
						  */
						  ¢dpsPos = ¢dpsPos + $fmjaml__chars_read
						
						  ];"")
						  ); /* End parseDTS */
						
						
						/*****************
						Default data types
						*****************/
						
						$fmjaml__dtype = Case(
						    not IsEmpty ( $fmjaml__dtype ) ; $fmjaml__dtype ;
						    ¢posEquals = 0    ; JSONObject ;
						    IsEmpty ( ¢expr ) ; JSONNull ;
						    GetAsNumber ( ¢expr ) = ¢expr ; JSONNumber ;
						    ¢expr = "true" or ¢expr = "false" ; JSONBoolean ;
						      JSONString
						  );
						
						
						
						
						
						/******************
						Output line to JSON
						******************/
						
						$fmjaml__json = Case (
						
						    /* DROP line, if no path */
						    IsEmpty( $fmjaml__path ) ;
						      $fmjaml__json ;
						
						    /* Set element based on the calculated path value and data type */
						      JSONSetElement ( $fmjaml__json ; $fmjaml__path ; 
						        Case(
						          /* Encode Date/Time/Timestamp using ISO 8601 (RFC 3339) without timezone (YYYY-MM-DD / HH:MM:SS / YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS) */
						          $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONDate ;      Year  ( $fmjaml__val )
						               & SerialIncrement ( "-00" ; Month ( $fmjaml__val ) )
						               & SerialIncrement ( "-00" ; Day   ( $fmjaml__val ) ) ;
						          $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONTime ;
						                 SerialIncrement ( "00"  ; Hour    ( $fmjaml__val ) )
						               & SerialIncrement ( ":00" ; Minute  ( $fmjaml__val ) )
						               & SerialIncrement ( ":00" ; Seconds ( $fmjaml__val ) ) ;
						          $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONTimestamp ; Year    ( $fmjaml__val )
						               & SerialIncrement ( "-00" ; Month   ( $fmjaml__val ) )
						               & SerialIncrement ( "-00" ; Day     ( $fmjaml__val ) )
						               & SerialIncrement ( "T00" ; Hour    ( $fmjaml__val ) )
						               & SerialIncrement ( ":00" ; Minute  ( $fmjaml__val ) )
						               & SerialIncrement ( ":00" ; Seconds ( $fmjaml__val ) ) ;
						          /* Encode container using RFC 4648, above all, no problematic line breaks! */
						          $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONContainer ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
						                           ;["file.name" ; GetContainerAttribute ( $fmjaml__val ; "filename" ) ; JSONString]
						                           ;["file.data" ; Base64EncodeRFC ( 4648 ; $fmjaml__val ) ; JSONString]
						                           ) ;
						            $fmjaml__val
						        ) ;
						        Case(
						          $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONDate      ; JSONString ;
						          $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONTime      ; JSONString ;
						          $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONTimestamp ; JSONString ;
						          $fmjaml__dtype = ¢JSONContainer ; JSONObject ;
						            $fmjaml__dtype
						        ) 
						     )
						  );
						
						If=Case( ¢debug ≥ 5 ; Let([
						    $$fmjaml_debug_dtype = List( $$fmjaml_debug_dtype ; "line " & ¢line & ": " & $fmjaml__dtype ) ;
						    $$fmjaml_log = List( $$fmjaml_log ; "line["&¢line_i&"] -> " & 
						      Case( IsEmpty($fmjaml__path ) ; "PATH DROPPED" ; Quote( $fmjaml__json ) ) ;
						      "--------"
						    ) ;
						EndIf=0];""));
						
						/*********
						NEXT ¢line
						**********
						
						skipping multi-line heredocs, as necessary
						*/
						
						¢line_i = ¢line_i + 1 + $fmjaml__inc
						
						];  
						
						/************
						RETURN result
						*************
						
						…tidying vars before returning the actions array
						*/
						
						Let(
						[
						$fmjaml__chars_read = "" ;
						$fmjaml__context = "" ;
						$fmjaml__dtype = "" ;
						$fmjaml__inc = "" ;
						$fmjaml__present = "" ;
						$fmjaml__skip = "" ;
						$fmjaml__sticky_op = "" ;
						$fmjaml__test = "" ;
						$fmjaml__path = "" ;
						$fmjaml__path_prev  = "" ;
						$fmjaml__val = ""
						];
						
						$fmjaml__json
						
						)
						
						)
						/*---------------- ✂︎ ----------------*/
						) ]
		# ` ^^^^^^^^^^^
	Else
		# --------------------------
		# ### fmJAML - loose CF-call
		# --------------------------
		# Call the custom function by evaluation
		  -> so that this code does not break when pasted into a solution without the CF
		Set Variable [ $fmide__fmjaml[1] ; Let(
						[
						
						pOptionalInitialJSON = "" ;
						pFmJAML = $fmide__sp
						
						];
						
						Evaluate ( Substitute ( 
						  "fmJAML ( pOptionalInitialJSON ; pFmJAML )"
						  ; [ "pOptionalInitialJSON" ; Quote( pOptionalInitialJSON ) ]
						  ; [ "pFmJAML"              ; Quote ( pFmJAML )             ]
						))
						
						) ]
	End If
	
	Set Variable [ $fmide__jsp[1] ; Case (
					
					/* Embed (actions) array in an actions object */
					Left( $fmide__fmjaml ; 1 ) = "[" ; 
					  JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "actions" ; $fmide__fmjaml ; JSONArray ) ;
					
					/* Pass rubbish as is for fail fast */
					Left( $fmide__fmjaml ; 1 ) ≠ "{" ; $fmide__fmjaml ;
					
					1 ; Let(
					[
					
					vKeys = JSONListKeys ( $fmide__fmjaml ; "" )
					
					];
					
					Case( 
					
					/* Pass actions object as is */
					vKeys = "actions" ; $fmide__fmjaml ;
					
					/* Embed action object in first element of an actions object array */
					vKeys = "action" ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "actions[0]" ; JSONGetElement( $fmide__fmjaml ; "action" ) ; JSONObject ) ;
					
					/* Otherwise pass result as is for fail fast */
					$fmide__fmjaml
					
					))) ]
	
Else
	# ===================================
	# ## Handle all other parameter types
	# ===================================
	Set Variable [ $fmide__jsp[1] ; Case (
					
					/* A standard JSONObject was passed */
					$fmide__jsp_type = JSONObject ;
					  Let(
					  [
					
					  ¢JSON = JSONFormatElements ( $fmide__sp )
					
					  ];
					
					  Case( Left ( ¢JSON ; 1 ) = "{" ; ¢JSON ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "malformed_parameter" ; $fmide__sp ; JSONString ) )
					
					  ) ;
					
					
					
					
					
					/* A naked actions array was passed -> standardise it as an actions-object */
					$fmide__jsp_type = JSONArray ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "actions" ; $fmide__sp ; JSONArray ) ;
					
					
					
					
					
					
					
					/* Variable definitions were passed -> Evaluate */
					$fmide__jsp_type = "Let" ; Let (
					  [
					
					  vEvaluateOK = Evaluate ( List(
					  "Let([" ;
					  $fmide__sp ;
					  Case ( Right ( Substitute ( $fmide__sp ; [" " ; ""] ; [Char(9) ; ""] ; [Char(10) ; ""] ; [¶ ; ""] ) ; 1 ) ≠ ";" ; ";" ) ;
					  "vVoid=0];\"OK\")"
					  ))
					
					  ];
					
					  "{}" & Case( vEvaluateOK ≠ "OK" ; Let([
					    $$fmide_get_last_error = 960 ;
					    $$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "fmIDE Let-parameter is invalid." ;
					    $fmide__error_thrown = 1
					    ];""))
					
					   );
					
					/* something else was passed - some kind of pseudocode? */
					$fmide__jsp_type = "Text" ; While ( 
					[
					
					¢lines = $fmide__sp ;
					
					¢result = "{}" ;
					
					¢i = 1 ;
					¢n = ValueCount ( ¢lines )
					
					]; ¢i <= ¢n ;[
					
					¢line = GetValue ( ¢lines ; ¢i ) ;
					¢index = "actions.[" & ¢i-1 & "]" ;
					
					¢result = JSONSetElement ( ¢result ; ¢index & ".action_name" ; ¢line ; JSONString ) ;
					¢i = ¢i + 1
					
					];
					
					¢result
					
					) ;
					
					
					
					
					
					
					
					/* nothing was passed */
					$fmide__jsp_type = JSONNull ; "{}"
					
					
					
					
					
					) ]
End If
Set Variable [ $fmide__jsp_keys[1] ; JSONListKeys( $fmide__jsp ; "" ) ]




If [ $fmide_debug or not IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( $fmide__jsp_keys ; "fmide_debug" ) ) ]
	# ## Debug
	# If the $fmide_debug variable has been set in the fmp url, then show what has arrived
	Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide_dialog_title & " DEBUG" ; Message: While([
					
					¢fmide__parameter_names = $fmide__parameter_names_all ;
					
					¢result = "" ;
					¢i = 1;
					¢n = ValueCount( ¢fmide__parameter_names )
					
					] ; ¢i <= ¢n ; [
					
					¢fmide_parameter_name = GetValue( ¢fmide__parameter_names ; ¢i ) ;
					¢fmide_scriptvar_name = "$" & ¢fmide_parameter_name ;
					¢fmide_scriptvar_value = Evaluate( ¢fmide_scriptvar_name );
					
					¢result = ¢result & Case( not IsEmpty( ¢fmide_scriptvar_value ) ; ¢fmide_parameter_name & ": " & ¢fmide_scriptvar_value ) & "¶" ;
					
					¢i = ¢i + 1
					
					];
					
					¢result
					
					) ; Default Button: "OK" ; Button 2: "Pause" ; Button 3: "Halt" ]
	If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 2 ]
		Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
	Else If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 3 ]
		Halt Script
	End If
End If



# About fmIDE `hard pauses` and `continuations` (in order to access the Custom Function Dialog):
# - Some FileMaker dialogs, notably the custom function dialog, are not scriptable and can only be opened when no script is running.
# - This requires fmIDE to exit the script before opening the custom functions dialog, and then continue from where it left off afterwards
# - fmIDE supports such 'hard pauses' via a `continuation`, which works like this:
#   1. The entire fmIDE running state is packed into a continuation object
    2. Using MBS a delayed keyboard press or menu call is triggered to open the Custom Functions
    3. Using MBS a delayed restart of the fmIDE script is scheduled passing the continuation state via the "continuation" parameter
    4. The Script then exits
       - The Custom Functions dialog is opened
       - The user does his work in the CF Dialog
       - The user closes the CF dialog
       - The MBS Schedule calls the fmIDE script passing the "continuation" parameter
    5. The Script is restarted by the MBS Schedule, all state variables are reinstated from the `continuation` parameter
       - and the MBS Schedue is reset - using the saved $$fmide__continuation_mbs_schedule_ref value

# Read more about Continuations here
# - About Continuations in Java: @see https://www.artima.com/articles/continuations-in-java
# - About Continuations in general (Wikipedia): @see https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Continuation

Set Variable [ $fmide__continuation[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__jsp ; "continuation" ) ]
If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide__continuation ) ]
	# # Normal start
	
	# ## Read action parameters from variables or the JSON Script Parameter
	Set Variable [ $fmide_actions[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide_actions ) ; $fmide_actions ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__jsp ; "actions" )) ]
	If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide_actions ) ]
		# ## Extract action information from variables or from the JSON Script Parameter
		Set Variable [ $fmide__parameter_names_found[1] ; While([
						
						¢fmide__parameter_names = $fmide__parameter_names_all ;
						
						¢result = "" ;
						¢i = 1;
						¢n = ValueCount( ¢fmide__parameter_names )
						
						] ; ¢i <= ¢n ; [
						
						¢fmide_parameter_name = GetValue( ¢fmide__parameter_names ; ¢i ) ;
						¢fmide_parameter_value = JSONGetElement ( $fmide__jsp ; ¢fmide_parameter_name ) ;
						¢fmide_scriptvar_name = "$" & ¢fmide_parameter_name ;
						¢fmide_scriptvar_value = Evaluate( ¢fmide_scriptvar_name );
						
						¢result = List( ¢result ; Case(
						not IsEmpty( ¢fmide_scriptvar_value ) ; ¢fmide_parameter_name ; /* Variable exists already */
						not IsEmpty( ¢fmide_parameter_value ) ; Let(
						  [
						  ¢defineVarAndReturnParameterName = "Let([" & ¢fmide_scriptvar_name & "=JSONGetElement ( $fmide__jsp ; " & Quote( ¢fmide_parameter_name ) & ")];" & Quote( ¢fmide_parameter_name ) & ")"
						  ];
						  Evaluate ( ¢defineVarAndReturnParameterName )
						  )
						));
						
						¢i = ¢i + 1
						
						];
						
						¢result
						
						) ]
		
		# # fmIDE 'Name that Thing' API default actions
		
		# Make actions array from action, if present, or construct default action(s) for the variables
		Set Variable [ $fmide_actions[1] ; Case ( 
						
						/* a single action object */
						not IsEmpty ( $fmide_action ) and Left( $fmide_action ; 1 ) = "{" ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0]" ; $fmide_action ; JSONObject]
						);
						
						
						/* a single action, but incorrectly passed as a string -> treat as action_name */
						not IsEmpty ( $fmide_action ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].action_name" ; $fmide_action ; JSONObject]
						);
						
						
						/* a single action_name */
						not IsEmpty ( $fmide__action_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].action_name" ; $fmide__action_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						/* otherwise construct the default actions, if only a parameter is passed */
						
						not IsEmpty ( $curl_options ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Insert from URL.curl_options" ; $curl_options ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Insert from URL.url" ; $url ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Insert from URL.with_dialog" ; $with_dialog ; JSONString]
						);
						
						/* fmIDE Name that Thing API */
						
						not IsEmpty ( $account_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Account.account_name" ; $account_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $custom_function_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Custom Function.custom_function_name" ; $custom_function_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $custom_menu_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Custom Menu.custom_menu_name" ; $custom_menu_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $custom_menu_item_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Custom Menu Item.custom_menu_item_name" ; $custom_menu_item_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $custom_menu_set_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Custom Menu Set.custom_menu_set_name" ; $custom_menu_set_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $custom_menu_item_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Custom Menu Item.custom_menu_item_name" ; $custom_menu_item_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $dapi_request ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Execute FileMaker Data API.dapi_request" ; $dapi_request ; JSONObject]
						);
						
						
						
						not IsEmpty ( $external_data_source_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To External Data Source.external_data_source_name" ; $external_data_source_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $extended_privilege_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Extended Privilege.extended_privilege_name" ; $extended_privilege_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $fmxmlsnippet ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Set Clipboard Objects.fmxmlsnippet" ; $fmxmlsnippet ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $fmxmlsnippet_value ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Set Clipboard Objects.fmxmlsnippet_value" ; $fmxmlsnippet_value ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $base_table_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Base Table.base_table_name" ; $base_table_name ; JSONString] 
						; ["[0].Go To Base Table.field_name" ; $field_name ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Base Table.field_id" ; $field_id ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $table_name ) or Position( $field_name ; "::" ; 1 ; 1 ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Table.table_name" ; $table_name ; JSONString] 
						; ["[0].Go To Table.field_name" ; $field_name ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Table.field_id" ; $field_id ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $field_id ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Field.field_id" ; $field_id ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $field_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Field.field_name" ; $field_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $field_number ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Field.field_number" ; $field_number ; JSONString]
						);
						
						
						not IsEmpty ( $layout_field_id ) or
						not IsEmpty ( $layout_field_name ) or
						not IsEmpty ( $layout_field_number ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Field.layout_id" ; $layout_id ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Field.layout_name" ; $layout_name ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Field.layout_number" ; $layout_number ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Field.layout_field_id" ; $layout_field_id ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Field.layout_field_name" ; $layout_field_name ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Field.layout_field_number" ; $layout_field_number ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $layout_part_id ) or
						not IsEmpty ( $layout_part_name ) or
						not IsEmpty ( $layout_part_number ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Part.layout_id" ; $layout_id ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Part.layout_name" ; $layout_name ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Part.layout_number" ; $layout_number ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Part.layout_part_id" ; $layout_part_id ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Part.layout_part_name" ; $layout_part_name ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout Part.layout_part_number" ; $layout_part_number ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $object_id ) or
						not IsEmpty ( $object_name ) or
						not IsEmpty ( $object_number ); JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Object.layout_id" ; $layout_id ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Object.layout_name" ; $layout_name ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Object.layout_number" ; $layout_number ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Object.object_id" ; $object_id ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Object.object_name" ; Quote( $object_name ) ; JSONString] /* Object name is a calculation */
						; ["[0].Go To Object.object_number" ; $object_number ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $layout_id ) or
						not IsEmpty ( $layout_name ) or
						not IsEmpty ( $layout_number ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Layout.layout_id" ; $layout_id ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout.layout_name" ; $layout_name ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Layout.layout_number" ; $layout_number ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty( $message ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Show Custom Dialog.message" ; $message ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Show Custom Dialog.buttons" ; $buttons ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $privilege_set_id ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Privilege Set.privilege_set_id" ; $privilege_set_id ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $privilege_set_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Privilege Set.privilege_set_name" ; $privilege_set_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $privilege_set_number ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Privilege Set.privilege_set_number" ; $privilege_set_number ; JSONString]
						);
						
						
						not IsEmpty ( $script_name ) or 
						not IsEmpty ( $script_name_stub ) or 
						not IsEmpty ( $script_id ) or 
						not IsEmpty ( $script_number ); JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						
						; ["[0].Go To Script.script_id" ; $script_id ; JSONString] 
						; ["[0].Go To Script.script_name" ; $script_name ; JSONString] 
						; ["[0].Go To Script.script_name_stub" ; $script_name_stub ; JSONString] 
						; ["[0].Go To Script.script_number" ; $script_number ; JSONString]
						
						; ["[0].Go To Script.script_step_number" ; $script_step_number ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Script.script_step_number_to" ; $script_step_number_to ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Script.script_step_range" ; $script_step_range ; JSONString]
						
						; ["[0].Go To Script.script_step_search" ; $script_step_search ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Go To Script.script_step_search_value" ; $script_step_search_value ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $script_step_number_to ) or
						not IsEmpty ( $script_step_range ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Select Script Steps.script_step_number" ; $script_step_number ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Select Script Steps.script_step_number_to" ; $script_step_number_to ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Select Script Steps.script_step_range" ; $script_step_range ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $script_step_number ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Script Step.script_step_number" ; $script_step_number ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $script_step_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].FindScriptStep.script_step_search" ; $script_step_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $script_step_search ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].FindScriptStep.script_step_search" ; $script_step_search ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $script_step_search_value ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].FindScriptStep.script_step_search_value" ; $script_step_search_value ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $t_o_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Table Occurrence.t_o_name" ; $t_o_name ; JSONString] 
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $theme_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Theme.theme_name" ; $theme_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $url ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Open URL.url" ; $url ; JSONString]
						; ["[0].Open URL.with_dialog" ; $with_dialog ; JSONString]
						);
						
						not IsEmpty ( $value_list_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Go To Value List.value_list_name" ; $value_list_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						
						not IsEmpty ( $window_name ) ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0].Select Window.window_name" ; $window_name ; JSONString]
						);
						
						ValueCount( $fmide__jsp_keys ) = 1 ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" 
						; ["[0]" ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__jsp ; GetValue( $fmide__jsp_keys ; 1 ) ) ; JSONObject] 
						; ["[0].action_name" ; GetValue( $fmide__jsp_keys ; 1 ) ; JSONString]
						);
						
						
						/* leave empty if no variables found */
						) ]
		
	End If
	
	
	# # Build actions array
	If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide_actions ) ]
		# actions = empty
		If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide_action ) ]
			# Pack single action in an array
			Set Variable [ $fmide_actions[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" ; "[0]" ; $fmide_action ; JSONObject ) ]
		Else
			Set Variable [ $fmide_actions[1] ; "[]" ]
		End If
	Else If [ Left ( $fmide_actions ; 1 ) = "[" ]
		# actions = JSONArray
	Else
		# A text parameter has already been handled above by $fmide__jsp
		# FIXME - Can this line be reached? When?
	End If
	
	
	# # Initialise the execution state
	
	# Initialise stack (for If and Loop)
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack[1] ; "[{}]" ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack_top[1] ; "[0]" ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_block[1] ; "" ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; "" ]
	
	# Initialise programme counter
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_index[1] ; 0 ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_index_max[1] ; ValueCount( JSONListKeys ( $fmide_actions ; "" ) ) ]
	
	# Local Vars
	Set Variable [ $fmide__local_variables[1] ; "" ]
	
	# Original context
	Set Variable [ $fmide__original_layout_number[1] ; Get( LayoutNumber ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__original_window_name[1] ; GetValue ( WindowNames ; 1 ) ]
	
Else
	# # Continuation start: read the current execution state back from the continuation
	If [ Get ( ActiveModifierKeys ) = 1+2+4+8+16 ]
		Show Custom Dialog [ Title: "fmIDE Continuation" ; Message: Get( ScriptParameter ) ; Default Button: "OK" ; Button 2: "Pause" ]
		If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 2 ]
			Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
		End If
	End If
	
	// # Unnecessary - the schedules free themselves up automatically
	// # First, free up the MBS Schedule used to trigger the continuation call
	// Set Variable [ $fmide__r[1] ; Let(
					[
					vSchedleRef = $$fmide__continuation_mbs_schedule_ref;
					$$fmide__continuation_mbs_schedule_ref = ""
					];
					MBS( "Schedule.Release"; vSchedleRef )
					) ]
	
	# Actions
	Set Variable [ $fmide_actions[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__continuation ; "actions" ) ]
	
	# Initialise stack (for If and Loop)
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__continuation ; "action_stack" ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack_top[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__continuation ; "action_stack_top" ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_block[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__continuation ; "action_block" ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__continuation ; "action_skip" ) ]
	# Initialise programme counter
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_index[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__continuation ; "action_index" ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_index_max[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__continuation ; "action_index_max" ) ]
	
	# Redefine local variables
	Set Variable [ $fmide__local_variables[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__continuation ; "fmide_local_variables" ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__local_variables[1] ; While(
					[
					
					¢vars = $fmide__local_variables ;
					¢i = 1;
					¢n = ValueCount ( ¢vars )
					
					]; ¢i <= ¢n ;[
					
					¢var = Substitute( GetValue ( ¢vars ; ¢i ) ; "$" ; "" ) ;
					
					¢expression = "
					  Let([
					  $" & ¢var & "=$$fmide_local_variable_" & ¢var & " ;
					  $$fmide_local_variable_" & ¢var & "=\"\" ;
					  r=0];\"\”)" ;
					$$expression = ¢expression ;
					/* Redfine the variable as a local variable */
					¢ignore_errors = Evaluate ( ¢expression ) ;
					
					¢i = ¢i + 1
					
					];
					
					""
					
					) ]
	
	# Original context
	Set Variable [ $fmide__original_layout_number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__continuation ; "original_layout_number" ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__original_window_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__continuation ; "original_window_name" ) ]
	
	# Clear the continuation
	Set Variable [ $fmide__continuation[1] ; "" ]
End If

# # Vars defined at this point
  
  ## Public Vars
  
  `$fmide_version
  `$fmide_dialog_title
  `$fmide_actions
  
  ## Private Vars
  
  ### Parameter vars
  
  - `$fmide__jsp`: JSON Script Parameter
  - `$fmide__jsp_type`: The type of parameter format that `$fmide__jsp` has
  - `$fmide__jsp_keys`: The list of keys that `$fmide__jsp` contains / or a JSON `?…` error
  
  - `$fmide__parameter_names_all`: List of all possible parameter names
  - `$fmide__parameter_names_found`: List of found parameters names
  
  ### Environment vars
  
  - `$fmide__is_mac`: 1 if on a mac
  - `$fmide__is_mbs`: 1 if the MBS plugin is present
  - `$fmide__is_win`: 1 if on windows
  - `$fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key`: The key for MBS to use when controlling the keyboard
  - `$fmide__original_layout_number`: The number of the original layout for the `Go to Original Layout` action
  - `$fmide__original_window_name`: The name of the original window for the `Go to Original Window` action
  
  ### fmIDE Action Script Interpreter state
  
  - `$fmide__action_index`: loop variable / index into the `$fmide_actions array / 'action pointer'
  - `$fmide__action_index_max`:  loop variable / length of the `$fmide_actions array / 'action pointer' exclusive maximum value
  - `$fmide__action_line`: FIXME: What is?
  - `$fmide__action_block`: inside blocks = the name of the action that started the current block
  
  - `$fmide__action_skip`: Defines what to skip:
  
    - `If`: Skip the current `If`/`Else If` section
    - `End If`: Skip to the next `End If` step, because an `If`/`ElseIf` section is completed
    - `{block}`:  Skip everything in the rest of the block, that is, until the stack is popped off
  
  - `$fmide__action_stack`: The stack controlling the execution depth and logic
  
    - contains action_stack frame objects each having the following information
  
      - `block`: The name of the action that started the block
      - `index`: The action index identifying the line in the action array
      - `skip`: 1 = skip
  
  - `$fmide__action_stack_top`: Pointer to the top of the action stack
  - `$fmide__actions_string`: FIXME: What is?
  
  - `$fmide__local_variables`: A list of variables defined with the `Set` action
  
  
  - `$fmide__continuation`: 1 = hiatus + continuation
