  - `$fmide__continuation_seconds`: Number of seconds before the hiatus continues
  
  ### Temporary variables
  
  - `$fmide__i`: Loop variable
  - `$fmide__n`: Loop variable length
  - `$fmide__r`: (MBS) result variable
  




# ########################
# # Process actions array
# ########################





Loop [ Flush: Always ]
	Exit Loop If [ $fmide__action_index >= $fmide__action_index_max ]
	# ## Process next action
	Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_actions ; $fmide__action_index ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_element_type[1] ; JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_actions ; $fmide__action_index ) ]
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_number[1] ; $fmide__action_index+1 ]
	
	If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide_pause_on_condition ) and not IsEmpty ( $fmide_pause_on_condition_calculation ) ]
		# Evaluate pause on condition calculation
		Set Variable [ $fmide__pause_on_condition_value[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide_pause_on_condition_calculation ) ]
		If [ not $fmide_debugger and $fmide__pause_on_condition_value ]
			# true -> trigger debugger
			Set Variable [ $fmide_debugger[1] ; 1 ]
		End If
	End If
	
	# # TRY (fmIDE error handling)
	Loop [ Flush: Always ]
		Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; "" ]
		
		If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide_action ) ]
			
			# Skip null
			Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
			
		Else
			# ## Process action_name
			Set Variable [ $fmide__action_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "action_name" ) ]
			If [ Left( $fmide__action_name ; 1 ) = "?" ]
				# JSON error - is handeled below
			Else If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide__action_name ) ]
				# Syntax = action name is one of the action parameters
				  action.action_name
				  action.action_param1
				  action.action_param2
				  …
				# use explicit action name
			Else
				# action name is json key
				Set Variable [ $fmide__action_name[1] ; JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ]
				If [ ValueCount ( $fmide__action_name ) = 1 ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide__action_element_type[1] ; JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_name ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_name ) ]
				Else
					# FIXME Handle - action name has a ¶ in it
				End If
				
			End If
			
			If [ $fmide_debugger ]
				Show Custom Dialog [ Title: "fmIDE Debugger" ; Message: Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide__pause_on_condition_value ) ; "Pausing on condition: " & $fmide__pause_on_condition_value & ¶ ) & 
								$fmide__action_number & "/" & $fmide__action_index_max & " " & Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide__action_skip ) ; "(skip " & $fmide__action_skip & ") " ) & Quote ( $fmide__action_name ) & ¶ & 
								$fmide_action ; Default Button: "Continue" ; Button 2: "Pause/Debug" ; Button 3: "…" ]
				If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 1 ]
					# 1. Continue
				Else If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 2 ]
					# 2. Pause
					Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
				Else If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 3 ]
					# 3. …
					Show Custom Dialog [ Title: "fmIDE Debugger" ; Message: "[" & $fmide__action_index & "]/" & $fmide__action_index_max & " " & Quote ( $fmide__action_name ) & ¶ & 
									$fmide_action & "¶" & 
									Case( $$fmide_get_last_error ; "Error: " & $$fmide_get_last_error ) &
									Case( not IsEmpty ( $$fmide_get_last_error ) ; " (" & $$fmide_get_last_error_detail & ")" ) ; Default Button: "Debugger OFF" ; Button 2: "Continue" ; Button 3: "HALT" ]
					If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 1 ]
						# 1. Debugger off
						Set Variable [ $fmide_debugger[1] ; "" ]
					Else If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 2 ]
						# 2. Continue
					Else If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 3 ]
						# 3. HALT
						Halt Script
					End If
				End If
			End If
			
			# ## Convert action_name to UpperCamelCase, so that all these formats are supported:
			# - Go to Layout (Natural)
			# - go_to_layout (lower_snake_case)
			# - gotolayout (onelowerwordcase)
			# - GoToLayout (UpperCamelCase)
			# - Go   to   layout (Unnatural spacing)
			Set Variable [ $fmide__action_name[1] ; Let(
							[
							
							vTemp = Substitute ( Proper( $fmide__action_name )
							;[" " ; ""]
							;[Char(9) ; ""]
							;["_" ; ""]
							;["-" ; ""]
							)
							
							];
							
							/* Remove "/" chars from the action name whilst keeping the disabled marker `//` at the beginning of the line */
							Case( Left ( vTemp ; 2 ) = "//" ; "//" ) & Substitute ( vTemp ; ["/" ; ""] )
							
							) ]
			
			# ## Action enable / disable
			# An action is considered disabled if
			  - disable is true, or
			  - enable is (explicitly) false, or
			  - the action starts with //
			Set Variable [ $fmide__enable[1] ; Case ( $fmide__action_element_type = JSONObject ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "enable" ) ) ]
			Set Variable [ $fmide__disable[1] ; Case ( $fmide__action_element_type = JSONObject ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "disable" ) ) ]
			
			Set Variable [ $fmide__enable[1] ; Case (
							IsEmpty ( $fmide__enable ) ; 1 ;       /* default is enabled */
							Left( $fmide__enable ; 1 ) = "?" ; 0 ; /* explicitly disable if enable value is mangled */
							$fmide__enable                         /* enable if enabled */
							) ]
			Set Variable [ $fmide__disable[1] ; Case (
							Left( $fmide__action_name ; 2 ) = "//" or  /* visually disabled in action name */ 
							$fmide__disable                        or  /* disabled via disable property set to true */ 
							not $fmide__enable                     or  /* disabled via enable property set to false */ 
							Left( $fmide__disable ; 1 ) = "?" ; 1      /* disable if the disable property is mangled */ 
							) ]
			
			If [ 0 ]
				
				# # Process disabled/missing/garbled actions
				
			Else If [ $fmide__disable ]
				# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#-/
				#  Disabled action => skip
				
				Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
				
			Else If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide__action_name ) ]
				# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#
				# No action => skip
				
				Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
				
			Else If [ Left ( $fmide__action_name ; 1 ) = "?" ]
				# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#?
				# Mangled action => skip
				Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
				
			Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "#" or $fmide__action_name = "# (comment)" or Left ( $fmide__action_name ; 1 ) = "#" or $fmide__action_name = "Comment" ]
				# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/##
				# Comment => skip
				Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
				
				
			Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "If" or
							$fmide__action_name = "ElseIf" or
							$fmide__action_name = "Else" or
							$fmide__action_name = "EndIf" or
							
							$fmide__action_name = "Loop" or
							$fmide__action_name = "ExitLoopIf" or
							$fmide__action_name = "EndLoop" ]
				
				# # Process control actions and Stack Logic
				
				# - Manage skipping
				# - Detect illegal block semantics
				# - Push + Pop stack at start + end of blocks
				
				# ## Process LongForm
				
				If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONObject ]
					# If.calculation -> If = «calculation»
					Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement( $fmide_action ; "calculation" ) ]
				End If
				
				# ## Process Stack
				If [ 0 ]
					# SWITCH
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "If" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#if
					# See https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#if
					If [ not IsEmpty( $fmide__action_skip ) ]
						# Skip this entire block
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; "{block}" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
					Else
						# Perform this step // If the calculation does not evaluate to true then skip the `If` section
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_test_success[1] ; GetAsBoolean( Evaluate ( $fmide_action ) ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; Case( not $fmide__action_test_success ; "If" ) ]
						# $fmide__action_skip REMAINS EMPTY IF THE IF-BLOCK IS TO BE RUN
					End If
					# Push action block onto stack
					Set Variable [ $fmide__action_block[1] ; $fmide__action_name ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack_top[1] ; SerialIncrement ( $fmide__action_stack_top ; 1 ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack[1] ; JSONSetElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top ; 
									JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; 
									["block" ; $fmide__action_block ; JSONString];
									["index" ; $fmide__action_index ; JSONNumber];
									["skip" ; $fmide__action_skip ; JSONString]
									) ; JSONObject ) ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ElseIf" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#else-if
					If [ $fmide__action_block ≠ "If" ]
						# Error / Bad Structure / Else If without If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 5 ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_detail[1] ; "Invalid fmIDE action script structure at action " & $fmide__action_number & ": " & $fmide__action_name ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__error_thrown[1] ; 1 ]
						
					Else If [ $fmide__action_skip = "{block}" ]
						# Skip this entire block
						Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
					Else If [ $fmide__action_skip = "EndIf" ]
						# Skip to EndIf
						Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
					Else
						# Perform this step
						If [ IsEmpty( $fmide__action_skip ) ]
							# Empty -> previous (Else)If-part was run => skip to EndIf
							Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; "EndIf" ]
						Else If [ $fmide__action_skip = "If" ]
							# If-part was skipped => try this one
							Set Variable [ $fmide__action_test_success[1] ; GetAsBoolean( Evaluate ( $fmide_action ) ) ]
							If [ $fmide__action_test_success ]
								# Perform this section
								Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; "" ]
							End If
						End If
						# Modify top stack block
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack[1] ; JSONSetElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top & ".skip" ; $fmide__action_skip ; JSONString ) ]
					End If
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Else" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#else
					If [ $fmide__action_block ≠ "If" ]
						# Error / Bad Structure / Else If without If
						# @todo
						
					Else If [ $fmide__action_skip = "{block}" ]
						# Skip this entire block
						Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
					Else If [ $fmide__action_skip = "EndIf" ]
						# Skip to EndIf
						Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
					Else
						# Perform this step
						# If the If-part was skipped run the else part, otherwise skip to EndIf
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; Case( $fmide__action_skip = "If" ; "" ; "EndIf" ) ]
						# Modify top stack block
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack[1] ; JSONSetElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top & ".skip" ; $fmide__action_skip ; JSONString ) ]
					End If
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "EndIf" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#end-if
					
					If [ $fmide__action_block ≠ "If" ]
						# Error / Bad Structure / End If without If
						# @todo
						
					Else
						# Pop stack
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack[1] ; JSONDeleteElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack_top[1] ; SerialIncrement ( $fmide__action_stack_top ; -1 ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_block[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top & ".block" ) ]
						# If ExitAfterLast is active, keep skipping, otherwise reinstate the previous skip-state outside of the if
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; Case ( $fmide__action_skip = "ExitAfterLast" ; $fmide__action_skip ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top & ".skip" ) ) ]
						
					End If
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Loop" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#loop
					# Push action block onto stack
					Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; Case( not IsEmpty( $fmide__action_skip ) ; "{block}" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide__action_block[1] ; $fmide__action_name ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack_top[1] ; SerialIncrement ( $fmide__action_stack_top ; 1 ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack[1] ; JSONSetElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top ; 
									JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; 
									["block" ; $fmide__action_block ; JSONString];
									["index" ; $fmide__action_index ; JSONNumber];
									["skip" ; $fmide__action_skip ; JSONString]
									) ; JSONObject ) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ExitLoopIf" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#exit-loop-if
					If [ $fmide__action_block ≠ "Loop" ]
						# Error / Bad Structure / ExitLoopIf without Loop
						# @todo
						
					Else If [ $fmide__action_skip = "{block}" ]
						# Skip this entire block
						Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
					Else If [ $fmide__action_skip = "EndLoop" ]
						# Skip to EndLoop
						Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
					Else
						# Perform this step
						If [ GetAsBoolean( Evaluate ( $fmide_action ) ) ]
							# Exit loop!
							Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; $fmide__action_name ]
							# Modify top stack block
							Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack[1] ; JSONSetElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top & ".skip" ; $fmide__action_skip ; JSONString ) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "EndLoop" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#end-loop
					If [ $fmide__action_block ≠ "Loop" ]
						# Error / Bad Structure / ExitLoopIf without Loop
						# @todo
					Else If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide__action_skip ) ]
						# Loop back to action after the corresponding Loop step
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_index[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top & ".index" ) ]
					Else
						# Pop stack
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack[1] ; JSONDeleteElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_stack_top[1] ; SerialIncrement ( $fmide__action_stack_top ; -1 ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_block[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top & ".block" ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide__action_stack ; $fmide__action_stack_top & ".skip" ) ]
					End If
					
					# END SWITCH
				End If
				# ## End Process Stack
				
			Else If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide__action_skip ) ]
				# Skip all other steps
				Set Variable [ $fmide__skipped[1] ; 1 ]
				
				
				
			Else
				# # Process actions
				
				If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONObject ]
					// Set Variable [  ;  ]
					
				End If
				
				
				If [ 0 ]
					# Action switch
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "AdjustWindow" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#adjust-window
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "state" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "state" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___state ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ 0 ]
						# Switch
					Else If [ $fmide___state = "Resize to Fit" ]
						Adjust Window [ Resize to Fit ]
					Else If [ $fmide___state = "Maximize" ]
						Adjust Window [ Maximize ]
					Else If [ $fmide___state = "Minimize" ]
						Adjust Window [ Minimize ]
					Else If [ $fmide___state = "Restore" ]
						Adjust Window [ Restore ]
					Else If [ $fmide___state = "Hide" ]
						Adjust Window [ Hide ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; "" ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "AllowUserAbort" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#allow-user-abort
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "state" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "state" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___state ) ]
					
					# Action
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_allow_abort_state[1] ; GetAsBoolean ( $fmide___state ) ]
					If [ $$fmide_get_allow_abort_state ]
						Allow User Abort [ ON ]
					Else
						Allow User Abort [ OFF ]
					End If
					
					# Reset vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Beep" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#beep
					
					# Action
					Beep
					
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Clear" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#clear
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "select" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___select ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___select ]
						Clear [ Select: ON ]
					Else
						Clear [ Select: OFF ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "CloseDataFile" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#close-data-file
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "file_id" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_id" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file_id ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___file_id ) ]
						# Default to last file opened
						Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; LeftWords ( RightValues ( Get ( OpenDataFileInfo ) ; 1 ) ; 1 ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Close Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = 1
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "CloseFile" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#close-file
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "file" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___file ) ]
						# Close file
						# …using fmIDE to close the other file
						Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; $fmide___file & "::fmIDE" ; Parameter: "[+].Close File" ]
					Else
						# Close this file
						Close File [ Current File ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ClosePopover" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#close-popover
					
					# Action
					Close Popover
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "CloseScriptWorkspace" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#close-script-workspace
					
					# Action
					If [ MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsVisible") ]
						# Visible
						If [ not MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsActive") ]
							# but not active -> activate
							Open Script Workspace
						End If
						Set Variable [ $r[1] ; MBS( "Menubar.RunMenuCommand" ; 52097 /*        View Close Workspace */ ) ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "CommitRecordsRequests" or $fmide__action_name = "CommitRecords" or $fmide__action_name = "Commit" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#commit-records
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "with_dialog" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___skip_data_entry_validation[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "skip_data_entry_validation" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___override_ess_locking_conflicts[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "override_ess_locking_conflicts" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___with_dialog ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___skip_data_entry_validation[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___skip_data_entry_validation ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___override_ess_locking_conflicts[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___override_ess_locking_conflicts ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___override_ess_locking_conflicts ]
						# With override ESS conflicts
						If [ $fmide___skip_data_entry_validation ]
							# With validation
							If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
								# With dialog
								Commit Records/Requests [ Skip data entry validation ; With dialog: ON ; Force Commit ]
							Else
								# No dialog
								Commit Records/Requests [ Skip data entry validation ; With dialog: OFF ; Force Commit ]
							End If
						Else
							# No validation
							If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
								# With dialog
								Commit Records/Requests [ With dialog: ON ; Force Commit ]
							Else
								# No dialog
								Commit Records/Requests [ With dialog: OFF ; Force Commit ]
							End If
						End If
					Else
						# No override ESS conflicts
						If [ $fmide___skip_data_entry_validation ]
							# With validation
							If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
								# With dialog
								Commit Records/Requests [ Skip data entry validation ; With dialog: ON ]
							Else
								# No dialog
								Commit Records/Requests [ Skip data entry validation ; With dialog: OFF ]
							End If
						Else
							# No validation
							If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
								# With dialog
								Commit Records/Requests [ With dialog: ON ]
							Else
								# No dialog
								Commit Records/Requests [ With dialog: OFF ]
							End If
						End If
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___override_ess_locking_conflicts[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___skip_data_entry_validation[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "CloseWindow" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#close-window
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "window_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "window_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "current_file" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___window_name ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___current_file ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___window_name ) ]
						Close Window
					Else If [ $fmide___current_file ]
						Close Window [ Name: $fmide___window_name ; Current file ]
					Else
						Close Window [ Name: $fmide___window_name ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Case ( Get ( LastError ) = 112 ; "Window '" & $fmide___window_name  & "' is missing" ;  Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Recalculate the uuid of the current window
					Set Variable [ $fmide__current_window_uuid[1] ; Let(
									[
									¢window_id = Evaluate ( "Get ( WindowUUID )" ) ;
									¢window_id = Case( ¢window_id ≠ "?" ; ¢window_id ; MBS( "Window.Current") )
									];
									¢window_id
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ConfigurePersistentData" or 
								$fmide__action_name = "SetPersistentData" or 
								$fmide__action_name = "DeletePersistentData" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#configure-persistent-data
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-persistent-data
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#delete-persistent-data
					
					# Process Short Form
					# FIXME
					  - missing Short forms with instance_id from path (like fmIDE macro actions):
					    - Configure Persistent Data.«instance_id» = «value»
					    - Set Persistent Data.«instance_id» = «value»
					    - Delete Persistent Data.«instance_id»
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "instance_id" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
						If [ $fmide__action_name = "ConfigurePersistentData" ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( $fmide_action ; "delete" ; True ; JSONBoolean ) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___instance_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "instance_id" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "calculation" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___delete[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "delete" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___instance_id[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___instance_id ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___calculation ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___delete[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___delete ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					If [ $fmide__action_name = "DeletePersistentData" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___delete[1] ; 1 ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___delete ]
						# Delete Persistent Data
						Configure Persistent Data [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "PersistentStore" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						
					Else
						# Set Persistent Data
						Configure Persistent Data [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "PersistentStore" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___delete[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___instance_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; "" ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ConvertClipboard" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#convert-clipboard
					# Convert Clipboard - converts the clipboard between xml and FileMaker objects
					# Convert Clipboard ( to ) - converts the clipboard to the specified type
					
					#   where
					#     to can be one of the following values
					
					#       empty or null - convert automatically between xml and FileMaker objects
					#       auto - convert automatically between xml and FileMaker objects
					#       xml - convert to xml
					#       CustomFunction
					#       Field
					#       Layout
					#       Script
					#       ScriptStep
					#       Table
					#       ValueList
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "to" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					# [+].ConvertClipboard
					  [:]..to
					Set Variable [ $fmide___to[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "to" ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___to[1] ; Case ( $fmide___to = "Layout"   ; "Layout12" ; $fmide___to ) ]
					
					# Action
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									¢ClipboardText = MBS( "Clipboard.GetText" ) ;
									¢ClipboardXml = Case( not IsEmpty ( ¢ClipboardText ) ; ¢ClipboardText ; MBS( "Clipboard.GetFileMakerData" ; "auto" ) )
									
									];
									
									Case ( 
									
									/* Convert clipboard object to xml (or leave as xml) */
									$fmide___to = "xml" ; MBS( "Clipboard.SetText" ; ¢ClipboardXml ) ;
									
									/* convert clipboard xml to specific object */
									$fmide___to ≠ "" ;  MBS( "Clipboard.SetFileMakerData" ; "auto" ; ¢ClipboardXml) ;
									
									/* automatic - text/xml -> object */
									not IsEmpty ( ¢ClipboardText ) ; MBS( "Clipboard.SetFileMakerData" ; "auto" ; ¢ClipboardText ) ;
									
									/* automatic - object -> xml */
									MBS( "Clipboard.SetText" ; MBS( "Clipboard.GetFileMakerData" ; "auto" ) )
									
									)
									
									) ]
					# FIXME error handling
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___to[1] ; "" ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Copy" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#copy
					
					# FIXME Why is this implemented with a hiatus?
					
					# Press CmdCtrl+C
					
					# Action

						Else

						# Option parameters
						  
						  @param $fmide_on_exit_script_write_data_to_file_path - FileMaker path to the file where the script result should be written
						  @param $fmide_on_exit_script_write_data_to_folder_path  - FileMaker path to the folder where the script result and error values should be written
						
						If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide_on_exit_script_write_data_to_folder_path ) ]

							Set Variable [ $fmide___file_name[1] ; Right( $fmide_on_exit_script_write_data_to_file_path ; Length ( $fmide_on_exit_script_write_data_to_file_path ) - $fmide___pos ) ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___n[1] ; 1 ]
							
							Set Variable [ $fmide___pos[1] ; "" ]
							
						Else
							# Target folder
							
							# Write script result and error files to the folder
							Set Variable [ $fmide___file_name[1] ; "script_result.txt" ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___script_result[2] ; $$fmide_get_last_error ]

							Set Variable [ $fmide___script_result[4] ; $$fmide_get_last_error_location ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___file_name[4] ; "last_error_location.txt" ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___script_result[5] ; $$fmide_get_last_error_description ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___file_name[5] ; "last_error_description.txt" ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___n[1] ; 5 ]
							
						End If
						
						Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; 1 ]
						Loop [ Flush: Always ]
							Exit Loop If [ $fmide___i > $fmide___n ]
							
							# Read Details
							Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; $fmide_on_exit_script_write_data_to_folder_path & $fmide___file_name[$fmide___i] ]
							
							# Write File
							Create Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Create folders: ON ]
							Open Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___file_id ]
							Write to Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___script_result ; Append line feed: ON ]
							Close Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
							
							# Reset vars
							Set Variable [ $fmide___file_name[$fmide___i] ; "" ]
							If [ $fmide___i > 1 ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___script_result[$fmide___i] ; "" ]
							End If
							
							# Next file
							Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; $fmide___i + 1 ]
						End Loop
						
						
					Else If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide_callback_url ) ]
						If [ $fmide_callback_verify_ssl_certificates ]
							Insert from URL [ Select ; With dialog: OFF ; Target: $fmide___callback_response ; Verify SSL Certificates ; cURL options: $fmide_callback_curl ]
						Else
							Insert from URL [ Select ; With dialog: OFF ; Target: $fmide___callback_response ; cURL options: $fmide_callback_curl ]
						End If
						# FIXME - Handle callback errors?
					End If
					
					# and return result
					// Set Variable [ $$fmide_debug_script_result[1] ; $fmide___script_result & ": " & $fmide___script_result ]
					Exit Script [ $fmide___script_result ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "FlushCacheToDisk" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#flush-cache-to-disk
					
					# Action
					Flush Cache to Disk
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "FmideCall" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#fmide-call
					
					# Process Short Form (target)
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						# Error: No file name
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "Action is invalid: Expected JSONObject."  ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = 1
										
										];
										
										5
										
										) ]
					Else
						
						# Params
						Set Variable [ $fmide___file[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file" ) ]
						
						# Process Short Form
						If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___file ) ]
							# Process .«file.possibly.with.dots»…
							Set Variable [ $fmide___not_parameter_names[1] ; "0" ]
							Loop [ Flush: Always ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ]
								If [ JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "" ) = JSONObject and 
												ValueCount( $fmide___key ) = 1 and IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( $fmide___not_parameter_names ; $fmide___key ) ) ]
									# Extract file
									Set Variable [ $fmide__action_element_type[1] ; JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; $fmide___key  ) ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; $fmide___key  ) ]
									# Concatenate file key parts with a dot…
									Set Variable [ $fmide___file[1] ; $fmide___file & Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___file ) ; "." ) & $fmide___key ]
								Else
									# End of the line…
									If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___file ) ]
										# Error: No file
										Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
														[
														
														$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "Action is invalid: Expected single «file» but found multiple elements."  ;
														$fmide__error_thrown = 1
														
														];
														
														5
														
														) ]
									End If
									Exit Loop If [ 1 ]
								End If
							End Loop
						Else
							# Calculations
							Set Variable [ $fmide___file[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file ) ]
						End If
						
						# Process Short Form
						If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
											;["param" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type]
											) ]
						End If
						
						# Params
						Set Variable [ $fmide___frontmatter[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "frontmatter" ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___param[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "param" ) ]
						
						# Calculations
						Set Variable [ $fmide___frontmatter[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___frontmatter ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___param[1] ; Case(
										Position( JSONArray & JSONObject ; JSONGetElementType ( $fmide___param ; "" ) ; 1 ; 1 ) ; $fmide___param ;
										Evaluate ( $fmide___param )
										) ]
						
						# Combine frontmatter with script parameter
						Set Variable [ $fmide___param[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										vFrontmatter    = $fmide___frontmatter ;
										vFmideParameter = $fmide___param
										
										];
										
										Case (
										
										/* 0 frontmatter */
										IsEmpty ( vFrontmatter ) ; 
										  vFmideParameter ;
										
										/* 2 frontmatters -> Insert frontmatter in 2nd line */
										GetValue ( vFmideParameter ; 1 ) = "---" ;
										  Replace ( vFmideParameter ; 5 ; 0 ; vFrontmatter & ¶ ) ;
										
										/* 1 frontmatter -> Prepend frontmatter */
										List(
										"---" ;
										vFrontmatter
										; "---" ;
										vFmideParameter;
										)
										
										)
										
										) ]
						
						# Action
						If [ $fmide___file = Get ( FileName ) ]
							# Target THIS file
							Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: $fmide___param ]
						Else
							# Target OTHER file
							
							# Reset the ScriptResult
							Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: "fmIDESS-ExitScript" // no parameter = reset ]
							
							# Run fmIDE *synchronously* (works from fm19 - the minimum version that fmIDE works with ☺ )
							Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; "fmp://$/" & 
											GetAsURLEncoded ( $fmide___file ) &
											"?script=fmIDE" &
											"&option=5"     & /* 5 = run synchronously :-) */
											"&param=" & GetAsURLEncoded ( $fmide___param ) ]
							// Set Variable [ $$fmide_debug_url[1] ; $fmide___url ]
							Open URL [ In external browser: OFF ; With dialog: OFF ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "URL" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___frontmatter[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___param[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "FmideDefineMacro" or $fmide__action_name = "FmideGetMacro" or $fmide__action_name = "FmideRunMacro" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#fmide-define-macro
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#fmide-get-macro
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#fmide-run-macro
					
					# Process Common Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						# `[+].fmIDE Get Macro = "MyMacro"` -> `[+].fmIDE Get Macro.macro_name = "MyMacro"`
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
										;["macro_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type]
										) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__action_element_type[1] ; JSONObject ]
					End If
					
					# Common Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___macro_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "macro_name" ) ]
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___macro_name ) ]
						# Reset Error
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = ""  ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = ""
										
										];
										
										0
										
										) ]
					Else
						# Process .«macro_name.possibly.with.dots»…
						# codex 2026-10-14 (#189): reserve macro_name so empty/null parameters cannot become literal macro names.
						Set Variable [ $fmide___not_parameter_names[1] ; List (
										"macro_name" ;
										Case(
										
										$fmide__action_name = "FmideDefineMacro" ; "code¶metadata" ;
										$fmide__action_name = "FmideGetMacro"    ; "target" ;
										$fmide__action_name = "FmideRunMacro"    ; "frontmatter" ;
										
										)
										) ]
						Loop [ Flush: Always ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ]
							If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONObject and ValueCount( $fmide___key ) = 1 and IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( $fmide___not_parameter_names ; $fmide___key ) ) ]
								# Extract macro_name
								Set Variable [ $fmide__action_element_type[1] ; JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; $fmide___key  ) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; $fmide___key  ) ]
								# Concatenate macro_name key parts with a dot…
								Set Variable [ $fmide___macro_name[1] ; $fmide___macro_name & Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___macro_name ) ; "." ) & $fmide___key ]
							Else
								# End of the line…
								If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___macro_name ) ]
									# Error: No macro_name
									Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
													[
													
													$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "Action is invalid: «macro_name» may not be empty."  ;
													$fmide__error_thrown = 1
													
													];
													
													5
													
													) ]
								End If
								# Macro name is a calculation, so we have to quote the literal name here
								Set Variable [ $fmide___macro_name[1] ; Quote ( $fmide___macro_name ) ]
								
								# Reset Vars
								Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; "" ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___not_parameter_names[1] ; "" ]
								Exit Loop If [ 1 ]
							End If
						End Loop
					End If
					
					If [ not $$fmide_get_last_error ]
						# Calculations
						Set Variable [ $fmide___macro_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___macro_name ) ]
						
						If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___macro_name ) ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "Action is invalid: «macro_name» may not be empty."  ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = 1
											
											];
											
											5
											
											) ]
						Else
							If [ $fmide__action_name = "FmideDefineMacro" ]
								# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#fmide-define-macro
								
								# Process Short Form
								If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
									# no metadata
									Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
													;["code" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ]
													) ]
								End If
								
								# Params
								# [+].FmideDefineMacro
								  [:]..macro_name
								  [:]..code
								  [:]..metadata
								  [:]...description
								  [:]...author
								Set Variable [ $fmide___metadata[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "metadata" ) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___code[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "code" ) ]
								
								# Calculations
								Set Variable [ $fmide___code[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___code ) ]
								
								# Action
								
								# Construct the macro object
								Set Variable [ $fmide___macro[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
												/* Optional element hack: If there is no metadata the empty value is output to be overwritten by the following `code` key */
												;[Case( IsEmpty ( $fmide___metadata ) ; "code" ; "metadata" ) ; $fmide___metadata ; JSONObject]
												;["code" ; $fmide___code ; JSONString]
												;["name" ; $fmide___macro_name ; JSONString]
												) ]
								
								# Save the macro to persistent storage
								Configure Persistent Data [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "PersistentStore" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
								Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
												[
												
												$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
												$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
												
												];
												
												Get ( LastError )
												
												) ]
								
								# Reset Vars
								Set Variable [ $fmide___code[1] ; "" ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___macro[1] ; "" ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___macro_name[1] ; "" ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___metadata[1] ; "" ]
								
								
							Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "FmideGetMacro" ]
								# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#fmide-get-macro
								
								# Process Short Form
								If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
									# no target
									Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
													;["target" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ]
													) ]
								End If
								
								# Params
								# [+].FmideGetMacro
								  [:]..macro_name
								  [:]..target
								Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ]
								
								# Action
								If [ $fmide__fm_version_major < 26 ]
									# Here we just use the fm26 logic of "?" to indicate a missing resource
									Set Variable [ $fmide___macro[1] ; "?" ]
								Else
									# FM26+ GetPersistentData
									Set Variable [ $fmide___macro[1] ; GetPersistentData ( "fmide_macros" ; $fmide___macro_name ) ]
								End If
								
								
								If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___target ) ]
									# Target is optional
									Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; $fmide___macro ]
									If [ Left( $fmide___target ; 1 ) ≠ "$" or 
													Evaluate(
													
													"Let([" & 
													
													/* Define the var */
													$fmide___target & "=$fmide___value" & 
													
													/* Log the variable name */
													"¶;$fmide__local_variables = List ( $fmide__local_variables ; Case( Position ( \¶ & $fmide__local_variables & \¶ ; \¶ & $fmide___target & \¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; $fmide___target ) ) " & 
													
													"¶];0)"
													
													) ]
										# Set or Append content to target field
										Set Field By Name [ Specify target field: ON ; $fmide___target ; $fmide___value ]
										Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
														[
														
														$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
														$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
														
														];
														
														Get ( LastError )
														
														) ]
									End If
									Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; "" ]
								Else
									
									# Inject an Exit Script action
									Set Variable [ $fmide__script_result[1] ; $fmide___macro ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide__inject_actions[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" ; "[0].Exit Script" ; "$fmide__script_result" ; JSONString ) ]
								End If
								
								# Reset Vars
								Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
								
								
							Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "FmideRunMacro" ]
								# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#fmide-run-macro
								
								# Process Short Form
								If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
									# fmIDE Run Macro.«macro_name» = «value»
									  -> 
									  fmIDE Run Macro
									  .macro_name = «macro_name»
									  .frontmatter = «value»
									Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
													;["frontmatter" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ]
													) ]
								End If
								
								# Params
								# [+].FmideRunMacro
								  [:]..macro_name
								  [:]..frontmatter
								Set Variable [ $fmide___frontmatter[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "frontmatter" ) ]
								
								# Calculations
								Set Variable [ $fmide___frontmatter[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___frontmatter ) ]
								
								# Action
								Set Variable [ $fmide___macro[1] ; GetPersistentData ( "fmide_macros" ; $fmide___macro_name ) ]
								If [ $fmide___macro = "?" ]
									# macro is missing
									# Error 1700, Resource doesn't exist, is a good error for this
									Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 1700 /* Resource doesn't exist */ ]
									Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_detail[1] ; Substitute ( "fmide macro '{1}' not found" ; "{1}" ; $fmide___macro_name ) ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide__error_thrown[1] ; 1 ]
								Else
									# Extract the macro code
									Set Variable [ $fmide___macro_code[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___macro ;  "code" ) ]
									
									# Pass inputs to the macro
									# codex 2026-10-14 (#202): insert after the opening delimiter and separate supplied/stored frontmatter without evaluating twice.
									Set Variable [ $fmide___parameter[1] ; Case(
													
													/* Case 0: No frontmatter */
													IsEmpty ( $fmide___frontmatter ) ;
													  $fmide___macro_code ;
													
													/* Case 1: Add frontmatter before code */
													Left( $fmide___macro_code ; 4 ) ≠ "---¶" ; 
													
													  List(
													  "---" ; 
													  $fmide___frontmatter ;
													  "---" ;
													  $fmide___macro_code
													  ) ;
													
													/* Otherwise Case 2: Merge frontmatter */
													  Let(
													  [
													  vFrontmatterNoWs = Substitute ( $fmide___frontmatter ; [" " ; ""] ; [Char(9) ; ""] ; [Char(10) ; ""] ; [¶ ; ""] )
													  ];
													  Replace ( $fmide___macro_code ; 5 ; 0 ;
													    $fmide___frontmatter & ¶ & Case ( Right ( vFrontmatterNoWs ; 1 ) ≠ ";" ; ";" ) & ¶
													  )
													  )
													
													) ]
									
									Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: $fmide___parameter ]
								End If
								
								# Reset Vars
								Set Variable [ $fmide___frontmatter[1] ; "" ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___macro_code[1] ; "" ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___parameter[1] ; "" ]
								
							End If
						End If
					End If
					
					# Reset Common Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___macro[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___macro_name[1] ; "" ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "FreezeWindow" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#freeze-window
					
					# Action
					Freeze Window
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GetDataFilePosition" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#get-data-file-position
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "target" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_id" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; Case(
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" )
									) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file_id ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___file_id ) ]
						# Default to last file opened
						Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; LeftWords ( RightValues ( Get ( OpenDataFileInfo ) ; 1 ) ; 1 ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Get Data File Position [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					If [ $$fmide_get_last_error = 0 ]
						# Target is only set if successfully opened
						If [ Left( $fmide___target ; 1 ) ≠ "$" or 
										Evaluate(
										
										"Let([" & 
										
										/* Define the var */
										$fmide___target & "=$fmide___target_temp" & 
										
										/* Log the variable name */
										"¶;$fmide__local_variables = List ( $fmide__local_variables ; Case( Position ( \¶ & $fmide__local_variables & \¶ ; \¶ & $fmide___target & \¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; $fmide___target ) ) " & 
										
										"¶];0)"
										
										) ]
							# Set or Append content to target field
							Set Field By Name [ Specify target field: ON ; $fmide___target ; $fmide___target_temp ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target_temp[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GetFieldNames" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#get-field-names
					# @DEPRECATED - just do it yourself
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "table_name" ) ]
					
					# Action
					Set Variable [ $fmide__script_result[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
									;["data.table_name" ; $fmide___table_name ; JSONString]
									;["data.field_names" ; ExecuteSQL ( "SELECT FieldName FROM FileMaker_Fields WHERE TableName=?" ; Char(9) ; "¶" ; $fmide___table_name  ) ; JSONString]
									) ]
					
					# Inject an Exit Script action
					Set Variable [ $fmide__inject_actions[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" ; "[0].Exit Script" ; "$fmide__script_result" ; JSONString ) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GetFileExists" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#get-file-exists
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "file_path" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					# 20261014 @codex #210: file_path takes precedence by key presence; source_file remains a compatibility alias. Evaluate the selected expression once below.
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; JSONGetElement (
									$fmide_action ;
									Case(
									not IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ; "file_path" ) ) ; "file_path" ;
									"source_file"
									)
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; Case(
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" )
									) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file_path ) ]
					
					# Action
					Get File Exists [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					If [ $$fmide_get_last_error = 0 ]
						# Target is only set if successfully opened
						If [ Left( $fmide___target ; 1 ) ≠ "$" or 
										Evaluate(
										
										"Let([" & 
										
										/* Define the var */
										$fmide___target & "=$fmide___target_temp" & 
										
										/* Log the variable name */
										"¶;$fmide__local_variables = List ( $fmide__local_variables ; Case( Position ( \¶ & $fmide__local_variables & \¶ ; \¶ & $fmide___target & \¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; $fmide___target ) ) " & 
										
										"¶];0)"
										
										) ]
							# Set or Append content to target field
							Set Field By Name [ Specify target field: ON ; $fmide___target ; $fmide___target_temp ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target_temp[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GetFileSize" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#get-file-size
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "file_path" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					# 20261014 @codex #210: file_path takes precedence by key presence; source_file remains a compatibility alias. Evaluate the selected expression once below.
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; JSONGetElement (
									$fmide_action ;
									Case(
									not IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ; "file_path" ) ) ; "file_path" ;
									"source_file"
									)
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; Case(
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" )
									) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file_path ) ]
					
					# Action
					Get File Size [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					If [ $$fmide_get_last_error = 0 ]
						# Target is only set if successfully opened
						If [ Left( $fmide___target ; 1 ) ≠ "$" or 
										Evaluate(
										
										"Let([" & 
										
										/* Define the var */
										$fmide___target & "=$fmide___target_temp" & 
										
										/* Log the variable name */
										"¶;$fmide__local_variables = List ( $fmide__local_variables ; Case( Position ( \¶ & $fmide__local_variables & \¶ ; \¶ & $fmide___target & \¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; $fmide___target ) ) " & 
										
										"¶];0)"
										
										) ]
							# Set content to target field
							Set Field By Name [ Specify target field: ON ; $fmide___target ; $fmide___target_temp ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target_temp[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToAccount" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-account
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "account_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___account_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "account_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___account_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___account_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "[]"
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; /*Char(9) & */$fmide___account_name ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Security" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									) ]
					
					# Inject actions
					Set Variable [ $fmide__inject_actions[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "[]"
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; Char(9) & $fmide___account_name ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Security" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___account_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToBaseLayout" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-base-layout
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "table_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "table_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "base_table_name" ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___table_name ) and IsEmpty ( $fmide___base_table_name ) ]
						# Default = Go to base layout for the current layout
						Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; Case ( $fmide___by = "name" ; Quote ( Get ( LayoutTableName ) ) ; Get ( LayoutTableName ) ) ]
					End If
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___table_name ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___base_table_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE.GetBaseLayoutName" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; 
									["base_table_name" ; $fmide___base_table_name ; JSONString] ;
									["table_name" ; $fmide___table_name ; JSONString]
									) ]
					If [ Get ( LastError ) = 0 ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___jsr[1] ; Let( vResult = Get ( ScriptResult ) ;
										Case ( not IsEmpty ( vResult ) ; vResult ; "{}" )
										) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___jsr ; "base_layout_name" ) ]
					Else
						Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_name[1] ; Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___base_table_name ) ; $fmide___base_table_name ; $fmide___table_name ) ]
					End If
					Go to Layout [ Layoutname: $fmide___layout_name ; Animation: None ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___jsr[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToBaseTable" or $fmide__action_name = "GoToTable" or $fmide__action_name = "GoToBaseField" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-base-table
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-table
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-base-field
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/Base-Tables,-Tables,-Table-Occurrences-&-Base-Layouts-in-fmIDE
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; 
										
										Case(
										$fmide__action_name = "GoToBaseTable" ; "base_table_name" ;
										$fmide__action_name = "GoToTable"     ; "t_o_name" ;
										$fmide__action_name = "GoToBaseField" ; Case( $fmide__action_element_type = JSONNumber ; "field_id" ; "field_name" ) ; 
										)
										 ; $fmide_action ; JSONString ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "base_table_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "base_table_id" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "table_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___table_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "table_id" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___field_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_id" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___field_number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_number" ) ]
					
					# FIXME CHAOS: base_table has been added here, but the functionality here is only for table
					
					# Split FQFN (fully qualified field name)
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___table_name ) and Position( $fmide___field_name ; "::" ; 1 ; 1 ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name_bits[1] ; Substitute( $fmide___field_name ; "::" ; ¶ ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; GetValue ( $fmide___field_name_bits ; 1 ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name[1] ; GetValue ( $fmide___field_name_bits ; 2 ) ]
						
						If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___table_name ) and +$fmide___table_name = $fmide___table_name and Length ( $fmide___table_name ) < 7 ]
							# field_name starts with a base_table_id -> move to base_table_name
							Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_name[1] ; $fmide___table_name ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; "" ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Interpret pure numbers in base_table_name, table_name and field_name as ids
					# This allows us to reference a field by its identitiy, for example:
					  
					  - field_name = "1065089::1" is referring to the first field of the first table occurence
					  - field_name = "129::1" is referring to the first field of the first base table
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___table_name ) and +$fmide___table_name = $fmide___table_name ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___table_id[1] ; +$fmide___table_name ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; "" ]
					End If
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___base_table_name ) and +$fmide___base_table_name = $fmide___base_table_name ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_id[1] ; +$fmide___base_table_name ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_name[1] ; "" ]
					End If
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___field_name ) and +$fmide___field_name = $fmide___field_name ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___field_id[1] ; +$fmide___field_name ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name[1] ; "" ]
					End If
					
					# FOR LATER
					  
					  Let ([
					  
					  /* Lookup the name of a «base_table_id::field_id» field reference */
					  
					  $field_name = "129::1" ;
					  $base_table_id = GetValue ( Substitute ( $field_name ; "::" ; ¶ ) ; 1 ) ;
					  $field_id = GetValue ( Substitute ( $field_name ; "::" ; ¶ ) ; 2 ) ;
					  
					  ¢sql =
					  "SELECT f.BaseTableName || '::' || f.FieldName" &
					  "  FROM FileMaker_BaseTableFields f" &
					  "  JOIN FileMaker_BaseTables t" &
					  "    ON f.BaseTableName = t.BaseTableName" &
					  " WHERE t.BaseTableId = ?" &
					  "   AND f.FieldId = ?"
					  
					  ];
					  
					  ExecuteSQL ( ¢sql ; "" ; "" ; $base_table_id ; $field_id )
					  
					  )
					  
					  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
					  Let ([
					  
					  /* Lookup the name of a «table_id::field_id» field reference */
					  
					  $field_name = "1065089::1" ;
					  $table_id = GetValue ( Substitute ( $field_name ; "::" ; ¶ ) ; 1 ) ;
					  $field_id = GetValue ( Substitute ( $field_name ; "::" ; ¶ ) ; 2 ) ;
					  
					  ¢sql =
					  "SELECT f.TableName || '::' || f.FieldName" &
					  "  FROM FileMaker_Fields f" &
					  "  JOIN FileMaker_Tables t" &
					  "    ON f.TableName = t.TableName" &
					  " WHERE t.TableId = ?" &
					  "   AND f.FieldId = ?"
					  
					  ];
					  
					  ExecuteSQL ( ¢sql ; "" ; "" ; $table_id ; $field_id )
					  
					  )
					
					# Lookup base_table_name
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___base_table_name ) and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___base_table_id ) ]
						# Lookup base_table_name from base_table_id
						Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_name[1] ; Let([
										
										¢sql = 
										"SELECT BaseTableName" &
										"  FROM FileMaker_BaseTables" &
										" WHERE BaseTableId=?" &
										""
										
										];
										
										ExecuteSQL ( ¢sql ; "" ; "" ; $fmide___base_table_id )
										
										) ]
					End If
					# Lookup table_name
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___table_name ) and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___table_id ) ]
						# Lookup table_name from table_id
						Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; Let([
										
										¢sql = 
										"SELECT TableName" &
										"  FROM FileMaker_Tables" &
										" WHERE TableId=?" &
										""
										
										];
										
										ExecuteSQL ( ¢sql ; "" ; "" ; $fmide___table_id )
										
										) ]
					End If
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___table_name ) and IsEmpty ( $fmide___base_table_name ) ]
						# If no table specified use the current layouts table
						Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; Get ( LayoutTableName ) ]
					End If
					
					# Lookup field_name
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___field_id ) ]
						# Lookup field_name from field_id
						If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___table_name ) ]
							# from table_name and field_id
							Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name[1] ; Let([
											
											¢sql = 
											"SELECT FieldName" &
											"  FROM FileMaker_Fields" &
											" WHERE TableName=?" &
											"   AND FieldID=?" &
											""
											
											];
											
											ExecuteSQL ( ¢sql ; "" ; "" ; $fmide___table_name ; $fmide___field_id )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___base_table_name ) ]
							# from base_table_name and field_id
							Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name[1] ; Let([
											
											¢sql = 
											"SELECT FieldName" &
											"  FROM FileMaker_BaseTableFields" &
											" WHERE BaseTableName=?" &
											"   AND FieldID=?" &
											""
											
											];
											
											ExecuteSQL ( ¢sql ; "" ; "" ; $fmide___base_table_name ; $fmide___field_id )
											
											) ]
						End If
					Else If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___field_number ) ]
						# Lookup name by number
						If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___table_name ) ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___field_names[1] ; Let([
											
											¢sql = 
											"SELECT FieldName" &
											"  FROM FileMaker_Fields" &
											" WHERE TableName=?" &
											" ORDER BY FieldID" &
											""
											
											];
											
											ExecuteSQL ( ¢sql ; "" ; "" ; $fmide___table_name )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___base_table_name ) ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___field_names[1] ; Let([
											
											¢sql = 
											"SELECT FieldName" &
											"  FROM FileMaker_BaseTableFields" &
											" WHERE BaseTableName=?" &
											" ORDER BY FieldID" &
											""
											
											];
											
											ExecuteSQL ( ¢sql ; "" ; "" ; $fmide___base_table_name )
											
											) ]
						End If
						# Feat #139 Interpret negative numbers 'from the end' (-1 -> n)
						Set Variable [ $fmide___field_number[1] ; Case (
										$fmide___field_number < 0 ; ValueCount ( $fmide___field_names )+1 + $fmide___field_number ;
										$fmide___field_number 
										) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name[1] ; GetValue ( $fmide___field_names ; $fmide___field_number ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "[]" ; 
									["[0].Go To Base Layout.base_table_name" ; $fmide___base_table_name ; JSONString];
									["[0].Go To Base Layout.table_name" ; $fmide___table_name ; JSONString];
									["[1].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___field_name ; JSONString];
									["[2].Open Manage Database" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									 ) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___base_table_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___field_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name_bits[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___field_number[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___table_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToCustomFunction" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-custom-function
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "custom_function_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_function_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "custom_function_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_function_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___custom_function_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "[]"
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___custom_function_name&¶ ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Custom Functions" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_function_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToCustomMenu" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-custom-menu
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "custom_menu_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_menu_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "custom_menu_name" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_menu_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___custom_menu_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "[]"
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___custom_menu_name&¶ ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Custom Menus" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_menu_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToCustomMenuItem" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-custom-menu-item
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "custom_menu_item_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_menu_item_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "custom_menu_item_name" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_menu_item_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___custom_menu_item_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "[]"
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___custom_menu_item_name ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Custom Menus" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_menu_item_name[1] ; "" ]
