				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToCustomMenuSet" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-custom-menu-set
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "custom_menu_set_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_menu_set_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "custom_menu_set_name" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_menu_set_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___custom_menu_set_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "[]"
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___custom_menu_set_name&¶ ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Custom Menus" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_menu_set_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToExtendedPrivilege" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-extended-privilege
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "extended_privilege_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___extended_privilege_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "extended_privilege_name" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___extended_privilege_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___extended_privilege_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( ""
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___extended_privilege_name&¶ ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Security" ; "" ; JSONNull]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___extended_privilege_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToExternalDataSource" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-external-data-source
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "external_data_source_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___external_data_source_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "external_data_source_name" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___external_data_source_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___external_data_source_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( ""
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___external_data_source_name&¶ ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Data Sources" ; "" ; JSONNull]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___external_data_source_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToFileAccess" or $fmide__action_name = "Go to File Authorization" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-file-access
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-file-authorization
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "file_authorization_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_authorization_name[1] ; Case(
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_authorization_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_authorization_name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_access_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_access_name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_name" ) ;
									) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___file_authorization_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file_authorization_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "[]"
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___file_authorization_name&¶ ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Security" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_access_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToLayout" or
								$fmide__action_name = "GoToLayoutPart" or
								$fmide__action_name = "GoToLayoutField"  or
								$fmide__action_name = "GoToField" or
								$fmide__action_name = "GoToObject" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-layout
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-layout-part
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-layout-field
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-object
					# FIXME:
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-field
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToLayout" ]
						If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONString ]
							# Go to Layout "«selected layout»" -> .by="selection"
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ;["layout_name" ; $fmide_action ; JSONString] ) ]
						Else If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONNumber ]
							# Go to Layout by Number
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "layout_number" ; $fmide_action ; JSONNumber ) ]
						Else If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONNull or
										$fmide__action_element_type = JSONObject and IsEmpty ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ) ]
							# null/{} -> Go to Layout «original layout»
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "layout_number" ; $fmide__original_layout_number ; JSONNumber ) ]
						End If
						
					Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToObject" ]
						If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONString ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "object_name" ; $fmide_action ; JSONString ) ]
						End If
						
					Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToField" or $fmide__action_name = "GoToLayoutField" ]
						If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONString ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "layout_field_name" ; $fmide_action ; JSONString ) ]
						End If
						
					End If
					
					# Layout Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "layout_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "layout_number" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "layout_id" ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; Case(
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___by ) ; Substitute ( $fmide___by ;  "original layout" ; "original" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___layout_id ) ; "id" ; 
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___layout_number ) ; "number"
									) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						# Explicit by name
						# Only when Go to Layout.by="name" … evaluate the layout_name calaculation
						Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___layout_name ) ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_number[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___layout_number ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_id[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___layout_id ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___by ) and $fmide__action_name = "GoToLayout" ]
						# Implicit by name
						Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "name" ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					# ### Go to Layout
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___by ) ]
						# No go to layout
						
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						# By name (selected or calculated)
						Go to Layout [ Layoutname: $fmide___layout_name ; Animation: None ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						
					Else
						# By number
						If [ $fmide___by = "id" ]
							# By id (explicit or implicit) -> lookup layout_number
							Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_number[1] ; Let( ¢LayoutIDs = LayoutIDs ( "" ) ;
											ValueCount ( Left ( ¢LayoutIDs ; Position ( ¶ & ¢LayoutIDs & ¶ ; ¶ & $fmide___layout_id & ¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) ) )
											) ]
							
						Else If [ $fmide___by = "original" ]
							# Go to original layout
							Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_number[1] ; $fmide__original_layout_number ]
							
						End If
						If [ $fmide___layout_number ]
							# Feat #139 Interpret negative numbers 'from the end' (-1 -> n)
							Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_number[1] ; Case (
											$fmide___layout_number < 0 ; ValueCount ( LayoutIDs ( "" ) )+1 + $fmide___layout_number ;
											$fmide___layout_number 
											) ]
							Go to Layout [ Layoutnr.: $fmide___layout_number ; Animation: None ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					
					# Layout Object Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_part_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "layout_part_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_part_number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "layout_part_number" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_part_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "layout_part_id" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_field_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "layout_field_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "object_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___repetition[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "repetition" ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___repetition[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___repetition ) ; $fmide___repetition ; 1 ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ 0 ]
						# Switch
						
					Else If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___object_name ) or $fmide__action_name = "GoToObject" ]
						# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-object
						# ### Go to Object
						
						# Calculation
						# Object name is always evaluated
						Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___object_name ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___repetition[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___repetition ) ]
						
						# Go to Object
						If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___object_name ) ]
							# Go to Object "" -> Leave all fields / objects
							Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS( "Window.SetFocus" ; 0 ) ]
						Else
							Go to Object [ $fmide___object_name ; Repetition: $fmide___repetition ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						End If
						
					Else If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___layout_field_name ) or $fmide__action_name = "GoToLayoutField" ]
						# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-layout-field
						# ### Go to Layout Field
						
						# layout_field_name is only evaluate when GoToLayoutField.by="name" … 
						If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToLayoutField" and $fmide___by = "name" ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_field_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___layout_field_name ) ]
						End If
						
						# Go to Layout Field
						If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___layout_field_name ) ]
							If [ $fmide__is_mac ]
								If [ not Get ( ActiveModifierKeys ) ≠ 2 ]
									# AppleScript
									Insert Text [ Select ; Target: $fmide___applescript_template ; "go to field {{'$fmide___layout_field_name'}} of current record of document {{'Get( FileName )'}}" ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide___applescript[1] ; Substitute ( $fmide___applescript_template
													;["{{'$fmide___layout_field_name'}}" ; Quote( $fmide___layout_field_name &
													Case ( $fmide___repetition > 1 ; "[" & $fmide___repetition & "]" ) ) ]
													;["{{'Get( FileName )'}}" ; Quote( Get( FileName ) )]
													) ]
									Set Variable [ $$fmide_debug_applescript[1] ; $fmide___applescript ]
									Perform AppleScript [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
									Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
													[
													
													$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
													$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
													
													];
													
													Get ( LastError )
													
													) ]
									If [ $$fmide_get_last_error = -1728 ]
										# Applescript error -> FM-Error 102: Field is missing
										Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 102 ]
									End If
									Set Variable [ $fmide___applescript[1] ; "" ]
								End If
							End If
						End If
						
					Else If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___layout_part_name ) or $fmide__action_name = "GoToLayoutPart" ]
						# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-layout-part
						# ### Go to Layout Part
						
						# ToDo - to get to a LayoutPart…
						# 1. Commit - in order to exit any field
						  2. Press Tab
						  3. Press SHIFT+TAB n times
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "Not yet implemented" ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = 1
										
										];
										
										3
										
										) ]
						
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___applescript[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___applescript_template[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_field_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_number[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_part_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_part_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_part_number[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___repetition[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToListOfRecords" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-list-of-records
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "list_of_record_ids" ; $fmide_action ; JSONString ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___list_of_record_ids[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "list_of_record_ids" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "layout_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___new_window[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "new_window" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "window_name" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___list_of_record_ids[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___list_of_record_ids ) ]
					# Only selected layout
					Set Variable [ $fmide___new_window[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___new_window ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___window_name ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___layout_name ) ]
						# Go to Layout by name
						If [ $fmide___new_window ]
							# In new document window
							Go to List of Records [ $fmide___list_of_record_ids ; Layoutname: $fmide___layout_name ; Animation: None ; Style: Document ; Name: $fmide___window_name ; Layoutname: $fmide___layout_name ]
						Else
							Go to List of Records [ $fmide___list_of_record_ids ; Layoutname: $fmide___layout_name ; Animation: None ]
						End If
					Else
						# Current Layout
						If [ $fmide___new_window ]
							# In new document window
							Go to List of Records [ $fmide___list_of_record_ids ; Layout: <original layout> ; Animation: None ; Style: Document ; Name: $fmide___window_name ; Layout: <original layout> ]
						Else
							Go to List of Records [ $fmide___list_of_record_ids ; Layout: <original layout> ; Animation: None ]
						End If
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___layout_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___list_of_record_ids[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___new_window[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToNextField" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-next-field
					
					# Action
					Go to Next Field
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToPortalRow" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-portal-row
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONNumber ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
										;["by" ; "number" ; JSONString]
										;["row_number" ; $fmide_action ; JSONNumber]
										) ]
					Else If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						If [ not IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( $fmide_action ; "First¶Previous¶Next¶Last" ) ) ]
							# Simple command
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "by" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
						Else
							# By Calculation
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
											;["by" ; "number" ; JSONString]
											;["row_number" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type]
											) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___exit_after_last[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "exit_after_last" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "select" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___row_number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "row_number" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "with_dialog" ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___by ) and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___row_number ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "Number" ]
					End If
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___exit_after_last[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___exit_after_last ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___select ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___row_number[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___row_number ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___with_dialog ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___by = "Next" ]
						# Next
						If [ $fmide___select ]
							Go to Portal Row [ Exit after last: OFF ; Next ]
						Else
							Go to Portal Row [ Exit after last: OFF ; Next ]
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___do_exit[1] ; Get( LastError ) = 101 /* Record is missing */ ]
						If [ $fmide___exit_after_last and $fmide___do_exit ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; "ExitAfterLast" ]
						End If
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "Previous" ]
						# Previous
						Go to Portal Row [ Exit after last: OFF ; Previous ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___do_exit[1] ; Get( LastError ) = 101 /* Record is missing */ ]
						If [ $fmide___exit_after_last and $fmide___do_exit ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; "ExitAfterLast" ]
						End If
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "First" ]
						# First
						If [ $fmide___select ]
							Go to Portal Row [ Select: ON ; First ]
						Else
							Go to Portal Row [ Select: OFF ; First ]
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "Last" ]
						# Last
						If [ $fmide___select ]
							Go to Portal Row [ Select: ON ; Last ]
						Else
							Go to Portal Row [ Select: OFF ; Last ]
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "Number" ]
						# By calculated number
						If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
							If [ $fmide___select ]
								Go to Portal Row [ With dialog: ON ; By Calculation… ]
							Else
								Go to Portal Row [ With dialog: ON ; By Calculation… ]
							End If
						Else
							If [ $fmide___select ]
								Go to Portal Row [ With dialog: OFF ; By Calculation… ]
							Else
								Go to Portal Row [ With dialog: OFF ; By Calculation… ]
							End If
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__get_last_error_thown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "`.by` Parameter invalid" ;
										$fmide__get_last_error_thown = 1
										
										];
										
										960
										
										) ]
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___do_exit[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___exit_after_last[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___row_number[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToPreviousField" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-previous-field
					
					# Action
					Go to Previous Field
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToPrivilegeSet" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-privilege-set
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "privilege_set_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___privilege_set_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "privilege_set_name" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___privilege_set_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___privilege_set_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "[]"
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___privilege_set_name&¶ ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Security" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___privilege_set_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToRecordRequestPage" or $fmide__action_name = "GoToRecord" or $fmide__action_name = "GoToRequest" or $fmide__action_name = "GoToPage" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-record-request-page
					
					# Process Short Forms
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONString ]
						# «string» -> record or by calculation
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; 
										
										/* Interpret a string… */
										Case(
										
										/* …as a valid direction enumeration… */
										not IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( $fmide_action ; "First¶Next¶Previous¶Last" ) ) ; "by" ; 
										
										/* …otherwise as a calculated number… */
										"number" 
										
										);
										
										$fmide_action ; JSONString ) ]
					Else If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						# otherwise -> number
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "number" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___exit_after_last[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "exit_after_last" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "with_dialog" ) ]
					
					# #Any ONE of these:
					Set Variable [ $fmide___number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "number" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___record_number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "record_number" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___request_number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "request_number" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___page_number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "page_number" ) ]
					
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___number[1] ; Case (
									/* preferred parameter to match the action */
									$fmide_action_name = "GoToRecord"  and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___record_number  ) ; $fmide___record_number ;
									$fmide_action_name = "GoToRequest" and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___request_number ) ; $fmide___request_number ;
									$fmide_action_name = "GoToPage"    and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___page_number    ) ; $fmide___page_number ;
									/* but any parameter will do */
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___record_number  ) ; $fmide___record_number ;
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___request_number ) ; $fmide___request_number ;
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___page_number    ) ; $fmide___page_number ;
									/* mosdt general case last */
									$fmide___number
									) ]
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___by ) and  not IsEmpty ( $fmide___number ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "Number" ]
					End If
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___number[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___number ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___exit_after_last[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___exit_after_last ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___with_dialog ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___by = "Next" ]
						# Exit after last is Off here and interpreted afterwards
						Go to Record/Request/Page [ Next ; Exit after last: OFF ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						If [ $fmide___exit_after_last and $$fmide_get_last_error = 101 ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; "ExitAfterLast" ]
						End If
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "Previous" ]
						# Exit after last is Off here and interpreted in the action script context below… 
						Go to Record/Request/Page [ Previous ; Exit after last: OFF ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						If [ $fmide___exit_after_last and $$fmide_get_last_error = 101 ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide__action_skip[1] ; "ExitAfterLast" ]
						End If
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "First" ]
						Go to Record/Request/Page [ First ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "Last" ]
						Go to Record/Request/Page [ Last ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "Number" ]
						If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
							Go to Record/Request/Page [ With dialog: ON ; $fmide___number ]
						Else
							Go to Record/Request/Page [ With dialog: OFF ; $fmide___number ]
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else
						# Fail fast error
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 5 ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_detail[1] ; $fmide__action_name & ": missing/unknown/illegal parameter" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__error_thrown[1] ; 1 ]
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___exit_after_last[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___number[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___page_number[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___record_number[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___request_number[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; "" ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToScript" or
								$fmide__action_name = "GoToScriptStep" or
								$fmide__action_name = "OpenScriptWorkspace" or 
								$fmide__action_name = "OpenScript" or
								$fmide__action_name = "SelectScript" or
								$fmide__action_name = "SelectScriptSteps" or
								$fmide__action_name = "SelectScriptFolder" or
								$fmide__action_name = "FindScript" or
								$fmide__action_name = "FindScriptStep" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#find-script
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#find-script-step
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-script
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-script-step
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-script
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-script-workspace
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#select-script
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#select-script-folder
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#select-script-steps
					
					# FIXME
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_debug_log[1] ; List( $$fmide_debug_log ; Get ( CurrentHostTimeStamp ) & Char( 9 ) & "Test # " & fmIDE_Test::Sort ) & " /" & $fmide__action_name & ": " & $fmide_action ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_debug_log[1] ; List( $$fmide_debug_log ; Get ( CurrentHostTimeStamp ) & Char( 9 ) & "START: " &
									
									Substitute( List(
									"State:	" & MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsVisible") &		// 1=ScriptWorkspace is open
									MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsActive") &				// 1=ScriptWorkspace has focus
									MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsFocusOnScriptList") ;		// 1=ScriptList has focus, 0=ScriptList DOESN'T have focus … but WHAT DOES have focus?
									"Script:	" & MBS("ScriptWorkspace.CurrentTab") ;	// Name of the script visible in the editor
									) ; ¶ ; " | " )
									
									) ]
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						If [ $fmide__action_name = "SelectScriptSteps" ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "script_step_range" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
						Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "FindScriptStep" ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "script_step_search" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
						Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "FindScript" ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "script_search" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
						Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToScript" or
										$fmide__action_name = "OpenScript" or
										$fmide__action_name = "SelectScript" or
										$fmide__action_name = "SelectScriptFolder" or
										0 ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "script_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
						Else
							# FIXME
						End If
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name_stub[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_name_stub" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_id" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_number" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___script_name ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name_stub[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___script_name_stub ) ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_id[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___script_id ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_number[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___script_number ) ]
					
					# Feat #139 Interpret negative numbers 'from the end' (-1 -> n)
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_number[1] ; Case (
									$fmide___script_number < 0 ; ValueCount ( ScriptIDs ( "" ) )+1 + $fmide___script_number ;
									$fmide___script_number 
									) ]
					
					# Lookup script name if necessary - from name_stub or id or number
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name[1] ; Case(
									
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_name ) ;
									  $fmide___script_name ;
									
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_name_stub ) ; Let([ /* Lookup script_name from script_name_stub */
									
									  vScriptNames = ScriptNames ( "" ) ; 
									  vScriptNumber = ValueCount( Left( vScriptNames ; Position( ¶ & vScriptNames ; ¶ & $fmide___script_name_stub ; 1 ; 1 ) ) )
									
									  ];
									
									  Case( vScriptNumber ; GetValue ( vScriptNames ; vScriptNumber ) )
									
									  ) ;
									
									
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_id ) ; Let([ /* Lookup script_name from script_id */
									
									  vScriptIDs = ScriptIDs ( "" ) ; 
									  vScriptNames = ScriptNames ( "" ) ; 
									  vScriptNumber = ValueCount( Left( vScriptIds ; Position( ¶ & vScriptIds ; ¶ & $fmide___script_id & ¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) ) )
									
									  ];
									
									  Case( vScriptNumber ; GetValue ( vScriptNames ; vScriptNumber ) )
									
									  ) ;
									
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_number ) ; 
									  GetValue ( ScriptNames ( "" )  ; $fmide___script_number )
									
									) ]
					
					# Action
					
					# ## 1. Script Workspace
					
					Open Script Workspace
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					Exit Loop If [ Case( $$fmide_get_last_error ; Let( $$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "ScriptWorkSpace could not be opened." ; 1 ) ) ]
					
					If [ $fmide__is_win or not $fmide__is_mbs ]
						# ### Windows / no MBS => stop here
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 9 ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_detail[1] ; "ScriptWorkSpace GUI interaction needs Mac + MBS plugin." ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__error_thrown[1] ; 1 ]
						Exit Loop If [ 1 ]
					End If
					
					# ### vvvvvvv Mac+MBS-only vvvvvvv
					
					# Make sure the script workspace is at the front
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS("ScriptWorkspace.BringToFront") ]
					If [ MBS("IsError") ]
						# Can't open ScriptWorkSpace
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 9 ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_detail[1] ; "Can't bring ScriptWorkSpace to the front." ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__error_thrown[1] ; 1 ]
						Exit Loop If [ 1 ]
					End If
					
					
					# ## 2. Script
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_search[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_search" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ JSONListKeys ( $fmide___script_search ; "" ) = "calculation" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_search[1] ; Evaluate( JSONGetElement ( $fmide___script_search ; "calculation" ) ) ]
					End If
					
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_search ) ]
						# ### Search scripts
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS( "ScriptWorkspace.SetScriptListSearch" ; $fmide___script_search ) ]
						
					Else If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_name ) ]
						# ### Go to Script
						If [ $fmide__action_name = "SelectScriptFolder" ]
							# Select a folder
							Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS( "ScriptWorkspace.SelectGroup"; $fmide___script_name ; 1 ) ]
						Else
							# Open the script - permanently
							Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS("ScriptWorkspace.OpenScript" ; $fmide___script_name ) ]
						End If
						If [ MBS("IsError") ]
							# Make sure the Script list is visible
							# …not yet possible in MBS
							# Make sure ALL scripts are being shown
							Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS( "ScriptWorkspace.ExpandScriptFolders" ) ]
							If [ MBS("IsError") ]
								# Maybe we need to open all script folders
								Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS( "ScriptWorkspace.ExpandScriptFolders" ) ]
							End If
							
							# Try again
							If [ $fmide__action_name = "SelectScriptFolder" ]
								# Select a folder
								Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS( "ScriptWorkspace.SelectGroup"; $fmide___script_name ; 1 ) ]
							Else
								# Open the script - permanently
								Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS("ScriptWorkspace.OpenScript" ; $fmide___script_name ) ]
							End If
							If [ MBS("IsError") ]
								# Error
								Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 104 ]
								Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_detail[1] ; "Error: " & $fmide___r
												
												/*& " - Couldn't find script \"" & $fmide___script_name & "\""*/ ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide__error_thrown[1] ; 1 ]
								Exit Loop If [ 1 ]
							End If
						End If
					End If
					
					
					# ### 3. Script Steps
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_search[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_step_search" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_range[1] ; Evaluate ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_step_range" ) ) ]
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_step_range ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_range[1] ; Substitute( $fmide___script_step_range ; ["…" ; ¶] ; ["..." ; ¶] ; [".." ; ¶] ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_number[1] ; +GetValue( $fmide___script_step_range ; 1 ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_number_to[1] ; +GetValue( $fmide___script_step_range ; 2 ) ]
					Else
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_number[1] ; +JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_step_number" ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_number_to[1] ; +JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_step_number_to" ) ]
					End If
					
					# Defaults / Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_number[1] ; Case( 
									
									IsEmpty( $fmide___script_step_number ) ; 1 ;
									
									Right ( $fmide___script_step_number ; 1 ) ≠ "%" ; Evaluate( $fmide___script_step_number );
									
									Let(
									[
									
									¢percent = GetAsNumber( $fmide___script_step_number ) ;
									¢n = MBS("ScriptWorkspace.LineCount") ;
									¢m = Floor ( (¢n-1) * ¢percent / 100 )
									
									];
									
									Case( ¢percent ≥ 0 ; 1 + ¢m ; ¢n - ¢m )
									
									)) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_number_to[1] ; Case( 
									
									IsEmpty( $fmide___script_step_number_to ) ; "" ;
									
									Left( $fmide___script_step_number_to ; 1 ) = "+" ; 
									  Case (
									    not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_step_search ) ; $fmide___script_step_number_to ; /* AFTER the search */
									    $fmide___script_step_number + $fmide___script_step_number_to                   /* No Search -> now */
									  ) ;
									
									Right ( $fmide___script_step_number_to ; 1 ) ≠ "%" ; Evaluate( $fmide___script_step_number_to );
									
									Let(
									[
									
									¢percent = GetAsNumber( $fmide___script_step_number_to ) ;
									¢n = MBS("ScriptWorkspace.LineCount") ;
									¢m = Floor ( (¢n-1) * ¢percent / 100 )
									
									];
									
									Case( ¢percent ≥ 0 ; 1 + ¢m ; ¢n - ¢m )
									
									)) ]
					
					If [ $fmide__action_name = "SelectScriptSteps" or not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_step_number_to ) ]
						# Select range (from start to end, if start not by otherwise)
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS("ScriptWorkspace.SelectLines" ; $fmide___script_step_number ; $fmide___script_step_number_to ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS("ScriptWorkspace.ScrollToSelection"  ) ]
						
					Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToScriptStep" or not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_step_number ) ]
						# Go to line
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS("ScriptWorkspace.SelectLine" ; $fmide___script_step_number ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS("ScriptWorkspace.ScrollToSelection"  ) ]
						
					End If
					
					# ### Script step search
					
					# Calculations
					# codex 2026-10-14 (#179): inspect script_step_search for its calculation wrapper, independently of script_search.
					If [ JSONListKeys ( $fmide___script_step_search ; "" ) = "calculation" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_search[1] ; Evaluate( JSONGetElement ( $fmide___script_step_search ; "calculation" ) ) ]
					End If
					
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___script_step_search ) ]
						# Set MBS Search…
						Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmAutoMate.Search[1] ; $fmide___script_step_search ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS( "SyntaxColoring.ScriptSearchBox.SetText"; $$fmAutoMate.Search ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										/* Get text of entire script */
										vScriptText     = MBS( "ScriptWorkspace.ScriptText" ; ""; "<\¶>" ) ;
										vScriptLength   = ValueCount( vScriptText ) ;
										vLineNr         = MBS( "ScriptWorkspace.SelectedLine" );
										vRestScriptText = RightValues( vScriptText ; vScriptLength - vLineNr ) ;
										
										/* Calculate how many lines further down the script, and select the line */
										vDeltaLineNr    = ValueCount( Left( vRestScriptText ; Position( vRestScriptText ; $$fmAutoMate.Search ; 1 ; 1 ) ) ) ;
										r               = Case( vDeltaLineNr ; MBS("ScriptWorkspace.SelectLine"; vLineNr + vDeltaLineNr ; 1 /* Scroll */ ) )
										
										];
										
										""
										
										) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_number[1] ; MBS( "ScriptWorkspace.SelectedLine" ) ]
					End If
					
					# ### Offset
					
					If [ Left ( $fmide___script_step_number_to ; 1 ) = "+" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_number_to[1] ; $fmide___script_step_number + $fmide___script_step_number_to ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS("ScriptWorkspace.SelectLines" ; $fmide___script_step_number ; $fmide___script_step_number_to ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS("ScriptWorkspace.ScrollToSelection"  ) ]
					End If
					
					# ## 4. Focus
					
					# FIXME
					// Set Variable [ $$fmide_debug_log[1] ; List( $$fmide_debug_log ; Get ( CurrentHostTimeStamp ) & Char( 9 ) & "BEFORE Select Script: " &
									
									$fmide__action_name & ": " & $fmide_action & "" & 
									
									Substitute( List(
									"State:	" & MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsVisible") &		// 1=ScriptWorkspace is open
									MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsActive") &				// 1=ScriptWorkspace has focus
									MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsFocusOnScriptList") ;		// 1=ScriptList has focus, 0=ScriptList DOESN'T have focus … but WHAT DOES have focus?
									"Script:	" & MBS("ScriptWorkspace.CurrentTab") ;	// Name of the script visible in the editor
									) ; ¶ ; " | " )
									
									) ]
					If [ $fmide__action_name = "SelectScript" ]
						# …and move focus to the Script list
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS( "ScriptWorkspace.SetFocusToScriptList" ) ]
						// Set Variable [ $$fmide_debug_log[1] ; List( $$fmide_debug_log ; Get ( CurrentHostTimeStamp ) & Char( 9 ) & "IN Select Script > MBS( \"ScriptWorkspace.SetFocusToScriptList\" ) -> " & $fmIDE___r ) ]
					Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToScript" or $fmide__action_name = "OpenScript" ]
						# …and move focus to the Script
						Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS( "ScriptWorkspace.SetFocusToScript" ) ]
						// Set Variable [ $$fmide_debug_log[1] ; List( $$fmide_debug_log ; Get ( CurrentHostTimeStamp ) & Char( 9 ) & "IN Select Script > MBS( \"ScriptWorkspace.SetFocusToScriptList\" ) -> " & $fmIDE___r ) ]
					End If
					// Set Variable [ $$fmide_debug_log[1] ; List( $$fmide_debug_log ; Get ( CurrentHostTimeStamp ) & Char( 9 ) & "AFTER Select Script: " &
									
									Substitute( List(
									"State:	" & MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsVisible") &		// 1=ScriptWorkspace is open
									MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsActive") &				// 1=ScriptWorkspace has focus
									MBS("ScriptWorkspace.IsFocusOnScriptList") ;		// 1=ScriptList has focus, 0=ScriptList DOESN'T have focus … but WHAT DOES have focus?
									"Script:	" & MBS("ScriptWorkspace.CurrentTab") ;	// Name of the script visible in the editor
									) ; ¶ ; " | " )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name_stub[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_number[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_number[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_number_to[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_range[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_step_search[1] ; "" ]
					
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToTableOccurrence" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-table-occurrence
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/Base-Tables,-Tables-and-Table-Occurrences-in-fmIDE
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "t_o_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___t_o_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "t_o_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "table_name" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___t_o_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___t_o_name ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___table_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___t_o_name ) and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___table_name ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___t_o_name[1] ; $fmide___table_name ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "[]" ; 
									["[0].Go To Base Layout.table_name" ; $fmide___t_o_name ; JSONString];
									["[1].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___t_o_name ; JSONString];
									["[2].Open Manage Database" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									 ) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___t_o_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___table_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToTheme" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-theme
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "theme_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___theme_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "theme_name" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___theme_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___theme_name ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "[]"
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___theme_name ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Themes" ; "" ; JSONObject]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___theme_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "GoToValueList" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#go-to-value-list
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "value_list_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value_list_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "value_list_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value_list_number[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "value_list_number" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___value_list_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___value_list_name ) ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value_list_number[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___value_list_number ) ]
					
					# Feat #139 Interpret negative numbers 'from the end' (-1 -> n)
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value_list_number[1] ; Case (
									$fmide___value_list_number < 0 ; ValueCount( ValueListIDs ( "" ))+1 + $fmide___value_list_number ;
									$fmide___value_list_number 
									) ]
					If [ $fmide___by = "number" or IsEmpty ( $fmide___value_list_name ) and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___value_list_number ) ]
						# Get name from number
						Set Variable [ $fmide___value_list_name[1] ; GetValue ( ValueListNames ( "" ) ; $fmide___value_list_number ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( ""
									; ["[0].Store Keyboard Buffer.text" ; $fmide___value_list_name&¶ ; JSONString]
									; ["[1].Open Manage Value Lists" ; "" ; JSONString]
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value_list_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value_list_number[1] ; "" ]
					# name
					  number
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "HaltScript" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#halt-script
					
					# Action
					Halt Script
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Hello" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#hello
					
					# Action
					Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide_dialog_title ; Message: "Hello" ; Default Button: "Hi" ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Hiatus" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#hiatus
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "duration" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___indefinitely[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "indefinitely" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___duration[1] ; Case ( 
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "duration" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "duration" ) ; 
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "s" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "s" ) ; 
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "seconds" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "seconds" )
									) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___indefinitely[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___indefinitely ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___duration[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___duration ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___indefinitely[1] ; $fmide___indefinitely or
									$fmide___duration = "indefinitely" or
									IsEmpty ( $fmide___duration ) ]
					
					
					# Action
					# Hiatus - Halt script for a moment
					If [ $fmide___indefinitely ]
						# ToDo - indefinite Hiatus needs a method for the user to exit the Hiatus and proceed with the continuation
						# <thinking>
						Halt Script
					Else
						# Request to halt and continue
						Set Variable [ $fmide__continuation_halt_for_seconds[1] ; $fmide___duration ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let( $$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "" ; 0 ) ]
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___duration[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___indefinitely[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "InsertFromUrl" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#insert-from-url
					
					# Process Short Form - Insert from URL = «url»
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						# No target - value is url
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "url" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					Else
						
						# Params
						Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; Case(
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ;
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ;
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ;
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" )
										) ]
						
						If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___target ) ]
							# Process Short Form - Insert from URL.«target» = «url»
							Set Variable [ $fmide__key[1] ; JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ]
							If [ ValueCount( $fmide__key ) = 1 and
											
											(
											
											Position( $fmide__key ; "::" ; 1 ; 1 )
											
											or
											
											(
											  Position( $fmide__key ; "$" ; 1 ; 1 ) and
											  (
											    Left( $fmide__key ; 1 ) = "$"
											    or 
											    Left( Substitute( $fmide__key ; [" ";""] ; [Char(9);""] ) ; 1 ) = "$" 
											  )
											)
											
											) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide__key_trimmed[1] ; Let ( 
												[
												vKey   = $fmide__key ;
												vNoHWS = Substitute ( vKey ; [" ";""] ; [Char(9);""] ) ;
												vChar1 = Left( vNoHWS ; 1 ) ;
												vPos1  = Position ( vKey ; vChar1 ; 1 ; 1 ) ;
												vCharN = Right( vNoHWS ; 1 ) ;
												vPosN  = Position ( vKey ; vCharN ; Length ( vNoHWS ) ; -1 )
												
												
												];
												
												Middle ( vKey ; vPos1 ; vPosN - vPos1 + 1 )
												
												) ]
								# Use first unknown key as the variable or field name
								Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; $fmide__key_trimmed ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide__action_element_type[1] ; JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; $fmide__key  ) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; $fmide__key  ) ]
								
								If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "url" ; $fmide_action ; JSONString ) ]
								End If
							End If
						End If
					End If
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___target ) and not IsEmpty ( Get ( ActiveFieldName ) ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; Get ( ActiveFieldTableName ) & "::" & Get ( ActiveFieldName ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "select" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "with_dialog" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "url" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___curl_options[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "curl_options" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___do_not_automatically_encode_url[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "do_not_automatically_encode_url" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___url ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___curl_options[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___curl_options ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___select ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___with_dialog ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___do_not_automatically_encode_url[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___do_not_automatically_encode_url ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___target ) ]
						# Select (=Set)
						# @var $fmide___target_temp
						If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
							If [ $fmide___do_not_automatically_encode_url ]
								Insert from URL [ Select ; With dialog: ON ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ; URL: $fmide___url ; cURL options: $fmide___curl_options ]
							Else
								Insert from URL [ Select ; With dialog: ON ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ; URL: $fmide___url ; cURL options: $fmide___curl_options ]
							End If
						Else
							If [ $fmide___do_not_automatically_encode_url ]
								Insert from URL [ Select ; With dialog: OFF ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ; URL: $fmide___url ; cURL options: $fmide___curl_options ]
							Else
								Insert from URL [ Select ; With dialog: OFF ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ; URL: $fmide___url ; cURL options: $fmide___curl_options ]
							End If
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						If [ Left( $fmide___target ; 1 ) ≠ "$fmide___" or 
										Evaluate(
										
										"Let([" & 
										
										/* Define the var */
										$fmide___target & "=Case( $fmide___select ; Evaluate( $fmide___target ) ) & $fmide___target_temp" & 
										
										/* Log the variable name */
										"¶;$fmide__local_variables = List ( $fmide__local_variables ; Case( Position ( \¶ & $fmide__local_variables & \¶ ; \¶ & $fmide___target & \¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; $fmide___target ) ) " & 
										
										"¶];0)"
										
										) ]
							# Set or Append content to target field
							Set Field By Name [ Specify target field: ON ; $fmide___target ; Case ( not $fmide___select ; GetField ( $fmide___target ) ) & $fmide___target_temp ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___curl_options[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___do_not_automatically_encode_url[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target_temp[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "InsertText" or $fmide__action_name = "InsertCalculatedResult" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#insert_calculated_script_result
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#insert_text
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						# No target
						Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					Else
						
						# Params
						Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; Case(
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ;
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ;
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ;
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" )
										) ]
						
						If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___target ) ]
							# Process Short Form
							Set Variable [ $fmide___keys[1] ; JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; 1 ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___n[1] ; ValueCount( $fmide___keys ) ]
							Loop [ Flush: Always ]
								Exit Loop If [ $fmide___i > $fmide___n ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; GetValue( $fmide___keys ; $fmide___i ) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___key_trimmed[1] ; Let ( 
												[
												vKey   = $fmide___key ;
												vNoHWS = Substitute ( vKey ; [" ";""] ; [Char(9);""] ) ;
												vChar1 = Left( vNoHWS ; 1 ) ;
												vPos1  = Position ( vKey ; vChar1 ; 1 ; 1 ) ;
												vCharN = Right( vNoHWS ; 1 ) ;
												vPosN  = Position ( vKey ; vCharN ; Length ( vNoHWS ) ; -1 )
												
												
												];
												
												Middle ( vKey ; vPos1 ; vPosN - vPos1 + 1 )
												
												) ]
								If [ Left( $fmide___key_trimmed ; 1 ) = "$" or Position( $fmide___key ; "::" ; 1 ; 1 ) ]
									# Use first unknown key as the variable or field name
									Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; $fmide___key_trimmed ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide__action_element_type[1] ; JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; $fmide___key  ) ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; $fmide___key  ) ]
									Exit Loop If [ 1 ]
								End If
								Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; $fmide___i + 1 ]
							End Loop
						End If
					End If
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						If [ $fmide__action_name = "InsertText" ]
							# Insert Text
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "text" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
						Else
							# Insert Calculated Result
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "calculation" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "select" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___text[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "text" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; Case(
									/* text */
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "text" ) > 0 ;
									  Quote( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "text" ) ) ;
									/* calc */
									  JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "calculation" )
									) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___select ) ]
					# $fmide___calculation is evaluated below
					
					# Action
					# Target field
					# FIXME FIXME
					If [ Case(
									
									/* Case 1: Field or empty target **/
									Left( $fmide___target ; 1 ) ≠ "$" ; 
									  True ; 
									
									/* Case 2: Target a Variable **/
									Let(
									[
									
									  vDefineVar = Evaluate( "Let([" & 
									    /* Define the var */
									    $fmide___target & "=" & $fmide___calculation  & 
									    /* Log the variable name */
									    "¶;$fmide__local_variables = List ( $fmide__local_variables ; Case( Position ( \¶ & $fmide__local_variables & \¶ ; \¶ & $fmide___target & \¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; $fmide___target ) ) " &
									    "¶];0)"
									  );
									
									  $$fmide_get_last_error        = 0 ;
									  $$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "" ;
									  $fmide__error_thrown = ""
									  ];
									  False
									  )
									
									) ]
						# Target (current) field
						If [ Position ( $fmide___target ; "::" ; 1 ; 1 ) ]
							# Target field
							
							# Hm… 'Target:…' does not support dynamic targets so we need to go to the field (to trigger triggers)
							Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE" ; Parameter: "[+].Go to Field = " & Quote ( $fmide___target ) ]
							
						Else
							# No target field -> the steps will throw an error if no field is active
						End If
						
						If [ $fmide___select ]
							Insert Calculated Result [ Select: ON ; Evaluate ( $fmide___calculation ) ]
						Else
							Insert Calculated Result [ Select: OFF ; Evaluate ( $fmide___calculation ) ]
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else
						# Target var
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___key_trimmed[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___keys[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___n[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___select[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___text[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "InstallOnTimerScript" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#install-on-timer-script
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_parameter[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_parameter" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___interval[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "interval" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___script_name ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_parameter[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___script_parameter ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___interval[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___interval ) ]
					
					# Input Vars
					# $fmide__current_window_uuid
					
					# Action
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___interval ) ]
						# ON
						
						# Add the OnTimer Script to the $$fmide__ontimer_script_name variable for the current window
						Set Variable [ $$fmide__ontimer_script_name[$fmide__current_window_uuid] ; $fmide___script_name ]
						
						# Start the OnTimer Script to the (hard coded) fmIDE Subscript that is the OnTimerScriptProxy which calls the target script just stored in the window variable
						Install OnTimer Script [ "fmIDE" ; $fmide___interval ]
						
					Else
						# OFF
						# Remove the OnTimer Script for the current window
						Install OnTimer Script
						
						# Remove the OnTimer Script from the $$fmide__ontimer_script_name variable for the current window
						Set Variable [ $$fmide__ontimer_script_name[$fmide__current_window_uuid] ; "" ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ModifyLastFind" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#modify-last-find
					
					# Action
					Modify Last Find
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "MoveResizeWindow" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#move-resize-window
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "window_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file_only[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "current_file_only" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___top[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "top" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___left[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "left" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___width[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "width" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___height[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "height" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___window_name ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file_only[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___current_file_only ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___top[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___top ) ; Evaluate ( $fmide___top ) ; Get ( WindowTop ) ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___left[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___left ) ; Evaluate ( $fmide___left ) ; Get ( WindowLeft ) ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___width[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___width ) ; Evaluate ( $fmide___width ) ; Get ( WindowWidth ) ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___height[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___height ) ; Evaluate ( $fmide___height ) ; Get ( WindowHeight ) ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___window_name ) ]
						Move/Resize Window [ Height: $fmide___height ; Width: $fmide___width ; Top: $fmide___top ; Left: $fmide___left ]
					Else If [ $fmide___current_file_only ]
						Move/Resize Window [ Name: $fmide___window_name ; Height: $fmide___height ; Width: $fmide___width ; Top: $fmide___top ; Left: $fmide___left ]
					Else
						Move/Resize Window [ Name: $fmide___window_name ; Height: $fmide___height ; Width: $fmide___width ; Top: $fmide___top ; Left: $fmide___left ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file_only[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___height[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___left[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___top[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___width[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "NewFile" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#new-file
					
					# Action
					New File
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "NewRecord" or $fmide__action_name = "NewRequest" or $fmide__action_name = "NewRecord/Request" or $fmide__action_name = "NewRecordRequest" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#new-record-request
					
					# Action
					New Record/Request
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "NewWindow" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#new_window
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "window_name" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; Case(
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___window_name ) ; Evaluate ( $fmide___window_name ) ;
									Let( 
									[
									
									vWindowName = Get ( WindowName ) ;
									vPosDash = Position ( vWindowName ; " - " ; Length( vWindowName ) ; -1 ) ;
									vNumber = RightWords ( vWindowName ; 1 )
									
									] ;
									
									Case ( Right ( vWindowName ; Length( vWindowName )+1 - vPosDash ) = " - " & vNumber ;
									  SerialIncrement ( vWindowName ; 1 ) ;
									  Get ( WindowName ) & " - 2"
									)
									
									)
									
									) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ 1 /* Document Window */ ]
						# In new document window
						New Window [ Style: Document ; Name: $fmide___window_name ; Layout: <original layout> ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OmitMultipleRecords" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#omit-multiple-records
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "calculation" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "calculation" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "with_dialog" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___calculation ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___with_dialog ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
						# with dialog 
						If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide_calculation ) ]
							Omit Multiple Records [ With dialog: ON ; $fmide___calculation ]
						Else
							Omit Multiple Records [ With dialog: ON ]
						End If
					Else
						# no dialog 
						If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___calculation ) ]
							Omit Multiple Records [ With dialog: OFF ; $fmide___calculation ]
						Else
							# FM Default = Omit 1 Record
							Omit Multiple Records [ With dialog: OFF ]
						End If
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; "" ]
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OmitRecord" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#omit-record
					
					# Action
					Omit Record
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenDataFile" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-data-file
					
					# Params
					# 20261014 @codex #210: file_path takes precedence by key presence; source_file remains a compatibility alias. Evaluate the selected expression once below.
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; Case(
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ;
