									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" )
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; JSONGetElement (
									$fmide_action ;
									Case(
									not IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ; "file_path" ) ) ; "file_path" ;
									"source_file"
									)
									) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file_path ) ]
					
					# Action
					Open Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					If [ $$fmide_get_last_error = 0 ]
						# Target is only set if successfully opened
						If [ Left( $fmide___target ; 1 ) ≠ "$" or 
										Evaluate(
										
										"Let([" & 
										
										/* Define the var */
										$fmide___target & "=$fmide___target_temp" & 
										
										/* Log the variable name */
										"¶;$fmide__local_variables = List ( $fmide__local_variables ; Case( Position ( \¶ & $fmide__local_variables & \¶ ; \¶ & $fmide___target & \¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; $fmide___target ) ) " & 
										
										"¶];0)"
										
										) ]
							# Set or Append content to target field
							Set Field By Name [ Specify target field: ON ; $fmide___target ; $fmide___target_temp ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target_temp[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenEditSavedFinds" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-edit-saved-finds
					
					# Action
					Open Edit Saved Finds
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenFavorites" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-favorites
					
					# Action
					Open Favorites
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenFile" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-file
					
					# TRYING to emulate an Open File step
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "file" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___hidden[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "hidden" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___hidden[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___hidden ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___file = "[Self]" ]
						# THIS file has been (force) opened by calling the fmIDE script with  fmJAML `[+].Open File = == [Self]`
						# FIXME … Start script must be run (IFF not already run and file not hidden)
						
						# Indicate that the opening was succesful
						Set Variable [ $fmide__script_result[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "file_opened" ; True ; JSONBoolean ) ]
						# Inject an Exit Script action
						Set Variable [ $fmide__inject_actions[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" ; "[0].Exit Script" ; "$fmide__script_result" ; JSONString ) ]
					Else
						# Open File = == «file»
						
						# TRY
						Loop [ Flush: Always ]
							# ## 1. First try via custom script…
							Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; "fmIDE.OpenFile" ; Parameter: JSONSetElement ( "{}"
											;["file" ; $fmide___file ; JSONString]
											;["hidden" ; $fmide___hidden ; JSONString]
											) ]
							If [ Get ( LastError ) = 104 ]
								# Script not present -> try next method
							Else
								Set Variable [ $fmide___jsr[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( Get ( ScriptResult ) ) ; Get ( ScriptResult ) ; "{}" ) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___file_opening[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___jsr ; "file_opening" ) ]
								Exit Loop If [ $fmide___file_opening ]
								# File not present in subscript -> try next method
							End If
							
							# ## 2. Then try by fmIDE Script call
							Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; $fmide___file & "::fmIDE" ; Parameter: "[+].Open File = == [Self]" ]
							If [ Get ( LastError ) = 100 ]
								# File not present -> try next method
							Else If [ Get ( LastError ) = 104 ]
								# fmIDE Script not present -> try next method
								# …or is the file open anyhow?
							Else
								# Read success result
								Set Variable [ $fmide___jsr[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( Get ( ScriptResult ) ) ; Get ( ScriptResult ) ; "{}" ) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___file_opened[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___jsr ; "file_opened" ) ]
								Exit Loop If [ $fmide___file_opened ]
								# File not opened by subscript -> try next method
							End If
							Exit Loop If [ not (
											Get ( LastError ) = 104 or /* script is missing */
											Get ( LastError ) = 100 or /* file is missing */
											Get ( LastError ) = 0 and JSONGetElement ( Get ( ScriptResult ) ; "fmide_get_last_error" ) ≠ 0
											) ]
							
							# ## 3. Finally try by fmpurl
							Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file_path[1] ; Get ( FilePath ) ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___host[1] ; Get ( HostIPAddress ) ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___fmpurl[1] ; Case (
											/* hosted file */
											LeftWords ( $fmide___current_file_path ; 1 ) = "fmnet" ; 
											"fmp://" & Get ( HostIPAddress ) & "/" & $fmide___file ;
											
											
											/* local file */
											ConvertFromFileMakerPath ( 
											Substitute ( Get ( FilePath ) ; Get ( FileName ) & ".fmp12" ; $fmide___file & ".fmp12" ) 
											; URLPath )
											
											) ]
							Open URL [ In external browser: OFF ; With dialog: OFF ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "URL" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
							
							# Pause for a moment to allow fmpurl to work…
							Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
							
							If [ IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( DatabaseNames ; $fmide___file ) ) ]
								# Error - file not opened
								# Error 100 - file is missing
								Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let( $$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "File is missing: '" & $fmide___file & "'"  ; 100 ) ]
								Exit Loop If [ 1 ]
							End If
							
							# END TRY
							Exit Loop If [ True ]
						End Loop
						# FIXME
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file_path[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_opened[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_opening[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___fmpurl[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___hidden[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___host[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___jsr[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenFileOptions" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-file-options
					
					# Action
					Open File Options
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenFindReplace" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-find-replace
					
					# Action
					Open Find/Replace
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenHelp" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-help
					
					# Action
					Open Help
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenHosts" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-hosts
					
					# Action
					Open Hosts
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenManageContainers" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-manage-containers
					
					# Action

									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenManageCustomFunctions" ]

					Select Window

					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(

				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "OpenUrl" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#open-url
					# Default is without dialog
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "url" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "url" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "with_dialog" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___external[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "external" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___url ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___with_dialog ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___external[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___external ) ]
					
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___external ]
						If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
							Open URL [ In external browser: ON ; With dialog: ON ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "URL" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Else
							Open URL [ In external browser: ON ; With dialog: OFF ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "URL" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						End If
					Else
						If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
							Open URL [ In external browser: OFF ; With dialog: ON ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "URL" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Else
							Open URL [ In external browser: OFF ; With dialog: OFF ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "URL" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						End If
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___external[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Paste" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#paste
					
					If [ 0 ]
						# By Menu
						If [ $fmide___select ]
							Paste [ Select: ON ; No style: OFF ]
						Else
							Paste [ Select: OFF ; No style: OFF ]
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else
						# Action
						# Press CmdCtrl+V
						Insert Text [ Select ; Target: $fmide__idle_calc_expression ; "MBS( $fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key ; 1 ) & 
MBS( "RemoteControl.PressKey" ; "v" ; "v" ) & 
MBS( $fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key ; 0 ) &
""" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__idle_calc_expression[1] ; Substitute( $fmide__idle_calc_expression ; "$fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key" ; Quote( $fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key ) ) ]
						
						# Request to halt and continue
						Set Variable [ $fmide__continuation_halt_for_seconds[1] ; $fmide__continuation_seconds ]
						// Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					End If
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "PasteIntoCurrentLayout" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#paste-into-current-layout
					
					# Action
					# Bring window to front and press CmdCtrl+L, CmdCtrl+V
					Select Window
					Insert Text [ Select ; Target: $fmide__idle_calc_expression ; "MBS( $fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key ; 1 ) & 
MBS( "RemoteControl.PressKey" ; "l" ; "l" ) & 
MBS( "RemoteControl.PressKey" ; "v" ; "v" ) & 
MBS( $fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key ; 0 ) &
""" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide__idle_calc_expression[1] ; Substitute( $fmide__idle_calc_expression ; "$fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key" ; Quote( $fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key ) ) ]
					
					# Request to halt and continue
					Set Variable [ $fmide__continuation_halt_for_seconds[1] ; $fmide__continuation_seconds ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Pause" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#pause
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "duration" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___indefinitely[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "indefinitely" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___duration[1] ; Case ( 
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "duration" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "duration" ) ; 
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "s" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "s" ) ; 
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "seconds" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "seconds" )
									) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___indefinitely[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___indefinitely ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___duration[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___duration ) ]
					// Set Variable [ $fmide___hiatus[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___hiatus ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___indefinitely[1] ; $fmide___indefinitely or
									$fmide___duration = "indefinitely" or
									IsEmpty ( $fmide___duration ) ]
					
					
					# Action
					# Pause
					If [ $fmide___indefinitely ]
						Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					Else
						Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___duration[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___hiatus[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___indefinitely[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "PerformJavaScriptInWebViewer" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#perform-javascript-in-web-viewer
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "script_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "object_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___function_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "function_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___parameter[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "parameter" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___object_name ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___function_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___function_name ) ]
					# Parameters are evaluated individually from the array as they are passed
					
					# Action
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___object_name ) ]
						# Active WebViewer
						Perform JavaScript in Web Viewer [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "FunctionRef" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Parameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					Else
						# Named WebViewer
						Perform JavaScript in Web Viewer [ Name: $fmide___object_name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "FunctionRef" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Parameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___function_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___parameter[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "PerformFind" or
								$fmide__action_name = "ConstrainFoundSet" or
								$fmide__action_name = "ExtendFoundSet" or
								$fmide__action_name = "ShowAllRecords" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#perform-find
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "query" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___query[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "query" ) ]
					
					# TRY
					Loop [ Flush: Defer ]
						If [ $fmide__action_name = "ShowAllRecords" ]
							# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#show-all-records
							
							# Action
							Show All Records
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else
							If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___query ) ]
								# Query
								
								# Action
								Enter Find Mode [ Pause: OFF ]
								Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
												[
												
												$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
												$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
												
												];
												
												Get ( LastError )
												
												) ]
								Exit Loop If [ $$fmide_get_last_error ]
								
								# Get query criteria and convert to Array if necessary
								Set Variable [ $fmide___request_array[1] ; Case(
												JSONGetElementType ( $fmide___query ; "request" ) ≠ JSONArray ; 
												"[" & JSONGetElement ( $fmide___query ; "request" ) & "]" ;
												
												JSONGetElement ( $fmide___query ; "request" )
												) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___request_index[1] ; 0 ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___request_count[1] ; ValueCount ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide___request_array ; "" ) ) ]
								# FOR EACH request
								Loop [ Flush: Always ]
									Exit Loop If [ $fmide___request_index ≥ $fmide___request_count ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide___request[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___request_array ; $fmide___request_index ) ]
									
									Set Variable [ $fmide___omit[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___request ; "omit" ) ]
									If [ $fmide___omit ]
										Omit Record
									End If
									
									# FOR EACH criteria in the request
									Set Variable [ $fmide___criteria_array[1] ; Case(
													JSONGetElementType ( $fmide___request ; "criteria" ) ≠ JSONArray ; 
													"[" & JSONGetElement ( $fmide___request ; "criteria" ) & "]" ;
													
													JSONGetElement ( $fmide___request ; "criteria" )
													) ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide___criteria_index[1] ; 0 ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide___criteria_count[1] ; ValueCount ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide___criteria_array ; "" ) ) ]
									Loop [ Flush: Always ]
										Exit Loop If [ $fmide___criteria_index ≥ $fmide___criteria_count ]
										Set Variable [ $fmide___criteria[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___criteria_array; "[" & $fmide___criteria_index & "]" ) ]
										
										# Get field_name, value and operator
										Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___criteria ; "field_name" ) ]
										Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___criteria ; "value" ) ]
										Set Variable [ $fmide___operator[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___criteria ; "operator" ) ]
										
										Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; Case (
														
														/* no operator */
														IsEmpty( $fmide___operator ) ; $fmide___value ;
														
														/* *"" -> *"«value»", or "" -> "«value»" */
														Right( $fmide___operator ; 1 ) = "\"" ; Replace ( $fmide___operator ; Length( $fmide___operator ) ; 0 ; $fmide___value ) ;
														
														/* «operator»«value», e.g. ==Some Name or identifier */
														$fmide___operator & $fmide___value
														
														) ]
										
										# Set criteria
										Set Field By Name [ Specify target field: ON ; $fmide___field_name ; $fmide___value ]
										
										# NEXT criteria
										Set Variable [ $fmide___criteria_index[1] ; $fmide___criteria_index+1 ]
									End Loop
									
									# NEXT request
									Set Variable [ $fmide___request_index[1] ; $fmide___request_index + 1 ]
									Exit Loop If [ $fmide___request_index ≥ $fmide___request_count ]
									
									# For each further request we need a New Record/Request
									New Record/Request
								End Loop
							End If
							
							If [ Get ( WindowMode ) = 1 /* 1 = Find mode */ ]
								# FIND / CONSTRAIN / EXTEND
								Set Variable [ $fmide___found_set[1] ; Case (
												$fmide__action_name = "ConstrainFoundSet" ; "constrain" ;
												$fmide__action_name = "ExtendFoundSet" ; "extend" ;
												JSONGetElement ( $fmide___query ; "found_set" )
												) ]
								If [ $fmide___found_set = "constrain" ]
									# Constrain found_set
									Constrain Found Set [ ⚠️ RESTORE ⚠️ ]
								Else If [ $fmide___found_set = "extend" ]
									# Extend found_set
									Extend Found Set
								Else
									# Replace found_set = find
									Perform Find
									
								End If
								Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
												[
												
												$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
												$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
												
												];
												
												Get ( LastError )
												
												) ]
							End If
						End If
						
						# END TRY
						Exit Loop If [ True ]
					End Loop
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___criteria[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___criteria_array[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___criteria_count[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___criteria_index[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___field_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___found_set[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___omit[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___operator[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___query[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___request[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___request_array[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___request_count[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___request_index[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___text[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Print" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#print
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "with_dialog" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___with_dialog ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
						Print [ With dialog: ON ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Restore" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Print" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "PageSetup" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					Else
						Print [ With dialog: OFF ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Restore" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Print" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "PageSetup" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ShowOmittedOnly" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#show-omitted-only
					
					# Action
					Show Omitted Only
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "PerformScript" or $fmide__action_name = "PerformScriptOnServer" or $fmide__action_name = "PerformScriptOnServerWithCallback" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#perform-script
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "script_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_parameter[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "script_parameter" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					If [ $fmide___by = "name" ]
						# Only evaluate the script-name if "by:name"
						Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___script_name ) ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_parameter[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___script_parameter ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_and_name[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___file_name ) ; $fmide___file_name & "::" ) & $fmide___script_name ]
					
					# Action
					If [ 0 ]
						# Switch
						
					Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "PerformScript" ]
						Perform Script [ Specified: By name ; $fmide___file_and_name ; Parameter: $fmide___script_parameter ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						
					Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "PerformScriptOnServer" ]
						# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#perform-script-on-server
						
						# Params
						Set Variable [ $fmide___wait_for_completion[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "wait_for_completion" ) ]
						
						# Calculations
						Set Variable [ $fmide___wait_for_completion[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___wait_for_completion ) ]
						
						If [ not $fmide___wait_for_completion ]
							# Don't wait / Asynchronous call
							Perform Script on Server [ By name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Parameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Wait for completion: OFF ]
						Else
							# Wait (default)
							Perform Script on Server [ By name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Parameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Wait for completion: ON ]
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						
						
						
					Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "PerformScriptOnServerWithCallback" ]
						# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#perform-script-on-server-with-callback
						
						# Params
						Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_script_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "callback_script_name" ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_file_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "callback_file_name" ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_script_parameter[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "callback_script_parameter" ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_script_option[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "callback_script_option" ) ]
						
						# Vars
						Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_file_and_name[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___callback_file_name ) ; $fmide___callback_file_name & "::" ) & $fmide___callback_script_name ]
						
						# Calculations
						Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_script_parameter[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___callback_script_parameter ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_script_option[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___callback_script_option ) ]
						
						# Action
						If [ 0 ]
							# SWITCH
							
						Else If [ $fmide___callback_script_option = "Halt" ]
							# Halt
							Perform Script on Server with Callback [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptState" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptByName" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptParameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; By name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Parameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Else If [ $fmide___callback_script_option = "Exit" ]
							# Exit
							Perform Script on Server with Callback [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptState" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptByName" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptParameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; By name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Parameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Else If [ $fmide___callback_script_option = "Resume" ]
							# Resume
							Perform Script on Server with Callback [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptState" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptByName" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptParameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; By name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Parameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Else If [ $fmide___callback_script_option = "Pause" ]
							# Pause
							Perform Script on Server with Callback [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptState" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptByName" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptParameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; By name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Parameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Else
							# Continue (default)
							Perform Script on Server with Callback [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptState" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptByName" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "CallbackScriptParameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; By name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Parameter" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						End If
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_file_and_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_file_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_script_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_script_option[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___callback_script_parameter[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_and_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___script_parameter[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___wait_for_completion[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Press" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#press
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "key" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "key" ) /* The key to press */ ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___command_key[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "command_key" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___option[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "option" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___shift[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "shift" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___pause[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "pause" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___delay[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "delay" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___times[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "times" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___option[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___option ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___shift[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___shift ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___pause[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___pause ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___delay[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___delay ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___times[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___times ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; Case( not IsEmpty( $fmide___key ) ; $fmide___key ; $fmide___command_key ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___command_down[1] ; not IsEmpty( $fmide___command_key ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___times[1] ; Max( 1 ; $fmide___times ) ]
					
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___pause ) and IsEmpty ( $fmide___delay ) ]
						# Pause -> delay for the time of the pause, so that the event occurs in the pause
						Set Variable [ $fmide___delay[1] ; $fmide___pause ]
					Else If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___pause ) ]
						# Always pause momentarily, if there is no specific delayed event
						Set Variable [ $fmide___pause[1] ; 1/1000 ]
					End If
					
					# fixme - delay not yet implemented
					If [ $fmide_debug ≥ 7 ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_debug_log[1] ; List( $$fmide_debug_log ;
										"Press: " &  Quote ( $fmide___key ) ;
										"fmide___command_down: " & $fmide___command_down ;
										"MBS( " & Quote ( $fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key ) & " ; 1 )"
										) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___key ) ]
						# One or more times
						Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; 1 ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___n[1] ; $fmide___times ]
						Loop [ Flush: Always ]
							# Loop over key_chars in keys
							Exit Loop If [ $fmide___i > $fmide___n ]
							
							Set Variable [ $fmide___j[1] ; 1 ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___m[1] ; Length( $fmide___key ) ]
							Loop [ Flush: Always ]
								Exit Loop If [ $fmide___j > $fmide___m ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___key_char[1] ; Middle ( $fmide___key ; $fmide___j ; 1 ) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___shift_down_automatic[1] ; 0//not Exact ( Lower( $fmide___key_char ) ; $fmide___key_char ) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___virtual_key[1] ; Case(
												
												$fmide___key_char = Char(8)   ; 51  ; /* BS - Backspace - Mac */
												$fmide___key_char = Char(28)  ; 123 ; /* Arrow left - Mac */
												$fmide___key_char = Char(29)  ; 126 ; /* Arrow up - Mac */
												$fmide___key_char = Char(30)  ; 124 ; /* Arrow right - Mac */
												$fmide___key_char = Char(31)  ; 125 ; /* Arrow down - Mac */
												//$fmide___key_char = Char(32)  ; 49  ; /* Space - Mac */
												$fmide___key_char = Char(127) ; 117 ; /* DEL - Delete - Mac */
												
												$fmide___key_char
												
												) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___scan_code[1] ; $fmide___virtual_key ]
								// Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; Case( $fmide___shift ; MBS( "RemoteControl.PressShiftKey" ; 1 ) )  & 
												Case( $fmide___option ; MBS( "RemoteControl.PressOptionKey" ; 1 ) )  & 
												Case( $fmide___command_down         ; MBS( $fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key ; 1 ) ) & 
												MBS( "RemoteControl.PressKey" ; $fmide___virtual_key ; $fmide___scan_code ) & 
												Case( $fmide___command_down         ; MBS( $fmide__mbs_remote_control_press_command_or_control_key ; 0 ) )  &
												Case( $fmide___option ; MBS( "RemoteControl.PressOptionKey" ; 0 ) )  &
												Case( $fmide___shift ; MBS( "RemoteControl.PressShiftKey" ; 0 ) )  &
												"" ]
								If [ MBS("IsError") or Position ( $fmide___r ; "MBS" ; 1 ; 1 ) ]
									Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
													[
													
													$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = 
													  "Error in Action 'Press': " & $fmide___r & " (Letter " & $fmide___j & ": " & Quote ( $fmide___key ) & 
													  " `RemoteControl.PressKey ; " & Quote( $fmide___virtual_key ) & " ; " & Quote ( $fmide___scan_code ) & ";" ;
													
													$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
													
													];
													
													-2
													
													) ]
									Exit Loop If [ $$fmide_get_last_error ]
								End If
								
								# Pause, if necessary, in order for the event to take acton (menus are freed up during a pause)
								If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___pause ) ]
									# Unless the event has been delayed, it occurs during this pause
									Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
								End If
								
								# next key
								Set Variable [ $fmide___j[1] ; $fmide___j + 1 ]
							End Loop
							Exit Loop If [ $$fmide_get_last_error ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; $fmide___i + 1 ]
						End Loop
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___command_down[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___command_key[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___delay[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___j[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___key_char[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___m[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___n[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___option[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___pause[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___scan_code[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___shift[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___shift_down_automatic[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___times[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___virtual_key[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ReadFromDataFile" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#read-from-data-file
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_id" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; Case(
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" )
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___amount[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "amount" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___read_as[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "read_as" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file_id ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___amount[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___amount ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___file_id ) ]
						# Default to last file opened
						Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; LeftWords ( RightValues ( Get ( OpenDataFileInfo ) ; 1 ) ; 1 ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___read_as = "Bytes" ]
						# Bytes
						Read from Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "size" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ]
					Else If [ Abs ( $fmide___read_as ) = 16 ]
						# UTF-16
						Read from Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "size" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ]
						// Else If [ GetAsNumber ( $fmide___read_as ) = 8 ]
					Else
						# UTF-8 (default)
						Read from Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "size" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___target_temp ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					If [ $$fmide_get_last_error = 0 ]
						# Target is only set if successfully opened
						If [ Left( $fmide___target ; 1 ) ≠ "$" or 
										Evaluate(
										
										"Let([" & 
										
										/* Define the var */
										$fmide___target & "=$fmide___target_temp" & 
										
										/* Log the variable name */
										"¶;$fmide__local_variables = List ( $fmide__local_variables ; Case( Position ( \¶ & $fmide__local_variables & \¶ ; \¶ & $fmide___target & \¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; $fmide___target ) ) " & 
										
										"¶];0)"
										
										) ]
							# Set or Append content to target field
							Set Field By Name [ Specify target field: ON ; $fmide___target ; $fmide___target_temp ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___amount[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___read_as[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target_temp[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "RecoverFile" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#recover-file
					
					# TRYING to emulate an Recover File step
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "file_path" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					# 20261014 @codex #210: file_path takes precedence by key presence; file remains a compatibility alias. Evaluate the selected expression once below.
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; JSONGetElement (
									$fmide_action ;
									Case(
									not IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ; "file_path" ) ) ; "file_path" ;
									"file"
									)
									) ]
					# 20261014 @codex #211: Read, evaluate once and reset with_dialog for Recover File.
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "with_dialog" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file_path ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___with_dialog ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___file_path ) ]
						If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
							Recover File [ With dialog: ON ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Else
							Recover File [ With dialog: OFF ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						End If
					Else
						If [ $fmide___with_dialog ]
							Recover File [ With dialog: ON ]
						Else
							Recover File [ With dialog: OFF ]
						End If
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___with_dialog[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "RefreshWindow" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#refresh-window
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
										;["flush_cached_join_script_results" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type]
										;["flush_cached_external_data" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type]
										) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___flush[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "flush" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___flush_cached_join_results[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "flush_cached_join_results" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___flush_cached_external_data[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "flush_cached_external_data" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___flush[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___flush ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___flush_cached_join_results[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___flush_cached_join_results ) or $fmide___flush ]

					Else

							Refresh Window [ Flush cached external data ]
						Else
							Refresh Window
						End If
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]

					Set Variable [ $fmide___flush_cached_external_data[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___flush_cached_join_results[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___flush[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ReLogin" ]

				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "RunMenuCommand" ]

					Else If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___delay ) ]

					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(

									$fmide__error_thrown = vIsError
									
									];
									
									vIsError

					# Pause, if necessary, in order for the event to take acton (menus are freed up during a pause)

											[

						Else If [ $fmide___by = "compacted" ]

							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___by = "clone" ]
							Save a Copy as [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; clone (no records) ; Create folders: ON ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___by = "self" ]
							Save a Copy as [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; clone (no records) ; Create folders: ON ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "Action is invalid: Unknown selection."  ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = 1
											
											];
											
											5
											
											) ]
						End If
					Else
						If [ $fmide___by = "copy" ]
							Save a Copy as [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; copy of current file ; Create folders: OFF ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___by = "compacted" ]
							Save a Copy as [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; compacted copy (smaller) ; Create folders: OFF ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___by = "clone" ]
							Save a Copy as [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; clone (no records) ; Create folders: OFF ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;

											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___by = "self" ]

							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(

				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SaveACopyAsXml" or $fmide__action_name = "SaveAsXml" ]

					# Process Short Form

					# either

					Set Variable [ $fmide___standalone_binarydata[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___standalone_binarydata ) ]
					# or

								End If
							Else
								If [ $fmide___standalone_binarydata ]
									# no details, with standalone binarydata
									Save a Copy as XML [ $fmide___window_name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Include details for analysis tools: OFF ; Save each layout object&apos;s binary data under its node: ON ; Specify options as JSON: OFF ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "JSONOutput" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
								Else
									# no details, no standalone binarydata
									Save a Copy as XML [ $fmide___window_name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Include details for analysis tools: OFF ; Save each layout object&apos;s binary data under its node: OFF ; Specify options as JSON: OFF ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "JSONOutput" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
								End If
							End If
						End If
					Else
						# Legacy code < FM26
						Save a Copy as XML [ $fmide___window_name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Include details for analysis tools: OFF ; Save each layout object&apos;s binary data under its node: OFF ; Specify options as JSON: OFF ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "JSONOutput" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;

									];

									) ]

					Set Variable [ $fmide___include_details[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___json_options[1] ; "" ]

				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ScrollWindow" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#scroll-window
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "by" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "by" ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ 0 ]
						# SWITCH
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "Home" ]
						
						Scroll Window [ Home ]
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "End" ]
						Scroll Window [ End ]
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "Page Up" ]
						Scroll Window [ Page Up ]
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "Page Down" ]
						Scroll Window [ Page Down ]
					Else If [ $fmide___by = "To Selection" ]
						Scroll Window [ To Selection ]
					Else
						# END SWITCH
						
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___by[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SelectAll" ]

					# Action
					Select All
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
