									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SelectWindow" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#select-window
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONNull or $fmide__action_element_type = JSONObject and IsEmpty ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ) ]
						# Select Window (null/{}) -> Select Window «original window»
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "window_name" ; Quote( $fmide__original_window_name ) ; JSONString ) ]
					Else If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "window_name" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "window_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "current_file" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___window_name ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___current_file ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___window_name ) ]
						Select Window
					Else If [ $fmide___current_file ]
						Select Window [ Name: $fmide___window_name ]
					Else
						Select Window [ Name: $fmide___window_name ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Case ( Get ( LastError ) = 112 ; "Window '" & $fmide___window_name  & "' is missing" ;  Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Recalculate the uuid of the current window
					Set Variable [ $fmide__current_window_uuid[1] ; Let(
									[
									¢window_id = Evaluate ( "Get ( WindowUUID )" ) ;
									¢window_id = Case( ¢window_id ≠ "?" ; ¢window_id ; MBS( "Window.Current") )
									];
									¢window_id
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Set" or $fmide__action_name = "SetVariable" or $fmide__action_name = "SetField" or $fmide__action_name = "SetFieldByName" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set
					# [+].Set.$var = 1+2
					# [+].Set
					  [:]..target = "$var"
					  [:]..calculation = 1+2
					# [+].Set.$var".calculation = 1+2
					
					# Process Short Form (target)
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						# Error: No target name
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "Action is invalid: Expected JSONObject."  ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = 1
										
										];
										
										5
										
										) ]
					Else
						
						# Params
						Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; Case(
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "target" ) ;
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "name" ) ;
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "field_name" ) ;
										not IsEmpty ( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" ) ) ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "variable_name" )
										) ]
						
						# Process Short Form
						If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___target ) ]
							# Process .«target.possibly.with.dots»…
							Set Variable [ $fmide___not_parameter_names[1] ; "calculation" ]
							Loop [ Flush: Always ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ]
								If [ ValueCount( $fmide___key ) = 1 and IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( $fmide___not_parameter_names ; $fmide___key ) ) ]
									# Extract target
									Set Variable [ $fmide__action_element_type[1] ; JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; $fmide___key  ) ]
									Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; $fmide___key  ) ]
									# Concatenate target key parts with a dot…
									Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; $fmide___target & Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___target ) ; "." ) & $fmide___key ]
								Else
									# End of the line…
									If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___target ) ]
										# Error: No target
										Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
														[
														
														$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "Action is invalid: Expected single «target» but found multiple elements."  ;
														$fmide__error_thrown = 1
														
														];
														
														5
														
														) ]
									End If
									Exit Loop If [ 1 ]
								End If
							End Loop
						End If
						
						# Process Short Form
						If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONArray ]
							# JsonArray -> repetition number
							Set Variable [ $fmide___repetition[1] ; ValueCount ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ) - 1 ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide__action_element_type[1] ; JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "[" & $fmide___repetition & "]"  ) ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "[" & $fmide___repetition & "]"  ) ]
						End If
						If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "calculation" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
						End If
						
						# Params
						Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "calculation" ) ]
						If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___repetition ) ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___repetition[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "repetition" ) ]
						End If
						
						# Evaluate repetition to get get Target name
						Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; $fmide___target & Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___repetition ) ; "[" & Evaluate ( $fmide___repetition ) & "]" ) ]
						
						# Calculations
						If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetFieldByName" ]
							# Set Field by Name -> evaluate the target
							Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___target ) ]
						End If
						# Are evaluated below
						
						# Action
						# Set the variable in the condition…
						If [ Case(
										/* Case 0: Error ### */
										Position( $fmide___target ; "$fmide__" ; 1 ; 1 ) ; Let([
										
										  $$fmide_get_last_error = 1300 ;
										  $$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "fmIDE error: The specified variable name '$fmide__*' is reserved for fmiIDE internal variables and may not be targeted in action " & $fmide__action_name ;
										  $fmide__error_thrown = 1
										
										  ];
										  False
										  ) ;
										
										
										
										/* Case 1: Error 5 target missing */
										IsEmpty ( $fmide___target ) ; Let([
										
										  $$fmide_get_last_error = Case( $fmide__action_name = "SetVariable" ; 5 ; 102 ) ;
										  $$fmide_get_last_error_thrown = 1 ;
										  $$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "fmIDE error: missing target in action " & $fmide__action_name
										
										  ];
										  False
										  ) ;
										
										/* Case 2: Set Field table::field **/
										Left( $fmide___target ; 1 ) ≠ "$" ; 
										  True ; 
										
										/* Case 3: Set Variable $fmide___target **/
										Let(
										[
										
										vDefineVar = Evaluate( "Let([" & 
										  /* Define the var */
										  $fmide___target & "=" & $fmide___calculation  & 
										  /* Log the variable name */
										  "¶;$fmide__local_variables = List ( $fmide__local_variables ; Case( Position ( \¶ & $fmide__local_variables & \¶ ; \¶ & $fmide___target & \¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; $fmide___target ) ) " &
										  "¶];0)"
										);
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error        = 0 ;
										$$fmide_get_last_error_thrown = "" ;
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = ""
										
										];
										
										False
										
										)
										
										) ]
							
							# …otherwise set the target field
							Set Field By Name [ Specify target field: ON ; $fmide___target ; Evaluate ( $fmide___calculation ) ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						End If
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___keys[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___n[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___repetition[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetClipboardObjects" or $fmide__action_name = "SetFileMakerData" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-clipboard-objects
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-filemaker-data
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "text" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___text[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "text" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___type[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "type" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___text[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___text ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___type[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___type ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___type[1] ; Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___type ) ; $fmide___type ; "auto" ) ]
					
					# Action
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; MBS( "Clipboard.SetFileMakerData" ; $fmide___type ; $fmide___text ) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___text[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___type[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetClipboardText" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-clipboard-text
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≤ JSONNumber ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "text" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___text[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "text" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___type[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "type" ) ]
					
					# Action
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; Case ( 
									
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___type ) ; 
									MBS( "Clipboard.SetText" ; Evaluate ( $fmide___text ) ; Evaluate ( $fmide___type ) ) ;
									MBS( "Clipboard.SetText" ; Evaluate ( $fmide___text ) )
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___r[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___text[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___type[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetDataFilePosition" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-data-file-position
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "position" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_id" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___position[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "position" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file_id ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___position[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___position ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___file_id ) ]
						# Default to last file opened
						Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; LeftWords ( RightValues ( Get ( OpenDataFileInfo ) ; 1 ) ; 1 ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					Set Data File Position [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "position" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___position[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetErrorCapture" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-error-capture
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "state" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "state" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___state ) ]
					
					# Action
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_error_capture_state[1] ; GetAsBoolean ( $fmide___state ) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetErrorLogging" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-error-logging
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "state" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "state" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_debug_info[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "custom_debug_info" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___state ) ]
					# Note: $fmide___custom_debug_info DOES contain a calculation, but this is not evaluated here,
					  rather the calculation is stored into global variable `$$fmide__custom_debug_info`
					  and evaluated by the Set Error Logging step each time it is called
					
					# Output fmIDE global (file) variable
					Set Variable [ $$fmide__custom_debug_info[1] ; $fmide___custom_debug_info ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___state ]
						Set Error Logging [ ON ; CustomDebugInfo: Evaluate( $$fmide__custom_debug_info ) ]
					Else
						Set Error Logging [ OFF ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___custom_debug_info[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetMultiUser" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-multi-user
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "state" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "state" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___hidden[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "hidden" ) ]
					# state
					  hidden
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___state ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___hidden[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___hidden ) ]
					
					
					# Action
					If [ not $fmide___state ]
						Set Multi-User [ Off ]
					Else If [ $fmide___state = 2 or $fmide___hidden ]
						Set Multi-User [ On (Hidden) ]
					Else
						Set Multi-User [ On ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___hidden[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetSelection" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-selection
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "selection" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___selection[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "selection" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___start[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "start" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___end[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "end" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___selection[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___selection ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___selection_values[1] ; Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___selection ) ; Substitute ( $fmide___selection ; ["…" ; ¶] ; ["..." ; ¶] ; [".." ; ¶] ; ["-" ; ¶] ) ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___start[1] ; Case(
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___start ) ; Evaluate ( $fmide___start ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___selection_values ) ; GetValue ( $fmide___selection_values ; 1 ) ;
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___end[1] ; Case(
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___end ) ; Evaluate ( $fmide___end ) ;
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___selection_values ) ; GetValue ( $fmide___selection_values ; 2 ) ;
									) ]
					
					# Action
					Set Selection [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Select" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___end[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___selection[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___selection_values[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___start[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetWindowTitle" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-window_title
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						# rename current window
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
										;["new_window_title" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type]
										;["current_window" ; True ; JSONBoolean]
										) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "current_file" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_window[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "current_window" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___new_window_title[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "new_window_title" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "window_name" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_uuid[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "window_uuid" ) ]
					
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___current_file ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_window[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___current_window ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___new_window_title[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___new_window_title ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___window_name ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_uuid[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___window_uuid ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_identifier[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___window_uuid ) ; $fmide___window_uuid ; $fmide___window_name ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___current_window ]
						# Rename current window
						Set Window Title
						
					Else If [ $fmide___current_file ]
						# Rename window in current file
						Set Window Title [ Name: $fmide___window_identifier ]
						
					Else
						# Rename window in other file
						Set Window Title [ Name: $fmide___window_identifier ]
						
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_file[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___current_window[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___new_window_title[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___window_uuid[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetZoomLevel" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-zoom-level
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "calculation" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___lock[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "lock" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "calculation" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___lock[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___lock ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___calculation ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___lock ]
						# Lock On
						If [ 0 ]
							# Switch
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 25 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; 25% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 50 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; 50% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 75 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; 75% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 100 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; 100% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 150 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; 150% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 200 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; 200% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 300 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; 300% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 400 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; 400% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = "Zoom In" ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; Zoom In ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = "Zoom Out" ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; Zoom Out ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ +GetValue( Substitute( Get( ApplicationVersion ) ; "." ; ¶ ) ; 1 ) ≥ 26 ]
							# FM26+ any Zoom value
							If [ $fmide___calculation ]
								Set Zoom Level [ Lock: ON ; Custom... ]
								Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
												[
												
												$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
												$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
												
												];
												
												Get ( LastError )
												
												) ]
							Else
								# FIXME ERROR
							End If
						Else
							# FIXME ERROR
						End If
					Else
						# Lock Off
						If [ 0 ]
							# Switch
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 25 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; 25% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 50 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; 50% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 75 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; 75% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 100 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; 100% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 150 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; 150% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 200 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; 200% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 300 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; 300% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = 400 ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; 400% ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = "Zoom In" ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; Zoom In ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ $fmide___calculation = "Zoom Out" ]
							Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; Zoom Out ]
							Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
											[
											
											$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
											$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
											
											];
											
											Get ( LastError )
											
											) ]
						Else If [ +GetValue( Substitute( Get( ApplicationVersion ) ; "." ; ¶ ) ; 1 ) ≥ 26 ]
							# FM26+ any Zoom value
							If [ $fmide___calculation ]
								Set Zoom Level [ Lock: OFF ; Custom... ]
								Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
												[
												
												$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
												$fmide__last_error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
												
												];
												
												Get ( LastError )
												
												) ]
							Else
								# FIXME ERROR
							End If
						Else
							# FIXME ERROR
						End If
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___lock[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___calculation[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "SetWebViewer" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#set-web-viewer
					
					Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; "" ]
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type = JSONObject ]
						# Process .«object_name.possibly.with.dots»…
						Set Variable [ $fmide___not_parameter_names[1] ; "action¶object_name¶url" ]
						Loop [ Flush: Always ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; JSONListKeys ( $fmide_action ; "" ) ]
							If [ ValueCount( $fmide___key ) = 1 and IsEmpty ( FilterValues ( $fmide___not_parameter_names ; $fmide___key ) ) ]
								# Extract object_name
								Set Variable [ $fmide__action_element_type[1] ; JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; $fmide___key  ) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; $fmide___key  ) ]
								# Concatenate object_name key parts with a dot…
								Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; $fmide___object_name & Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___object_name ) ; "." ) & $fmide___key ]
							Else
								# End of the line…
								Set Variable [ $fmide___not_parameter_names[1] ; "" ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___key[1] ; "" ]
								If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___object_name ) ]
									# It will be evaluated
									Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; Quote( $fmide___object_name ) ]
								End If
								Exit Loop If [ 1 ]
							End If
						End Loop
					End If
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "url" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "action" ) ]
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___object_name ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "object_name" ) ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "url" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___object_name ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___url ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___action[1] ; Case (
									/* Tidy */ 
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___action ) ; Substitute( $fmide___action ; ["…" ; ""] ; ["." ; ""] ) ;
									
									/* Short Form */
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___url ) ; "Go to URL" ;
									
									/* Default */
									"Reset"
									) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___object_name ) ]
						# Error
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "Missing object name in `Set Web Viewer` action" ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = 1
										
										];
										
										5
										
										) ]
						
					Else If [ $fmide___action = "Go to URL" ]
						Set Web Viewer [ $fmide___object_name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "action" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						
					Else If [ $fmide___action = "Reset" ]
						Set Web Viewer [ $fmide___object_name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "action" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						
					Else If [ $fmide___action = "Reload" ]
						Set Web Viewer [ $fmide___object_name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "action" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						
					Else If [ $fmide___action = "Go Back" ]
						Set Web Viewer [ $fmide___object_name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "action" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						
					Else If [ $fmide___action = "Go Forward" ]
						Set Web Viewer [ $fmide___object_name ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "action" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
						
					Else
						# Error
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = "Unknown `Set Web Viewer` action : " & $fmide___action ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = 1
										
										];
										
										5
										
										) ]
						
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___object_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___action[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___url[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ShowCustomDialog" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#show-custom-dialog
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "message" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___message[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "message" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___title[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "title" ) ]
					
					# Process Short Forms (String Values -> Array)
					Set Variable [ $fmide___buttons[1] ; Case ( 
									
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "buttons" ) = JSONString ;
									  /* Convert simple string to buttons array */
									  While ( 
									  [
									  ¢Buttons = Evaluate( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "buttons" ) );
									  ¢Result = "[]" ;
									
									  ¢Index = 0 ;
									  ¢Length = ValueCount( ¢Buttons )
									
									  ]; ¢Index < ¢Length ;[
									
									
									  ¢Result = JSONSetElement ( ¢Result ; ¢Index ; Quote ( GetValue ( ¢Buttons ; ¢Index + 1 ) ) ; JSONString ) ;
									  ¢Index = ¢Index + 1
									
									  ];
									
									  ¢Result
									
									  ) ;
									
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "buttons" ) = JSONArray ;
									  JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "buttons" )
									
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___commits[1] ; Case ( 
									
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "commits" ) = JSONString ;
									  /* Convert simple string to commits array */
									  While ( 
									  [
									  vButtons = Evaluate( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "commits" ) );
									  vResult = "[]" ;
									
									  vIndex = 0 ;
									  vLength = ValueCount( vButtons )
									
									  ]; vIndex < vLength ;[
									
									
									  vResult = JSONSetElement ( vResult ; vIndex ; GetValue ( vButtons ; vIndex + 1 ) ; JSONString ) ;
									  vIndex = vIndex + 1
									
									  ];
									
									  vResult
									
									  ) ;
									
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "commits" ) = JSONArray ;
									  JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "commits" )
									
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___inputs[1] ; Case ( 
									
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "inputs" ) = JSONString ;
									  /* Convert simple string to inputs array */
									  While ( 
									  [
									  vButtons = Evaluate( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "inputs" ) );
									  vResult = "[]" ;
									
									  vIndex = 0 ;
									  vLength = ValueCount( vButtons )
									
									  ]; vIndex < vLength ;[
									
									
									  vResult = JSONSetElement ( vResult ; vIndex ; GetValue ( vButtons ; vIndex + 1 ) ; JSONString ) ;
									  vIndex = vIndex + 1
									
									  ];
									
									  vResult
									
									  ) ;
									
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "inputs" ) = JSONArray ;
									  JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "inputs" )
									
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___labels[1] ; Case ( 
									
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "labels" ) = JSONString ;
									  /* Convert simple string to labels array */
									  While ( 
									  [
									  vButtons = Evaluate( JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "labels" ) );
									  vResult = "[]" ;
									
									  vIndex = 0 ;
									  vLength = ValueCount( vButtons )
									
									  ]; vIndex < vLength ;[
									
									
									  vResult = JSONSetElement ( vResult ; vIndex ; Quote( GetValue ( vButtons ; vIndex + 1 ) ) ; JSONString ) ;
									  vIndex = vIndex + 1
									
									  ];
									
									  vResult
									
									  ) ;
									
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide_action ; "labels" ) = JSONArray ;
									  JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "labels" )
									
									) ]
					# Currently only 4 combinations supported in dialogs WITHOU´T inputs
					Set Variable [ $fmide___left[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "left" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___top[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "top" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___width[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "width" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___height[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "height" ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					Set Variable [ $fmide___buttons[1] ; Case(
									/* Array ok */
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide___buttons ; "" ) = JSONArray and
									not IsEmpty ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide___buttons ; "" ) ) ; $fmide___buttons ;
									
									/* Default button OK */
									JSONSetElement ( "[]" ; 0 ; Quote( "OK" ) ; JSONString )
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___commits[1] ; Case(
									/* Array ok */
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide___commits ; "" ) = JSONArray ; $fmide___commits ;
									
									/* Default Commit main button only */
									 "[1]"
									
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___inputs[1] ; Case(
									/* Array ok */
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide___inputs ; "" ) = JSONArray ; $fmide___inputs ;
									
									/* Default none */
									 "[]"
									
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___labels[1] ; Case(
									/* Array ok */
									JSONGetElementType ( $fmide___labels ; "" ) = JSONArray ; $fmide___labels ;
									
									/* Default none */
									 "[]"
									
									) ]
					
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___message[1] ; Evaluate( $fmide___message ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___title[1] ; Evaluate( $fmide___title ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___buttons[1] ; While(
									[
									
									¢array = $fmide___buttons ;
									¢index = 0;
									¢index_len = ValueCount ( JSONListKeys ( ¢array ; "" ) )
									
									]; ¢index < ¢index_len ;[
									
									¢array = JSONSetElement ( ¢array ; "[" & ¢index & "]" ; Evaluate ( JSONGetElement ( ¢array ; "[" & ¢index & "]" ) ) ; JSONString ) ;
									
									
									¢index = ¢index + 1
									
									];
									
									¢array
									
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___commits[1] ; While(
									[
									
									¢array = $fmide___commits ;
									¢index = 0;
									¢index_len = ValueCount ( JSONListKeys ( ¢array ; "" ) )
									
									]; ¢index < ¢index_len ;[
									
									¢array = JSONSetElement ( ¢array ; "[" & ¢index & "]" ; Evaluate ( JSONGetElement ( ¢array ; "[" & ¢index & "]" ) ) ; JSONString ) ;
									
									
									¢index = ¢index + 1
									
									];
									
									¢array
									
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___labels[1] ; While(
									[
									
									¢array = $fmide___labels ;
									¢index = 0;
									¢index_len = ValueCount ( JSONListKeys ( ¢array ; "" ) )
									
									]; ¢index < ¢index_len ;[
									
									¢array = JSONSetElement ( ¢array ; "[" & ¢index & "]" ; Evaluate ( JSONGetElement ( ¢array ; "[" & ¢index & "]" ) ) ; JSONString ) ;
									
									
									¢index = ¢index + 1
									
									];
									
									¢array
									
									) ]
					# Inputs are evaluated below into numbered variables for Show Custom Dialog (which does not support repetitions)
					Set Variable [ $fmide___input_count[1] ; ValueCount( JSONListKeys ( $fmide___inputs ; "" ) ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___left[1] ; Evaluate( $fmide___left ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___top[1] ; Evaluate( $fmide___top ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___width[1] ; Evaluate( $fmide___width ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___height[1] ; Evaluate( $fmide___height ) ]
					
					
					# Action
					If [ not $fmide___input_count ]
						# 0 inputs
						If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___left ) and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___top ) ]
							# With Position
							If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___width ) and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___height ) ]
								# With Size
								Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "height" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "width" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "top" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "left" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ]
							Else
								# No Size
								Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "top" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "left" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ]
							End If
						Else
							# no Position
							If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide___width ) and not IsEmpty ( $fmide___height ) ]
								# Size
								# There are 16 combinations of left, top, width, height that could occur - TOO MANY! :-/
								Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "height" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "width" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ]
							Else
								# Keine Size
								Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ]
							End If
						End If
					Else
						Set Variable [ $fmide___outputs[1] ; "[]" ]
						
						# Read the inputs
						Set Variable [ $fmide___input1_target[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___inputs ; "[0]" ) ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___input1[1] ; Evaluate( $fmide___input1_target ) ]
						If [ $fmide___input_count ≥ 2 ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___input2_target[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___inputs ; "[1]" ) ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___input2[1] ; Evaluate( $fmide___input2_target ) ]
							If [ $fmide___input_count ≥ 3 ]
								# 3 Inputs (more are just silently ignored)
								Set Variable [ $fmide___input3_target[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___inputs ; "[2]" ) ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___input3[1] ; Evaluate( $fmide___input3_target ) ]
								
								If [ JSONGetElement ( $fmide___commits ; "[2]" ) ]
									# Commit, Commit, Commit
									Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ; Input 2: $fmide___input2 ; Label 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[1]" ) ; Input 3: $fmide___input3 ; Label 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[2]" ) ]
								Else If [ JSONGetElement ( $fmide___commits ; "[1]" ) ]
									# Commit, Commit, Revert
									Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ; Input 2: $fmide___input2 ; Label 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[1]" ) ; Input 3: $fmide___input3 ; Label 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[2]" ) ]
								Else If [ JSONGetElement ( $fmide___commits ; "[0]" ) ]
									# Commit, Revert, Revert
									Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ; Input 2: $fmide___input2 ; Label 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[1]" ) ; Input 3: $fmide___input3 ; Label 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[2]" ) ]
								Else
									# Revert, Revert, Revert
									Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ; Input 2: $fmide___input2 ; Label 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[1]" ) ; Input 3: $fmide___input3 ; Label 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[2]" ) ]
								End If
								# Write input 3 to outputs rep and reset variables
								Set Variable [ $fmide___outputs[3] ; $fmide___input3 ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___input3[1] ; "" ]
								Set Variable [ $fmide___input3_target[1] ; "" ]
								
							Else
								# 2 Inputs
								If [ JSONGetElement ( $fmide___commits ; "[2]" ) ]
									# Commit, Commit, Commit
									Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ; Input 2: $fmide___input2 ; Label 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[1]" ) ]
								Else If [ JSONGetElement ( $fmide___commits ; "[1]" ) ]
									# Commit, Commit, Revert
									Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ; Input 2: $fmide___input2 ; Label 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[1]" ) ]
								Else If [ JSONGetElement ( $fmide___commits ; "[0]" ) ]
									# Commit, Revert, Revert
									Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ; Input 2: $fmide___input2 ; Label 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[1]" ) ]
								Else
									# Revert, Revert, Revert
									Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ; Input 2: $fmide___input2 ; Label 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[1]" ) ]
								End If
							End If
							# Write input 2 to outputs rep and reset variables
							Set Variable [ $fmide___outputs[2] ; $fmide___input2 ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___input2[1] ; "" ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___input2_target[1] ; "" ]
						Else
							# 1 input
							If [ JSONGetElement ( $fmide___commits ; "[2]" ) ]
								# Commit, Commit, Commit
								Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ]
							Else If [ JSONGetElement ( $fmide___commits ; "[1]" ) ]
								# Commit, Commit, Revert
								Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ]
							Else If [ JSONGetElement ( $fmide___commits ; "[0]" ) ]
								# Commit, Revert, Revert
								Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ]
							Else
								# Revert, Revert, Revert
								Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide___title ; Message: $fmide___message ; Default Button: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[0]" ) ; Button 2: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[1]" ) ; Button 3: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___buttons ; "[2]" ) ; Input 1: $fmide___input1 ; Label 1: JSONGetElement ( $fmide___labels ; "[0]" ) ]
							End If
						End If
						# Write input 1 to outputs rep and reset variables
						Set Variable [ $fmide___outputs[1] ; $fmide___input1 ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___input1[1] ; "" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___input1_target[1] ; "" ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# React to interaction
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_message_choice[1] ; Get( LastMessageChoice ) ]
					If [ JSONGetElement ( $fmide___commits ; "[" & ($$fmide_get_last_message_choice-1) & "]" ) ]
						# Write outputs back to inputs
						Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; 1 ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide___n[1] ; $fmide___input_count ]
						Loop [ Flush: Always ]
							Exit Loop If [ $fmide___i > $fmide___n ]
							
							# Get ith target+value
							Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide___inputs ; "[" & $fmide___i-1 & "]" ) ]
							Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; $fmide___outputs[$fmide___i] ]
							
							# Write target
							If [ Left( $fmide___target ; 1 ) ≠ "$" or 
											Evaluate(
											
											"Let([" & 
											
											/* Define the var */
											$fmide___target & "=$fmide___value" & 
											
											/* Log the variable name */
											"¶;$fmide__local_variables = List ( $fmide__local_variables ; Case( Position ( \¶ & $fmide__local_variables & \¶ ; \¶ & $fmide___target & \¶ ; 1 ; 1 ) = 0 ; $fmide___target ) ) " & 
											
											"¶];0)"
											
											) ]
								# Set or Append content to target field
								Set Field By Name [ Specify target field: ON ; $fmide___target ; $fmide___value ]
								Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
												[
												
												$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
												$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
												
												];
												
												Get ( LastError )
												
												) ]
							End If
							
							# Clear vars
							Set Variable [ $fmide___outputs[$fmide___i] ; "" ]
							
							# Next
							Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; $fmide___i + 1 ]
						End Loop
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___buttons[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___commits[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___i[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___inputs[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___input_count[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___labels[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___message[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___n[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___outputs[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___target[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___title[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; "" ]
					
					// Set Variable [ $fmide___left[1] ; "" ]
					// Set Variable [ $fmide___top[1] ; "" ]
					// Set Variable [ $fmide___width[1] ; "" ]
					// Set Variable [ $fmide___height[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ShowHideTextRuler" or
								$fmide__action_name = "ShowTextRuler" or
								$fmide__action_name = "HideTextRuler" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#show-hide-text-ruler
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
										;["state" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ]
										) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "state" ) ]
					
					# Clean up state
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; Substitute ( $fmide___state ; [-1;"Toggle"] ; [1;"Show"] ; [0;"Hide"] ; ["\"" ; ""] ) ]
					
					# Default
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; Case(
									$fmide__action_name = "ShowTextRuler" ; "Show" ;
									$fmide__action_name = "HideTextRuler" ; "Hide" ;
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___state ) ; $fmide___state ;
									"Toggle"
									) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___state = "Hide" ]
						Show/Hide Text Ruler [ Hide ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else If [ $fmide___state = "Show" ]
						Show/Hide Text Ruler [ Show ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else If [ $fmide___state = "Toggle" ]
						Show/Hide Text Ruler [ Toggle ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else
						# Fail fast error
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 5 ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_detail[1] ; $fmide__action_name & ": missing/unknown/illegal parameter" ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide__error_thrown[1] ; 1 ]
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ShowHideToolbars" or
								$fmide__action_name = "ShowToolbars" or
								$fmide__action_name = "HideToolbars" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#show-hide-toolbars
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
										;["state" ; Trim( Substitute ( $fmide_action ; ["unlock" ; ""] ; ["lock" ; ""] ) ) ; $fmide__action_element_type ]
										;["lock"  ; Position ( $fmide_action ; "lock" ; 1 ; 1 ) and not Position ( $fmide_action ; "unlock" ; 1 ; 1 ) ; JSONBoolean ]
										) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___lock[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "lock" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "state" ) ]
					
					# Calculate state
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; Case(
									$fmide__action_name = "ShowToolbars" ; "Show" ;
									$fmide__action_name = "HideToolbars" ; "Hide" ;
									not IsEmpty ( $fmide___state ) ; Substitute ( $fmide___state ; [1;"Show"] ; [0;"Hide"] ) ;
									"Toggle"
									) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___lock[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___lock ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___lock ]
						If [ $fmide___state = "Hide" ]
							Show/Hide Toolbars [ Lock: ON ; Include Edit Record Toolbar: OFF ; Hide ]
						Else If [ $fmide___state = "Show" ]
							Show/Hide Toolbars [ Lock: ON ; Include Edit Record Toolbar: OFF ; Show ]
						Else
							Show/Hide Toolbars [ Lock: ON ; Include Edit Record Toolbar: OFF ; Toggle ]
						End If
					Else
						If [ $fmide___state = "Hide" ]
							Show/Hide Toolbars [ Lock: OFF ; Include Edit Record Toolbar: OFF ; Hide ]
						Else If [ $fmide___state = "Show" ]
							Show/Hide Toolbars [ Lock: OFF ; Include Edit Record Toolbar: OFF ; Show ]
						Else
							Show/Hide Toolbars [ Lock: OFF ; Include Edit Record Toolbar: OFF ; Toggle ]
						End If
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___lock[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___state[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Speak" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#speak
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "message" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___message[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "message" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___wait[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "wait" ) ]
					
					# Default
					Set Variable [ $fmide___wait[1] ; Case( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___wait ) ; $fmide___wait ; 1 ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___message[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___message ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___wait[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___wait ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ $fmide___wait ]
						# wait (default)
						Speak [ $fmide___message ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Voice" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Wait" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					Else
						# don't wait
						Speak [ $fmide___message ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Voice" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; ⚠️ PARAMETER "Wait" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___message[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___wait[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "StoreKeyboardBuffer" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#store-keyboard-buffer
					
					# Writes the text (with optional line feed) to the fmIDE keyboard buffer file in the documents folder
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "text" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___text[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "text" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___append_line_feed[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "append_line_feed" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					# FIXME text?
					Set Variable [ $fmide___append_line_feed[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___append_line_feed ) ]
					
					
					# Action
					Set Variable [ $fmide___folder_path[1] ; Get ( DocumentsPath ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_name[1] ; "fmIDE_keyboard_buffer.txt" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; $fmide___folder_path & $fmide___file_name ]
					
					# First delete the file if it exists
					# @var $fmide___file_exists
					Get File Exists [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___file_exists ]
					If [ $fmide___file_exists ]
						Delete File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					End If
					
					# Then create the file with text and optional EOL
					Create Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Create folders: ON ]
					
					# @var $fmide___file_id
					Open Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "UniversalPathList" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___file_id ]
					If [ $fmide___append_line_feed ]
						Write to Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___text ; Append line feed: ON ]
					Else
						Write to Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___text ; Append line feed: OFF ]
					End If
					Close Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___append_line_feed[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_exists[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_name[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_path[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___folder_path[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___text[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "UndoRedo" or $fmide__action_name = "Undo" or $fmide__action_name = "Redo" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#undo-redo
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#undo
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#redo
					
					# Action
					If [ 0 ]
						# Switch
					Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Undo" ]
						Undo/Redo [ Undo ]
					Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "Redo" ]
						Undo/Redo [ Redo ]
					Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "UndoRedo" ]
						Undo/Redo [ Toggle ]
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "UnsortRecords" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#unsort-records
					
					# Action
					Unsort Records
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "ViewAs" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#view-as
					
					# Process Short Form
					If [ $fmide__action_element_type ≠ JSONObject ]
						Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "value" ; $fmide_action ; $fmide__action_element_type ) ]
					End If
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "value" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					// Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___value ) ]
					
					# Default
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; Case ( not IsEmpty ( $fmide___value ) ; $fmide___value ; "Cycle" ) ]
					
					# Action
					If [ 0 ]
						# Switch
					Else If [ $fmide___value = "Form" ]
						View As [ View as Form ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else If [ $fmide___value = "List" ]
						View As [ View as List ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else If [ $fmide___value = "Table" ]
						View As [ View as Table ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else If [ $fmide___value = "Cycle" ]
						View As [ Cycle ]
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					Else
						# Error
						# FIXME
						Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
										[
										
										$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
										$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
										
										];
										
										Get ( LastError )
										
										) ]
					End If
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___value[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else If [ $fmide__action_name = "WriteToDataFile" ]
					# @see wiki https://github.com/fmIDE/fmIDE/wiki/fmIDE-Actions/#write-to-data-file
					
					# Params
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "file_id" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___data_source[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "data_source" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___append_line_feed[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "append_line_feed" ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___write_as[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_action ; "write_as" ) ]
					
					# Calculations
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___file_id ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___data_source[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___data_source ) ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___append_line_feed[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide___append_line_feed ) ]
					
					# Defaults
					If [ IsEmpty ( $fmide___file_id ) ]
						# Default to last file opened
						Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; LeftWords ( RightValues ( Get ( OpenDataFileInfo ) ; 1 ) ; 1 ) ]
					End If
					
					# Action
					If [ Abs( $fmide___write_as ) = 16 ]
						# UTF-16
						If [ $fmide___append_line_feed ]
							# With line feeds
							Write to Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___data_source ; Append line feed: ON ]
						Else
							Write to Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___data_source ; Append line feed: OFF ]
						End If
					Else
						# UTF-8 (default)
						If [ $fmide___append_line_feed ]
							# With line feeds
							Write to Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___data_source ; Append line feed: ON ]
						Else
							Write to Data File [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "id" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ; Target: $fmide___data_source ; Append line feed: OFF ]
						End If
					End If
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; Let(
									[
									
									$$fmide_get_last_error_detail = Get ( LastErrorDetail ) ;
									$fmide__error_thrown = Case ( Get ( LastError ) ; 1 )
									
									];
									
									Get ( LastError )
									
									) ]
					
					# Reset Vars
					Set Variable [ $fmide___append_line_feed[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___data_source[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___file_id[1] ; "" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide___write_as[1] ; "" ]
					
					
				Else
					# unknown action => skip
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error[1] ; 4 ]
					Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_detail[1] ; "Unknown action '" & $fmide__action_name & "'" ]
					Set Variable [ $fmide__error_thrown[1] ; 1 ]
					
				End If
			End If
		End If
		
		# # END TRY (fmIDE error handling)
		Exit Loop If [ 1 ]
	End Loop
	
	If [ not $fmide__skipped ]
		# # CATCH (fmIDE error handling)
		If [ $$fmide_get_last_error ]
			If [ $fmide__error_thrown ]
				# ## Caught *newly thrown* error

								)" ]
				# ^^^^^^ fmIDE.GetFileMakerErrorMessage CF embed here
				
				Set Variable [ $$fmide_get_last_error_description[1] ; Evaluate ( $$fmide_get_last_error_description ) ]
				If [ $$fmide_get_error_capture_state ]
					# ignore errors
				Else
					# show error
					Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide_dialog_title ; Message: "fmIDE error @ line " & $$fmide_get_last_error_location &

									Case( not IsEmpty ( $$fmide_get_last_error_description ) ; " (" & $$fmide_get_last_error_description & ")" ) &
									" " & $$fmide_get_last_error_detail ; Default Button: "OK" ; Button 2: "Pause/debug" ; Button 3: Case ( $$fmide_get_allow_abort_state ; "Stop…" ) ]
					If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 2 ]
						# Debugger einschalten
						Set Variable [ $fmide_debugger[1] ; 1 ]
						Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]

						Show Custom Dialog [ Title: $fmide_dialog_title ; Message: "fmIDE error @ line " & $$fmide_get_last_error_location &
