						If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 2 ]
							# 2: Exit Script
							If [ Get ( ActiveModifierKeys ) = 1+8 /* SHIFT-ALT for pause for debug */ ]
								Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
							Else
								Set Variable [ $fmide__script_result[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
												;["fmide_get_last_error" ; $$fmide_get_last_error ; JSONNumber]
												;["fmide_get_last_error_detail" ; $$fmide_get_last_error_detail ; JSONString]
												) ]
								# Inject an Exit Script action
								Set Variable [ $fmide__inject_actions[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "[]" ; "[0].Exit Script" ; "$fmide__script_result" ; JSONString ) ]
							End If
						Else If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 3 ]
							# 3: HALT
							Halt Script
						Else
							# 1: OK
						End If
					Else
						# 1: OK
					End If
				End If
			End If
			# # END CATCH
		End If
	End If
	
	# # Pause on Error
	
	If [ $fmide__error_thrown ]
		If [ $fmide_pause_on_error and $$fmide_get_error_capture_state ]
			# pause on error
			Set Variable [ $fmide_debugger[1] ; 1 ]
		End If
		Set Variable [ $fmide__error_thrown[1] ; "" ]
	End If
	
	# # Action Injection
	
	# Inject actions (used to expand convenience actions into real actions, e.g. `Get Version`-> `Exit Script (version number)`)
	If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide__inject_actions ) and $fmide__inject_actions ≠ "[]" ]
		# Inject code into the action script directly after the current step
		
		# Remember the current action and change it to a uuid
		Set Variable [ $fmide___uuid[1] ; Get (UUID ) ]
		Set Variable [ $fmide_action[1] ; JSONGetElement ( $fmide_actions ; $fmide__action_index ) ]
		Set Variable [ $fmide_actions[1] ; JSONSetElement ( $fmide_actions ; "[" & $fmide__action_index & "]" ; $fmide___uuid ; JSONString ) ]
		
		# Construct the replacement code from the current step and the code to inject, and inject it
		Set Variable [ $fmide__replace_code[1] ; $fmide_action & "," & Middle ( $fmide__inject_actions ; 2 ; Length ( $fmide__inject_actions ) -2 ) ]
		Set Variable [ $fmide_actions[1] ; Substitute ( $fmide_actions ; Quote ( $fmide___uuid ) ; $fmide__replace_code )// & Case( $fmide__action_index < $fmide__action_index_max - 1 ; "," ) ) ]
		Set Variable [ $fmide__action_index_max[1] ; $fmide__action_index_max + ValueCount ( JSONListKeys ( $fmide__inject_actions ; "" ) ) ]
		
		# Tidy up
		Set Variable [ $fmide__inject_actions[1] ; "" ]
		Set Variable [ $fmide___uuid[1] ; "" ]
	End If
	
	# NEXT actions_index
	Set Variable [ $fmide__action_index[1] ; $fmide__action_index + 1 ]
	
	If [ $fmide__continuation_halt_for_seconds ]
		# # Prepare Hiatus
		
		# Only continue, if there is anything to continue with!
		If [ $fmide__action_index < $fmide__action_index_max ]
			# ## Continuation for Hiatus - This is where the magic *really* starts 🪄
			Set Variable [ $fmide__continuation[1] ; JSONSetElement ( "{}"
							;["actions" ; $fmide_actions ; JSONArray]
							;["action_stack" ; $fmide__action_stack ; JSONArray]
							;["action_stack_top" ; $fmide__action_stack_top ; JSONString]
							;["action_block" ; $fmide__action_block ; JSONString]
							;["action_skip"  ; $fmide__action_skip  ; JSONString]
							;["action_index" ; $fmide__action_index ; JSONNumber]
							;["action_index_max" ; $fmide__action_index_max ; JSONNumber]
							;["fmide_local_variables" ; $fmide__local_variables ; JSONString]
							;["original_layout_number" ; $fmide__original_layout_number ; JSONNumber]
							;["original_window_name" ; $fmide__original_window_name ; JSONString]
							) ]
			Set Variable [ $fmide__local_variables[1] ; While(
							[
							
							¢vars = $fmide__local_variables ;
							¢i = 1;
							¢n = ValueCount ( ¢vars )
							
							]; ¢i <= ¢n ;[
							
							¢var = Substitute( GetValue ( ¢vars ; ¢i ) ; "$" ; "" ) ;
							
							¢expression = "
							  Let([
							  $$fmide_local_variable_" & ¢var & "=$" & ¢var & "¶
							  ];
							  \"\”
							  )" ;
							$$expression = ¢expression;
							/* Redfine the variable as a global variable */
							¢ignore_errors = Evaluate ( ¢expression ) ;
							
							¢i = ¢i + 1
							
							];
							
							""
							
							) ]
			Set Variable [ $$fmide__continuation_mbs_schedule_ref[1] ; MBS( "Schedule.StartScriptAfterDelay"; $fmide__continuation_halt_for_seconds + Case( Div ( Get ( CurrentTimeUTCMilliseconds ) ; 1000 ) > 500 ; 1 ) ; /* +1 Workaround - the  MBS functon seems to be about 1  second too slow! */
							Get ( FileName ) ;
							Get ( ScriptName ) ;
							JSONSetElement ( "{}" ; "continuation" ; $fmide__continuation ; JSONObject )
							) ]
		End If
		
		If [ not IsEmpty ( $fmide__idle_calc_expression ) ]
			# ## Idle Calc expression
			# Here an expression is evaluated, causing something to happen in idle time, e.g. PasteIntoCurrentLayout
			Set Variable [ $fmide__r[1] ; Evaluate ( $fmide__idle_calc_expression ) ]
		End If
		Halt Script
	End If
End Loop

# # fmIDE Debugger final message
If [ $fmide_debugger ]
	Show Custom Dialog [ Title: "fmIDE Debugger" ; Message: "[" & $fmide__action_index & "]/" & $fmide__action_index_max & " " & "Finished¶" & 
					Case( $$fmide_get_last_error ; "Error: " & $$fmide_get_last_error ) &
					Case( not IsEmpty ( $$fmide_get_last_error ) ; " (" & $$fmide_get_last_error_text & ")" ) & 
					" " & $$fmide_get_last_error_detail ; Default Button: "Continue" ; Button 2: "Pause" ; Button 3: "HALT" ]
	If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 2 ]
		# Pause
		Pause/Resume Script [ ⚠️ PARAMETER "Options" NOT PARSED ⚠️ ]
	Else If [ Get ( LastMessageChoice ) = 3 ]
		Halt Script
	End If
End If

# # Finished
# The fmIDE action script exits here with no Exit Script and no ScriptResult, when the fmIDEAS finishes without having encountered an Exit Script step.

# About the last line
  
  - It always pays to have an empty last comment line in a script, so you can skip to the last line with the debugger, if you want
